Saya akan buat sebagai cheat sheet praktis untuk onboarding: fokus ke fitur Java 17+, pola coding modern, JVM/runtime, concurrency, data/API, dan hal-hal yang sering muncul di codebase enterprise.

Berikut **cheat sheet Java 17+** untuk cepat onboarding di codebase enterprise modern.

Catatan versi: **Java 17** dan **Java 21** adalah LTS penting untuk enterprise, sementara per Juli 2026 **JDK 25 adalah LTS terbaru** dan **JDK 26 adalah latest feature release**. Untuk production, biasanya pilih **17 / 21 / 25 LTS**, bukan versi non-LTS kecuali organisasi punya kebijakan upgrade cepat. ([Oracle](https://www.oracle.com/java/technologies/javase/17all-relnotes.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# Java 17+ Cheat Sheet

## 1. Mental Model Java Modern

Java modern bukan hanya “OOP language”. Di enterprise backend, Java biasanya dipakai sebagai:

| Area | Makna Praktis |
|---|---|
| Language | Type-safe, object-oriented, increasingly pattern-oriented |
| Runtime | JVM, GC, JIT, profiling, memory tuning |
| Platform | Standard library, concurrency, networking, crypto, collections |
| Enterprise runtime | Jakarta EE, Spring, Quarkus, Micronaut, Camunda, JAX-RS |
| Build ecosystem | Maven/Gradle, dependency management, CI/CD |
| Operational system | Observability, container tuning, Kubernetes, cloud runtime |

Rule besar: **jangan hanya hafal syntax; pahami contract, lifecycle, failure mode, memory, thread, transaction, dan observability.**

---

# 2. Version Awareness

## Enterprise-safe baseline

| Version | Status Praktis | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Java 8 | Legacy baseline | Masih banyak di enterprise lama |
| Java 11 | Legacy LTS | Banyak sistem lama sudah migrasi ke sini |
| Java 17 | Modern minimum | Banyak framework modern menjadikan ini baseline |
| Java 21 | Strong modern LTS | Virtual threads final, cocok untuk backend modern |
| Java 25 | Latest LTS per 2026 | Relevan untuk roadmap baru |
| Java 26 | Latest feature release | Bukan pilihan default production conservative |

OpenJDK mencatat JDK 25 sebagai LTS dari banyak vendor, dan Oracle download page menyebut JDK 25 sebagai latest LTS serta JDK 26 sebagai latest release. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/projects/jdk/25/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Project Structure

Struktur Maven umum:

```text
my-service/
├── pom.xml
├── src/
│   ├── main/
│   │   ├── java/
│   │   └── resources/
│   └── test/
│       ├── java/
│       └── resources/
```

Package structure enterprise:

```text
com.company.product.module
├── api              // REST resources / controllers
├── application      // use cases / orchestration
├── domain           // domain model, rules, state machine
├── persistence      // repository, mapper, DAO
├── integration      // external clients
├── config           // DI/configuration
├── security         // auth/authz helpers
└── observability    // logging, tracing, metrics
```

Principle:

```text
API layer should not contain business rules.
Persistence layer should not own domain decisions.
Domain/application layer should not depend directly on HTTP.
```

---

# 4. Maven Java 17+ Config

Minimal Maven compiler config:

```xml
<properties>
    <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
    <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
</properties>
```

Untuk Java 21:

```xml
<properties>
    <maven.compiler.release>21</maven.compiler.release>
</properties>
```

Bedakan ini:

| Config | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `source` | Bahasa Java yang boleh dipakai |
| `target` | Bytecode target |
| `release` | Source + target + API compatibility |

Prefer:

```xml
<maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
```

Daripada:

```xml
<maven.compiler.source>17</maven.compiler.source>
<maven.compiler.target>17</maven.compiler.target>
```

Karena `release` juga membatasi API JDK agar tidak tidak sengaja memakai API versi lebih tinggi.

---

# 5. `var`

```java
var name = "Fajar";
var count = 10;
var users = List.of("A", "B");
```

Gunakan `var` saat type obvious:

```java
var order = orderRepository.findById(orderId);
```

Hindari saat type tidak jelas:

```java
var result = service.process(input); // buruk kalau result tidak jelas
```

Guideline:

| Use `var` | Hindari `var` |
|---|---|
| Constructor jelas | Return method ambigu |
| Loop local | Domain object penting |
| Stream intermediate | Public API |
| Try-with-resources | Complex generic type yang perlu dibaca |

---

# 6. Text Blocks

```java
String sql = """
    SELECT id, status, created_at
    FROM orders
    WHERE status = ?
    ORDER BY created_at DESC
    """;
```

Cocok untuk:

| Use Case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| SQL | Query panjang |
| JSON test fixture | Mock API response |
| XML | SOAP/Jakarta XML |
| HTML/email template kecil | Notification |
| Multi-line logs | Debug payload |

Hati-hati dengan indentation dan trailing newline.

```java
String json = """
    {
      "status": "ACTIVE"
    }
    """;
```

---

# 7. Records

Record adalah immutable data carrier.

```java
public record UserDto(
    UUID id,
    String name,
    String email
) {}
```

Compiler otomatis generate:

```text
constructor
accessor
equals
hashCode
toString
```

Akses field record:

```java
user.name();   // bukan getName()
```

Validasi constructor:

```java
public record Money(BigDecimal amount, String currency) {
    public Money {
        if (amount == null || amount.signum() < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("amount must be positive");
        }
        if (currency == null || currency.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency is required");
        }
    }
}
```

Gunakan record untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| DTO | Entity JPA mutable |
| API response | Object dengan lifecycle kompleks |
| Command/query object | Object dengan identity mutable |
| Value object sederhana | Object yang butuh lazy loading |
| Projection | Object dengan inheritance hierarchy |

Contoh bagus:

```java
public record CreateOrderCommand(
    UUID customerId,
    List<OrderLineCommand> lines
) {}
```

---

# 8. Sealed Classes

Sealed class membatasi subtype yang valid.

```java
public sealed interface PaymentResult
    permits PaymentSuccess, PaymentFailed, PaymentPending {
}

public record PaymentSuccess(String transactionId) implements PaymentResult {}
public record PaymentFailed(String reason) implements PaymentResult {}
public record PaymentPending(String reference) implements PaymentResult {}
```

Cocok untuk domain state/result yang finite.

```java
public sealed interface CaseStatus
    permits Draft, Submitted, UnderReview, Approved, Rejected {
}

public record Draft() implements CaseStatus {}
public record Submitted() implements CaseStatus {}
public record UnderReview(String officerId) implements CaseStatus {}
public record Approved(String approvalId) implements CaseStatus {}
public record Rejected(String reason) implements CaseStatus {}
```

Manfaat:

| Manfaat | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Exhaustiveness | Compiler bisa bantu cek semua case |
| Domain clarity | State valid eksplisit |
| Safer refactoring | Tambah subtype terlihat dampaknya |
| Better modeling | Cocok untuk workflow/state machine |

---

# 9. Pattern Matching `instanceof`

Modern:

```java
if (obj instanceof String s) {
    System.out.println(s.toUpperCase());
}
```

Lama:

```java
if (obj instanceof String) {
    String s = (String) obj;
    System.out.println(s.toUpperCase());
}
```

Contoh domain:

```java
void handle(PaymentResult result) {
    if (result instanceof PaymentSuccess success) {
        audit(success.transactionId());
    } else if (result instanceof PaymentFailed failed) {
        alert(failed.reason());
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Switch Expression

```java
String label = switch (status) {
    case "DRAFT" -> "Draft";
    case "SUBMITTED" -> "Submitted";
    case "APPROVED" -> "Approved";
    default -> "Unknown";
};
```

Dengan enum:

```java
enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT, SUBMITTED, APPROVED, REJECTED
}

String action = switch (status) {
    case DRAFT -> "edit";
    case SUBMITTED -> "review";
    case APPROVED -> "archive";
    case REJECTED -> "revise";
};
```

Benefit:

| Lama | Modern |
|---|---|
| Risky fall-through | No accidental fall-through |
| Banyak mutable variable | Expression langsung |
| Default sering ambigu | Exhaustive enum handling |

---

# 11. Pattern Matching Switch

Di Java modern, switch semakin cocok untuk sealed hierarchy.

```java
String message = switch (result) {
    case PaymentSuccess s -> "Paid: " + s.transactionId();
    case PaymentFailed f -> "Failed: " + f.reason();
    case PaymentPending p -> "Pending: " + p.reference();
};
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
Result modeling
Command handling
Domain event handling
Workflow state transition
Error classification
```

---

# 12. Enum

Gunakan enum untuk value finite.

```java
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED
}
```

Enum dengan behavior:

```java
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT {
        @Override
        boolean canSubmit() {
            return true;
        }
    },
    SUBMITTED,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED;

    boolean canSubmit() {
        return false;
    }
}
```

Tapi jangan overload enum menjadi “mini-framework”. Untuk workflow kompleks, lebih baik state machine/table-driven transition.

---

# 13. Collections

Factory method:

```java
List<String> names = List.of("A", "B");
Set<String> roles = Set.of("ADMIN", "USER");
Map<String, Integer> scores = Map.of(
    "A", 10,
    "B", 20
);
```

Important:

```java
List.of(...) // immutable
Set.of(...)  // immutable
Map.of(...)  // immutable
```

Mutasi akan error:

```java
names.add("C"); // UnsupportedOperationException
```

Mutable copy:

```java
var mutable = new ArrayList<>(names);
```

---

# 14. List vs Set vs Map

| Type | Gunakan Saat |
|---|---|
| `List` | Urutan penting, duplicate boleh |
| `Set` | Unique value |
| `Map` | Lookup by key |
| `Queue` | FIFO/work queue |
| `Deque` | Stack/queue double-ended |

Common implementation:

| Interface | Implementation |
|---|---|
| `List` | `ArrayList`, `LinkedList` jarang |
| `Set` | `HashSet`, `LinkedHashSet`, `TreeSet` |
| `Map` | `HashMap`, `LinkedHashMap`, `TreeMap`, `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| `Queue` | `ArrayDeque`, `PriorityQueue`, concurrent queues |

Rule:

```text
Default List  -> ArrayList
Default Set   -> HashSet
Default Map   -> HashMap
Ordered Map   -> LinkedHashMap
Sorted Map    -> TreeMap
Concurrent    -> ConcurrentHashMap
```

---

# 15. Stream API

```java
List<String> activeNames = users.stream()
    .filter(User::active)
    .map(User::name)
    .toList();
```

Common operators:

| Operator | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `filter` | Seleksi |
| `map` | Transformasi |
| `flatMap` | Flatten nested collection |
| `sorted` | Sorting |
| `distinct` | Remove duplicate |
| `limit` | Batasi |
| `collect` | Aggregate |
| `toList` | Convert ke list |
| `anyMatch` | Ada yang match |
| `allMatch` | Semua match |
| `findFirst` | Ambil pertama |

Contoh grouping:

```java
Map<OrderStatus, List<Order>> byStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Order::status));
```

Contoh sum:

```java
BigDecimal total = lines.stream()
    .map(OrderLine::amount)
    .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
```

Hindari side effect:

```java
// buruk
orders.stream()
    .map(order -> {
        order.setStatus(APPROVED);
        return order;
    })
    .toList();
```

Lebih jelas:

```java
for (Order order : orders) {
    order.approve();
}
```

Stream cocok untuk transformasi data, bukan business workflow yang banyak side effect.

---

# 16. Optional

Gunakan untuk return value yang mungkin kosong.

```java
Optional<User> findById(UUID id);
```

Pemakaian:

```java
User user = userRepository.findById(id)
    .orElseThrow(() -> new NotFoundException("User not found"));
```

Transformasi:

```java
String email = userRepository.findById(id)
    .map(User::email)
    .orElse("unknown@example.com");
```

Hindari:

```java
if (optional.isPresent()) {
    var value = optional.get();
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
optional.ifPresent(this::process);
```

Jangan pakai Optional untuk:

| Jangan | Alasan |
|---|---|
| Field entity JPA | Banyak framework tidak nyaman |
| Parameter method | Membuat call-site aneh |
| Collection | Pakai empty list |
| Serialization DTO | Bisa menyulitkan JSON binding |

---

# 17. Exception Handling

Hierarchy umum:

```text
Throwable
├── Error
└── Exception
    ├── RuntimeException
    └── checked exceptions
```

Rule:

| Jenis | Gunakan Untuk |
|---|---|
| Checked exception | Caller realistically bisa recover |
| Runtime exception | Programming/domain/runtime failure |
| Error | JVM/system fatal, jangan catch umum |

Custom exception:

```java
public class OrderNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
    public OrderNotFoundException(UUID orderId) {
        super("Order not found: " + orderId);
    }
}
```

Exception translation:

```java
try {
    externalClient.call();
} catch (SocketTimeoutException e) {
    throw new ExternalServiceTimeoutException("Payment service timeout", e);
}
```

Jangan lakukan:

```java
catch (Exception e) {
    throw new RuntimeException(e);
}
```

Lebih baik contextual:

```java
catch (SQLException e) {
    throw new OrderPersistenceException(
        "Failed to load order " + orderId,
        e
    );
}
```

---

# 18. Try-with-resources

```java
try (var input = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
    return input.readAllBytes();
}
```

Untuk JDBC:

```java
try (
    var connection = dataSource.getConnection();
    var statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)
) {
    statement.setString(1, status);
    try (var rs = statement.executeQuery()) {
        while (rs.next()) {
            // map row
        }
    }
}
```

Rule: resource yang implement `AutoCloseable` sebaiknya dibuka dalam try-with-resources.

---

# 19. Date and Time API

Gunakan `java.time`, bukan `Date` / `Calendar`.

| Type | Gunakan Untuk |
|---|---|
| `Instant` | Timestamp universal UTC |
| `LocalDate` | Tanggal tanpa waktu |
| `LocalTime` | Jam tanpa tanggal |
| `LocalDateTime` | Tanggal + jam tanpa timezone |
| `ZonedDateTime` | Date-time dengan timezone |
| `OffsetDateTime` | Date-time dengan offset |
| `Duration` | Selisih waktu berbasis detik/nano |
| `Period` | Selisih tanggal berbasis tahun/bulan/hari |

Enterprise rule:

```text
Store timestamp as Instant / timestamptz.
Display using user/business timezone.
Do not store business time as LocalDateTime unless timezone irrelevant.
```

Contoh:

```java
Instant now = Instant.now();

ZonedDateTime jakartaTime = now.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

Duration:

```java
Duration timeout = Duration.ofSeconds(30);
```

---

# 20. BigDecimal

Untuk money, jangan pakai `double`.

```java
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal("100.50");
```

Buruk:

```java
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal(100.50);
```

Comparison:

```java
amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0
```

Jangan:

```java
amount.equals(BigDecimal.ZERO)
```

Karena scale berpengaruh:

```java
new BigDecimal("1.0").equals(new BigDecimal("1.00")) // false
```

Rounding:

```java
BigDecimal result = amount.divide(
    divisor,
    2,
    RoundingMode.HALF_UP
);
```

---

# 21. Immutability

Immutable object:

```java
public final class CustomerId {
    private final UUID value;

    public CustomerId(UUID value) {
        this.value = Objects.requireNonNull(value);
    }

    public UUID value() {
        return value;
    }
}
```

Dengan record:

```java
public record CustomerId(UUID value) {
    public CustomerId {
        Objects.requireNonNull(value);
    }
}
```

Manfaat immutability:

| Benefit | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Thread-safe by default | Lebih aman di concurrent system |
| Easier reasoning | State tidak berubah diam-diam |
| Better testing | Predictable |
| Safer caching | Tidak mudah corrupt |

---

# 22. Equals and HashCode

Jika object masuk `HashMap`, `HashSet`, atau dipakai deduplication, `equals/hashCode` penting.

Record otomatis aman:

```java
public record ProductCode(String value) {}
```

Manual:

```java
@Override
public boolean equals(Object o) {
    if (this == o) return true;
    if (!(o instanceof Customer other)) return false;
    return Objects.equals(id, other.id);
}

@Override
public int hashCode() {
    return Objects.hash(id);
}
```

Entity rule:

```text
Untuk JPA entity, hati-hati equals/hashCode berbasis generated ID
karena ID bisa null sebelum persist.
```

---

# 23. Null Handling

Defensive rule:

```java
public User(String name) {
    this.name = Objects.requireNonNull(name, "name is required");
}
```

String blank:

```java
if (name == null || name.isBlank()) {
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("name is required");
}
```

Collection:

```java
List<Item> safeItems = items == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(items);
```

Prefer empty collection over null:

```java
return List.of();
```

Bukan:

```java
return null;
```

---

# 24. Concurrency Basics

Thread:

```java
Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
    doWork();
});
thread.start();
```

Executor:

```java
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(10);

Future<String> future = executor.submit(() -> loadData());

String result = future.get();

executor.shutdown();
```

CompletableFuture:

```java
CompletableFuture<User> userFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> userClient.getUser(id));

CompletableFuture<Order> orderFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> orderClient.getOrder(orderId));

CompletableFuture<Result> result =
    userFuture.thenCombine(orderFuture, Result::new);
```

Important:

| Concept | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Thread | Unit eksekusi |
| Executor | Thread pool abstraction |
| Future | Result nanti |
| CompletableFuture | Async composition |
| Lock | Mutual exclusion |
| Atomic | Lock-free atomic operation |
| Concurrent collection | Collection aman untuk concurrency tertentu |

---

# 25. Virtual Threads Java 21+

Virtual threads final di JDK 21 dan ditujukan untuk meningkatkan scalability aplikasi bergaya thread-per-request, terutama I/O-bound workload. ([Oracle](https://www.oracle.com/java/technologies/javase/21-relnote-issues.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java
try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
    Future<String> future = executor.submit(() -> callExternalApi());
    String result = future.get();
}
```

Mental model:

| Platform Thread | Virtual Thread |
|---|---|
| Mahal | Murah |
| Jumlah terbatas | Bisa sangat banyak |
| Mapped ke OS thread | Managed JVM |
| Cocok untuk CPU-bound terbatas | Cocok untuk blocking I/O |

Cocok untuk:

```text
HTTP request handling
DB calls
External API calls
File/network I/O
Thread-per-task style
```

Tidak otomatis menyelesaikan:

```text
Database connection pool bottleneck
External API rate limit
Lock contention
CPU saturation
Bad transaction design
```

Penting:

```text
Virtual thread ≠ infinite throughput.
Bottleneck tetap bisa pindah ke DB, connection pool, lock, rate limiter, atau downstream service.
```

---

# 26. Synchronization

```java
public synchronized void increment() {
    count++;
}
```

Lock manual:

```java
private final Lock lock = new ReentrantLock();

public void update() {
    lock.lock();
    try {
        // critical section
    } finally {
        lock.unlock();
    }
}
```

Atomic:

```java
AtomicInteger counter = new AtomicInteger();

counter.incrementAndGet();
```

ConcurrentHashMap:

```java
ConcurrentHashMap<String, LongAdder> counters = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

counters.computeIfAbsent("orders", key -> new LongAdder())
        .increment();
```

Rule:

| Problem | Tool |
|---|---|
| Simple counter | `AtomicInteger`, `LongAdder` |
| Shared map | `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| Critical section | `synchronized` / `Lock` |
| Producer-consumer | `BlockingQueue` |
| Async composition | `CompletableFuture` |
| High I/O concurrency | Virtual threads |

---

# 27. HTTP Client

Java punya built-in HTTP Client.

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
    .build();

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/orders"))
    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
    .GET()
    .build();

HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
);
```

Async:

```java
CompletableFuture<HttpResponse<String>> future =
    client.sendAsync(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
```

Enterprise concern:

| Concern | Praktik |
|---|---|
| Timeout | Always set |
| Retry | Only idempotent operation unless safe |
| Circuit breaker | Use library/framework |
| Logging | Jangan log token/PII |
| TLS | Validate certificate |
| Backpressure | Jangan unlimited async fan-out |

---

# 28. NIO Files

Read string:

```java
String content = Files.readString(Path.of("config.json"));
```

Write string:

```java
Files.writeString(Path.of("output.txt"), "hello");
```

Read lines:

```java
List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(path);
```

Stream large file:

```java
try (Stream<String> lines = Files.lines(path)) {
    lines
        .filter(line -> !line.isBlank())
        .forEach(System.out::println);
}
```

Copy:

```java
Files.copy(source, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
```

Check:

```java
Files.exists(path);
Files.isRegularFile(path);
Files.isDirectory(path);
```

---

# 29. Serialization: JSON/XML

Java SE tidak punya JSON binding standard utama seperti Jackson. Di enterprise biasanya:

| Need | Common Library |
|---|---|
| JSON | Jackson, JSON-B |
| XML | JAXB / Jakarta XML Binding |
| REST | JAX-RS provider |
| Validation | Jakarta Bean Validation |

Jackson DTO dengan record:

```java
public record UserResponse(
    UUID id,
    String name,
    String email
) {}
```

Jackson modern umumnya nyaman dengan record, tapi pastikan versi library mendukung Java version yang dipakai.

---

# 30. Modules

`module-info.java`:

```java
module com.company.orders {
    requires java.net.http;
    requires java.sql;

    exports com.company.orders.api;
}
```

Di enterprise backend, JPMS sering tidak dipakai penuh karena framework reflection-heavy. Tapi tetap penting paham:

| Keyword | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `module` | Deklarasi module |
| `requires` | Dependency module |
| `exports` | Package bisa dipakai module lain |
| `opens` | Package bisa direfleksi |
| `requires transitive` | Dependency diekspos ke consumer |

Untuk framework:

```java
opens com.company.orders.dto to com.fasterxml.jackson.databind;
```

---

# 31. Reflection

```java
Class<?> clazz = Class.forName("com.company.User");
Method method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("name");
```

Reflection dipakai oleh:

```text
Jackson
Hibernate/JPA
Jakarta REST
CDI/Spring DI
Validation
Testing frameworks
Serialization frameworks
```

Risiko:

| Risiko | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Runtime error | Tidak ketahuan compile-time |
| Performance overhead | Biasanya manageable, tapi tetap ada |
| Encapsulation breach | Bisa akses private |
| Native image issue | Perlu metadata config |
| Module issue | Perlu `opens` |

---

# 32. Annotation

```java
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.METHOD)
public @interface Audited {
    String value() default "";
}
```

Common annotation:

| Annotation | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `@Override` | Method override |
| `@Deprecated` | API tidak disarankan |
| `@FunctionalInterface` | Interface single abstract method |
| `@SuppressWarnings` | Suppress warning |
| `@Retention` | Annotation disimpan sampai mana |
| `@Target` | Annotation boleh dipasang di mana |

---

# 33. Functional Interface

```java
@FunctionalInterface
public interface Validator<T> {
    boolean validate(T value);
}
```

Lambda:

```java
Validator<String> notBlank = value -> value != null && !value.isBlank();
```

Built-in:

| Interface | Signature |
|---|---|
| `Function<T, R>` | `T -> R` |
| `Consumer<T>` | `T -> void` |
| `Supplier<T>` | `() -> T` |
| `Predicate<T>` | `T -> boolean` |
| `BiFunction<T, U, R>` | `(T, U) -> R` |
| `UnaryOperator<T>` | `T -> T` |
| `BinaryOperator<T>` | `(T, T) -> T` |

---

# 34. Generics

```java
public class Page<T> {
    private final List<T> items;

    public Page(List<T> items) {
        this.items = List.copyOf(items);
    }

    public List<T> items() {
        return items;
    }
}
```

Wildcard:

```java
public void printAll(List<? extends Number> numbers) {
    numbers.forEach(System.out::println);
}
```

PECS rule:

```text
Producer Extends, Consumer Super
```

Producer:

```java
List<? extends Number> numbers;
```

Consumer:

```java
List<? super Integer> integers;
```

---

# 35. Logging

Jangan:

```java
System.out.println("Order created");
```

Gunakan logger:

```java
private static final Logger log =
    LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderService.class);

log.info("Order created: orderId={}", orderId);
```

Rules:

| Level | Use |
|---|---|
| `TRACE` | Very detailed diagnostic |
| `DEBUG` | Developer troubleshooting |
| `INFO` | Business/system event normal |
| `WARN` | Unexpected but recoverable |
| `ERROR` | Failure requiring attention |

Jangan log:

```text
password
access token
refresh token
secret key
full NRIC/NIK/passport
credit card
sensitive payload
large binary
```

Good structured logging:

```java
log.info(
    "Order submitted: orderId={}, customerId={}, status={}",
    order.id(),
    order.customerId(),
    order.status()
);
```

---

# 36. Defensive Coding

Validate boundary input:

```java
public void submitOrder(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
    Objects.requireNonNull(command, "command is required");

    if (command.lines().isEmpty()) {
        throw new InvalidOrderException("order must have at least one line");
    }
}
```

Copy mutable input:

```java
public record Order(List<OrderLine> lines) {
    public Order {
        lines = List.copyOf(lines);
    }
}
```

Avoid leaking mutable collection:

```java
public List<OrderLine> lines() {
    return List.copyOf(lines);
}
```

---

# 37. Domain Modeling Example

Buruk:

```java
public void updateStatus(String status) {
    this.status = status;
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
public void submit() {
    if (status != OrderStatus.DRAFT) {
        throw new InvalidTransitionException(status, OrderStatus.SUBMITTED);
    }
    this.status = OrderStatus.SUBMITTED;
}
```

State transition eksplisit:

```java
public boolean canTransitionTo(OrderStatus target) {
    return switch (this.status) {
        case DRAFT -> target == OrderStatus.SUBMITTED;
        case SUBMITTED -> target == OrderStatus.APPROVED || target == OrderStatus.REJECTED;
        case APPROVED, REJECTED -> false;
    };
}
```

---

# 38. Common Enterprise Patterns

## Command

```java
public record SubmitOrderCommand(UUID orderId, UUID userId) {}
```

## Result

```java
public sealed interface SubmitOrderResult
    permits SubmitOrderSuccess, SubmitOrderRejected {
}

public record SubmitOrderSuccess(UUID orderId) implements SubmitOrderResult {}
public record SubmitOrderRejected(String reason) implements SubmitOrderResult {}
```

## Service

```java
public class SubmitOrderUseCase {
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;

    public SubmitOrderUseCase(OrderRepository orderRepository) {
        this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
    }

    public SubmitOrderResult execute(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
        Order order = orderRepository.findById(command.orderId())
            .orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(command.orderId()));

        order.submit(command.userId());

        orderRepository.save(order);

        return new SubmitOrderSuccess(order.id());
    }
}
```

---

# 39. JVM Memory Basics

JVM memory simplified:

```text
Heap
├── Young generation
├── Old generation

Non-heap
├── Metaspace
├── Code cache
├── Thread stacks
├── Direct memory
└── Native memory
```

Important flags:

```bash
java -Xms512m -Xmx512m -jar app.jar
```

Container-aware sizing:

```bash
java \
  -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75 \
  -XX:InitialRAMPercentage=50 \
  -jar app.jar
```

Common GC options:

```bash
# G1 default in many modern JDKs
-XX:+UseG1GC

# ZGC for low-latency workloads
-XX:+UseZGC
```

For Java 17+, do not tune GC blindly. Start from measurement:

```text
GC logs
heap dump
JFR
CPU profile
allocation profile
latency SLO
container memory limit
```

---

# 40. Java Flight Recorder / JFR

Start with JFR:

```bash
java \
  -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=recording.jfr,duration=60s,settings=profile \
  -jar app.jar
```

Use for:

```text
CPU hotspot
allocation hotspot
lock contention
thread behavior
GC pause
socket I/O
file I/O
method profiling
```

JFR is one of the most valuable tools for senior-level Java debugging.

---

# 41. Thread Dump

Generate:

```bash
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
```

Or:

```bash
jstack <pid>
```

Look for:

```text
BLOCKED
WAITING
TIMED_WAITING
deadlock
too many threads
thread pool exhaustion
database pool wait
HTTP client wait
```

---

# 42. Heap Dump

Generate:

```bash
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /tmp/heap.hprof
```

Use when:

```text
OutOfMemoryError
suspected memory leak
unexpected object retention
large cache
large collection
classloader leak
```

Analyze with:

```text
Eclipse MAT
VisualVM
JProfiler
YourKit
IntelliJ profiler
```

---

# 43. Common JVM Failures

| Failure | Possible Root Cause |
|---|---|
| `OutOfMemoryError: Java heap space` | Heap insufficient, leak, huge collection |
| `OutOfMemoryError: Metaspace` | Classloader leak, dynamic class generation |
| `StackOverflowError` | Recursive call, circular mapping |
| High GC pause | Allocation pressure, heap sizing, old-gen pressure |
| High CPU | Busy loop, regex, serialization, crypto, GC |
| Thread exhaustion | Unbounded executor, blocking I/O |
| Connection pool exhaustion | Slow DB/downstream, leaked connection |
| Deadlock | Lock ordering issue |

---

# 44. Jakarta REST / JAX-RS Relevance

Java 17+ di Jakarta REST biasanya muncul seperti ini:

```java
@Path("/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class OrderResource {
    private final SubmitOrderUseCase submitOrderUseCase;

    public OrderResource(SubmitOrderUseCase submitOrderUseCase) {
        this.submitOrderUseCase = submitOrderUseCase;
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/submit")
    public Response submit(@PathParam("id") UUID id) {
        var result = submitOrderUseCase.execute(new SubmitOrderCommand(id));

        return switch (result) {
            case SubmitOrderSuccess success ->
                Response.ok(success).build();

            case SubmitOrderRejected rejected ->
                Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                    .entity(rejected)
                    .build();
        };
    }
}
```

Modern Java features yang sangat berguna di JAX-RS:

| Feature | Use |
|---|---|
| Record | Request/response DTO |
| Sealed interface | API result / error model |
| Switch expression | Response mapping |
| Optional | Repository lookup |
| Text block | Test JSON |
| Virtual thread | Blocking REST workloads, tergantung runtime support |

---

# 45. PostgreSQL/JDBC Relevance

Prepared statement:

```java
String sql = """
    SELECT id, status, created_at
    FROM orders
    WHERE id = ?
    """;

try (
    var connection = dataSource.getConnection();
    var ps = connection.prepareStatement(sql)
) {
    ps.setObject(1, orderId);

    try (var rs = ps.executeQuery()) {
        if (!rs.next()) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        return Optional.of(mapOrder(rs));
    }
}
```

Transaction boundary biasanya jangan manual di semua tempat. Di enterprise, transaction biasanya dikelola framework:

```text
Jakarta Transactions
Spring @Transactional
JTA
Camunda transaction boundary
```

---

# 46. Testing

JUnit 5 style:

```java
@Test
void shouldSubmitDraftOrder() {
    Order order = Order.draft();

    order.submit(userId);

    assertEquals(OrderStatus.SUBMITTED, order.status());
}
```

Assert exception:

```java
@Test
void shouldRejectInvalidTransition() {
    Order order = Order.approved();

    assertThrows(InvalidTransitionException.class, () -> {
        order.submit(userId);
    });
}
```

Testing focus:

| Layer | Test Type |
|---|---|
| Domain | Unit test |
| Application service | Unit/integration with fake repository |
| Persistence | Integration test with real DB/Testcontainers |
| REST | API test |
| External client | Contract/mock server test |
| Workflow | State transition/process test |

---

# 47. Code Review Checklist Java 17+

Gunakan checklist ini saat baca codebase baru:

```text
[ ] Apakah boundary input divalidasi?
[ ] Apakah null handling jelas?
[ ] Apakah collection immutable saat perlu?
[ ] Apakah DTO bisa jadi record?
[ ] Apakah state finite bisa jadi enum/sealed type?
[ ] Apakah exception punya context?
[ ] Apakah timeout external call eksplisit?
[ ] Apakah retry aman/idempotent?
[ ] Apakah transaction boundary jelas?
[ ] Apakah logging tidak bocorkan sensitive data?
[ ] Apakah thread pool bounded?
[ ] Apakah resource ditutup?
[ ] Apakah query SQL pakai prepared statement?
[ ] Apakah BigDecimal dipakai untuk money?
[ ] Apakah Date/Time pakai java.time?
[ ] Apakah test cover invariant penting?
```

---

# 48. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah |
|---|---|
| Fat controller/resource | Business logic tersebar |
| Stringly typed status | Transition tidak aman |
| `catch Exception` lalu swallow | Failure hilang |
| `Optional.get()` sembarangan | Runtime failure |
| `parallelStream()` sembarangan | Pool contention |
| Unbounded executor | Thread explosion |
| No timeout HTTP call | Request menggantung |
| Mutable shared state | Race condition |
| Log full payload | Security/privacy risk |
| BigDecimal dari double | Precision bug |
| Return null collection | NPE risk |
| Static utility berlebihan | Sulit test/extend |
| Reflection tanpa guard | Runtime fragility |

---

# 49. Practical Java 17+ Style Guide

Prefer:

```java
public record OrderSummary(
    UUID id,
    OrderStatus status,
    Instant createdAt
) {}
```

Prefer:

```java
List<OrderSummary> summaries = orders.stream()
    .map(order -> new OrderSummary(
        order.id(),
        order.status(),
        order.createdAt()
    ))
    .toList();
```

Prefer:

```java
return switch (status) {
    case DRAFT -> allowedDraftActions();
    case SUBMITTED -> allowedSubmittedActions();
    case APPROVED -> allowedApprovedActions();
    case REJECTED -> List.of();
};
```

Prefer:

```java
throw new OrderNotFoundException(orderId);
```

Hindari:

```java
throw new RuntimeException("error");
```

Prefer:

```java
private final Clock clock;

public OrderService(Clock clock) {
    this.clock = clock;
}

Instant now = clock.instant();
```

Daripada:

```java
Instant now = Instant.now();
```

Karena `Clock` memudahkan testing.

---

# 50. Senior-Level Mental Model

Untuk cepat produktif di Java 17+ codebase, baca sistem dengan urutan ini:

```text
1. Entry point
   REST resource, scheduler, consumer, worker, Camunda delegate

2. Use case
   Application service / command handler

3. Domain model
   Entity, aggregate, state, invariant, transition

4. Persistence
   Repository, mapper, transaction, SQL

5. Integration
   HTTP client, message broker, external system

6. Cross-cutting
   Security, logging, tracing, metrics, retry, timeout

7. Runtime
   Threading, memory, GC, container limit, deployment config
```

Pertanyaan penting saat onboarding:

```text
Apa state utama sistem?
Apa invariant yang tidak boleh dilanggar?
Siapa owner transaction?
Apa yang terjadi jika downstream timeout?
Apa operasi yang idempotent?
Apa failure yang boleh retry?
Apa data yang immutable?
Apa data yang eventually consistent?
Apa yang dilog untuk audit?
Apa yang tidak boleh dilog?
Apa bottleneck runtime: DB, CPU, memory, lock, network, atau thread?
```

---

# 51. Minimal “Must Know” untuk Onboarding Cepat

Kalau waktu terbatas, prioritaskan ini:

| Priority | Topic |
|---|---|
| P0 | Records, switch expression, pattern matching |
| P0 | Collections, Optional, Stream |
| P0 | Exception handling and error mapping |
| P0 | java.time |
| P0 | BigDecimal |
| P0 | Maven Java release config |
| P1 | Virtual threads |
| P1 | Concurrency basics |
| P1 | JVM memory and GC basics |
| P1 | JFR/thread dump/heap dump |
| P1 | HTTP client and timeout |
| P2 | Modules |
| P2 | Reflection/annotation internals |
| P2 | Advanced generics |
| P2 | GC tuning |

---

## Ringkasan paling penting

Java 17+ yang paling berdampak untuk enterprise backend adalah:

```text
Records             -> DTO/value object lebih ringkas
Sealed classes      -> domain/result/state lebih aman
Pattern matching    -> type handling lebih bersih
Switch expression   -> decision mapping lebih eksplisit
java.time           -> time handling benar
Optional            -> explicit absence
Stream API          -> data transformation
Virtual threads     -> scalable blocking I/O di Java 21+
JFR/thread dump     -> production debugging
Maven release       -> build compatibility yang benar
```

Untuk codebase enterprise, senior engineer tidak cukup hanya tahu fitur bahasa. Yang paling penting adalah melihat **invariant, boundary, transaction, concurrency, failure mode, observability, dan runtime behavior**.

## Response:
7/12/2026, 8:17:09 PM

Saya akan pecah menjadi empat area yang sering dipakai saat membaca codebase Java enterprise: representasi data, aturan type system, pilihan struktur data, lalu pipeline processing dengan Stream.

Intinya: cheat sheet ini menjawab **bagaimana Java merepresentasikan data, bagaimana type-safety bekerja, kapan memilih collection tertentu, dan bagaimana memproses data dengan Stream secara benar**.

# Java 17+ Cheat Sheet: Data Type, Type System, Data Structure, Collection, Stream

---

# 1. Mental Model Utama

Java punya empat lapisan penting:

```text
Data Type
  ↓
Type System
  ↓
Data Structure / Collection
  ↓
Processing Model / Stream
```

Artinya:

| Area | Pertanyaan Utama |
|---|---|
| Data Type | “Nilai ini direpresentasikan sebagai apa?” |
| Type System | “Compiler menjamin apa?” |
| Data Structure | “Bagaimana data disimpan dan diakses?” |
| Processing Model | “Bagaimana data diproses, ditransformasi, difilter, dan diagregasi?” |

---

# 2. Java Data Type

Java punya dua kategori besar:

```text
Java Type
├── Primitive Type
└── Reference Type
```

---

## 2.1 Primitive Types

Primitive adalah value langsung, bukan object.

| Type | Size | Contoh | Use Case |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `byte` | 8-bit | `byte b = 1;` | binary data, buffer |
| `short` | 16-bit | `short s = 10;` | jarang dipakai |
| `int` | 32-bit | `int count = 100;` | default integer |
| `long` | 64-bit | `long id = 100L;` | ID, counter besar |
| `float` | 32-bit | `float f = 1.5f;` | jarang di business app |
| `double` | 64-bit | `double d = 1.5;` | scientific/approx number |
| `char` | 16-bit | `char c = 'A';` | UTF-16 code unit |
| `boolean` | JVM-dependent | `boolean ok = true;` | true/false |

Contoh:

```java
int retryCount = 3;
long userId = 123456789L;
boolean active = true;
double ratio = 0.75;
char grade = 'A';
```

Default pilihan:

| Need | Default Type |
|---|---|
| Whole number | `int` |
| Large whole number | `long` |
| Money | `BigDecimal`, bukan `double` |
| True/false | `boolean` |
| Text | `String` |
| Timestamp | `Instant` / `OffsetDateTime` |
| Date only | `LocalDate` |

---

## 2.2 Primitive vs Reference Type

| Aspek | Primitive | Reference |
|---|---|---|
| Menyimpan | Value langsung | Reference ke object |
| Bisa `null`? | Tidak | Ya |
| Method? | Tidak | Ya |
| Contoh | `int`, `boolean` | `String`, `List`, `User` |
| Equality | `==` banding value | `==` banding reference |

Contoh:

```java
int a = 10;
int b = 10;

System.out.println(a == b); // true
```

Reference:

```java
String x = new String("hello");
String y = new String("hello");

System.out.println(x == y);      // false
System.out.println(x.equals(y)); // true
```

Rule penting:

```text
Primitive pakai ==
Object pakai equals()
```

---

## 2.3 Wrapper Types

Setiap primitive punya wrapper object.

| Primitive | Wrapper |
|---|---|
| `byte` | `Byte` |
| `short` | `Short` |
| `int` | `Integer` |
| `long` | `Long` |
| `float` | `Float` |
| `double` | `Double` |
| `char` | `Character` |
| `boolean` | `Boolean` |

Contoh:

```java
int primitive = 10;
Integer wrapper = 10;
```

Autoboxing:

```java
Integer x = 10; // int -> Integer
```

Unboxing:

```java
int y = x; // Integer -> int
```

Bahaya:

```java
Integer value = null;
int number = value; // NullPointerException
```

Gunakan wrapper saat:

| Use Case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Value bisa null | `Integer age` |
| Collection | `List<Integer>` |
| Generic type | `Optional<Long>` |
| Framework binding | JSON/JPA/JAX-RS DTO |

Gunakan primitive saat:

| Use Case | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Required value | `int count` |
| Performance-sensitive loop | `long total` |
| Boolean flag wajib | `boolean active` |

---

## 2.4 String

`String` adalah immutable reference type.

```java
String name = "Fajar";
String upper = name.toUpperCase();
```

`String` tidak berubah; method menghasilkan object baru.

```java
String s = "hello";
s.toUpperCase();

System.out.println(s); // hello
```

Harus:

```java
s = s.toUpperCase();
```

String comparison:

```java
if ("ACTIVE".equals(status)) {
    // safe even if status is null
}
```

Hindari:

```java
if (status.equals("ACTIVE")) {
    // NPE if status is null
}
```

String building:

```java
String message = "Order " + orderId + " submitted";
```

Untuk loop besar:

```java
StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();

for (String item : items) {
    builder.append(item).append(",");
}

String result = builder.toString();
```

---

## 2.5 BigDecimal

Untuk uang, tarif, billing, tax, dan financial calculation, gunakan `BigDecimal`.

Benar:

```java
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal("100.50");
```

Buruk:

```java
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal(100.50);
```

Comparison:

```java
if (amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0) {
    // positive
}
```

Hati-hati:

```java
new BigDecimal("1.0").equals(new BigDecimal("1.00")); // false
new BigDecimal("1.0").compareTo(new BigDecimal("1.00")); // 0
```

Rule:

| Operation | Gunakan |
|---|---|
| Equality numeric | `compareTo()` |
| Exact object equality | `equals()` |
| Money calculation | `BigDecimal` |
| Approximate ratio | `double` |

---

## 2.6 Date and Time Types

Gunakan `java.time`.

| Type | Makna |
|---|---|
| `Instant` | Timestamp global UTC |
| `LocalDate` | Tanggal tanpa jam |
| `LocalTime` | Jam tanpa tanggal |
| `LocalDateTime` | Tanggal + jam tanpa timezone |
| `OffsetDateTime` | Date-time dengan offset |
| `ZonedDateTime` | Date-time dengan timezone |
| `Duration` | Selisih waktu berbasis detik/nano |
| `Period` | Selisih tanggal berbasis hari/bulan/tahun |

Contoh:

```java
Instant now = Instant.now();

LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();

ZonedDateTime jakartaTime =
    now.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

Rule enterprise:

```text
Store timestamp as Instant / timestamptz.
Display using business/user timezone.
Avoid LocalDateTime for cross-timezone event timestamp.
```

---

## 2.7 Enum

Enum cocok untuk finite value.

```java
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED
}
```

Use:

```java
OrderStatus status = OrderStatus.SUBMITTED;
```

Switch:

```java
String label = switch (status) {
    case DRAFT -> "Draft";
    case SUBMITTED -> "Submitted";
    case APPROVED -> "Approved";
    case REJECTED -> "Rejected";
};
```

Enum cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Status finite | Dynamic config value |
| Role internal | User-defined value |
| Type/category stable | Data yang sering berubah dari DB |
| State sederhana | Workflow kompleks dengan rule dinamis |

---

## 2.8 Record

Record adalah immutable data carrier.

```java
public record UserDto(
    UUID id,
    String name,
    String email
) {}
```

Access:

```java
userDto.id();
userDto.name();
userDto.email();
```

Bukan:

```java
userDto.getName();
```

Record otomatis punya:

```text
constructor
accessor
equals
hashCode
toString
```

Dengan validasi:

```java
public record Money(BigDecimal amount, String currency) {
    public Money {
        if (amount == null || amount.signum() < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("amount must be positive");
        }

        if (currency == null || currency.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency is required");
        }
    }
}
```

Cocok:

| Cocok | Hindari |
|---|---|
| DTO | JPA entity mutable |
| API response | Object dengan lifecycle kompleks |
| Command object | Object dengan banyak state mutation |
| Value object | Proxy/lazy-loading heavy object |
| Projection | Object yang butuh inheritance |

---

## 2.9 Array

Array fixed-size.

```java
String[] names = new String[3];

names[0] = "A";
names[1] = "B";
names[2] = "C";
```

Array literal:

```java
int[] numbers = {1, 2, 3};
```

Access:

```java
int first = numbers[0];
int length = numbers.length;
```

Array cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Lebih Baik Pakai Collection |
|---|---|
| Fixed-size data | Dynamic size |
| Low-level API | Business collection |
| Performance-sensitive | Frequent add/remove |
| Byte buffer | Domain list |

---

## 2.10 Optional

`Optional<T>` merepresentasikan value yang mungkin tidak ada.

```java
Optional<User> user = userRepository.findById(id);
```

Use:

```java
User user = userRepository.findById(id)
    .orElseThrow(() -> new UserNotFoundException(id));
```

Map:

```java
String email = userRepository.findById(id)
    .map(User::email)
    .orElse("unknown@example.com");
```

Jangan:

```java
if (user.isPresent()) {
    User u = user.get();
}
```

Lebih baik:

```java
user.ifPresent(this::process);
```

Jangan pakai `Optional` untuk:

| Hindari | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Field JPA entity | Framework compatibility |
| Method parameter | Call-site jelek |
| Collection | Pakai empty list |
| DTO JSON publik | Bisa menyulitkan binding |

---

# 3. Java Type System

Java type system bersifat:

```text
Static
Strong
Nominal
Mostly object-oriented
Generic with type erasure
```

---

## 3.1 Static Typing

Type dicek saat compile time.

```java
int count = 10;
count = "hello"; // compile error
```

Benefit:

| Benefit | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Early error detection | Banyak bug ketahuan sebelum runtime |
| IDE support | Refactor lebih aman |
| API contract jelas | Method signature jadi dokumentasi |
| Safer large codebase | Cocok untuk enterprise |

---

## 3.2 Strong Typing

Java tidak melakukan implicit conversion sembarangan.

```java
String value = "10";
int number = value; // compile error
```

Harus explicit:

```java
int number = Integer.parseInt(value);
```

Numeric widening allowed:

```java
int x = 10;
long y = x; // ok
```

Narrowing perlu cast:

```java
long big = 100L;
int small = (int) big;
```

Bahaya:

```java
long big = 3_000_000_000L;
int overflow = (int) big;
```

---

## 3.3 Nominal Typing

Java melihat nama/type declaration, bukan struktur.

```java
class CustomerId {
    UUID value;
}

class OrderId {
    UUID value;
}
```

Walaupun struktur sama, mereka type berbeda.

```java
CustomerId customerId = new CustomerId();
OrderId orderId = customerId; // compile error
```

Ini bagus untuk domain modeling:

```java
public record CustomerId(UUID value) {}
public record OrderId(UUID value) {}
```

Sehingga tidak tertukar:

```java
void loadOrder(OrderId orderId) {}
```

---

## 3.4 Reference Subtyping

Inheritance:

```java
class Animal {}

class Dog extends Animal {}
```

Subtyping:

```java
Dog dog = new Dog();
Animal animal = dog;
```

Interface:

```java
interface Repository {}

class UserRepository implements Repository {}
```

Use:

```java
Repository repository = new UserRepository();
```

Rule:

```text
Child can be assigned to parent.
Parent cannot be assigned to child without cast.
```

---

## 3.5 Casting

Upcast aman:

```java
Dog dog = new Dog();
Animal animal = dog;
```

Downcast berisiko:

```java
Animal animal = new Dog();
Dog dog = (Dog) animal;
```

Bisa error:

```java
Animal animal = new Cat();
Dog dog = (Dog) animal; // ClassCastException
```

Modern pattern matching:

```java
if (animal instanceof Dog dog) {
    dog.bark();
}
```

---

## 3.6 `var`

`var` adalah local variable type inference.

```java
var name = "Fajar";       // String
var count = 10;           // int
var users = List.of("A"); // List<String>
```

`var` bukan dynamic typing.

```java
var x = 10;
x = "hello"; // compile error
```

Gunakan saat type obvious:

```java
var order = new Order(orderId);
```

Hindari saat tidak obvious:

```java
var result = service.process(input);
```

---

## 3.7 Generics

Generics memberi type-safety untuk container dan abstraction.

```java
List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();

names.add("A");
names.add("B");
```

Compile-time safe:

```java
names.add(123); // compile error
```

Generic class:

```java
public class Page<T> {
    private final List<T> items;

    public Page(List<T> items) {
        this.items = List.copyOf(items);
    }

    public List<T> items() {
        return items;
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
Page<UserDto> page = new Page<>(users);
```

---

## 3.8 Generic Invariance

Java generics invariant.

```java
List<String> strings = List.of("A", "B");

List<Object> objects = strings; // compile error
```

Kenapa?

Kalau boleh, ini akan berbahaya:

```java
objects.add(123);
String value = strings.get(0); // corrupt type expectation
```

---

## 3.9 Wildcards

### `? extends T`

Untuk producer.

```java
void printNumbers(List<? extends Number> numbers) {
    for (Number number : numbers) {
        System.out.println(number);
    }
}
```

Bisa menerima:

```java
List<Integer>
List<Long>
List<Double>
```

Tapi tidak aman untuk add:

```java
numbers.add(10); // compile error
```

---

### `? super T`

Untuk consumer.

```java
void addIntegers(List<? super Integer> numbers) {
    numbers.add(1);
    numbers.add(2);
}
```

Bisa menerima:

```java
List<Integer>
List<Number>
List<Object>
```

---

## 3.10 PECS Rule

```text
Producer Extends
Consumer Super
```

| Situation | Use |
|---|---|
| Method membaca data dari collection | `? extends T` |
| Method menulis data ke collection | `? super T` |
| Method membaca dan menulis | concrete `T` |

Example:

```java
void copy(
    List<? extends Number> source,
    List<? super Number> target
) {
    target.addAll(source);
}
```

---

## 3.11 Type Erasure

Java generics mostly hilang saat runtime.

Compile-time:

```java
List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
List<Integer> numbers = new ArrayList<>();
```

Runtime kira-kira:

```java
List names = new ArrayList();
List numbers = new ArrayList();
```

Implikasi:

```java
if (list instanceof List<String>) {
    // illegal
}
```

Tidak bisa:

```java
new T(); // tidak bisa
```

Workaround:

```java
class JsonReader<T> {
    private final Class<T> type;

    JsonReader(Class<T> type) {
        this.type = type;
    }
}
```

---

## 3.12 Raw Type

Buruk:

```java
List list = new ArrayList();

list.add("A");
list.add(123);
```

Masalah muncul saat runtime:

```java
String value = (String) list.get(1); // ClassCastException
```

Baik:

```java
List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
```

Rule:

```text
Avoid raw types unless interacting with very old legacy APIs.
```

---

## 3.13 Sealed Type

Sealed type membatasi subtype yang valid.

```java
public sealed interface PaymentResult
    permits PaymentSuccess, PaymentFailed, PaymentPending {
}

public record PaymentSuccess(String transactionId)
    implements PaymentResult {}

public record PaymentFailed(String reason)
    implements PaymentResult {}

public record PaymentPending(String reference)
    implements PaymentResult {}
```

Diproses dengan switch:

```java
String message = switch (result) {
    case PaymentSuccess success ->
        "Success: " + success.transactionId();

    case PaymentFailed failed ->
        "Failed: " + failed.reason();

    case PaymentPending pending ->
        "Pending: " + pending.reference();
};
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
Result type
Domain event
Workflow state
Command variant
Error classification
Finite business condition
```

---

# 4. Data Structure & Collection

Collection hierarchy sederhana:

```text
Iterable
└── Collection
    ├── List
    ├── Set
    └── Queue
        └── Deque

Map is separate
```

---

## 4.1 Collection Interface Map

| Interface | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `Iterable<T>` | Bisa di-loop |
| `Collection<T>` | Kumpulan element |
| `List<T>` | Ordered, index-based, allow duplicate |
| `Set<T>` | Unique element |
| `Queue<T>` | FIFO-ish processing |
| `Deque<T>` | Double-ended queue |
| `Map<K,V>` | Key-value lookup |

---

## 4.2 List

List cocok untuk ordered sequence.

```java
List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();

names.add("A");
names.add("B");
names.add("A");
```

Duplicate boleh:

```java
[A, B, A]
```

Access by index:

```java
String first = names.get(0);
```

Implementasi umum:

| Implementation | Karakter |
|---|---|
| `ArrayList` | Default choice |
| `LinkedList` | Jarang perlu |
| `CopyOnWriteArrayList` | Read-heavy concurrent list |

Default:

```java
List<Order> orders = new ArrayList<>();
```

Gunakan `ArrayList` untuk mayoritas kasus.

---

## 4.3 Set

Set cocok untuk uniqueness.

```java
Set<String> roles = new HashSet<>();

roles.add("ADMIN");
roles.add("USER");
roles.add("ADMIN");
```

Hasil:

```text
ADMIN, USER
```

Implementasi:

| Implementation | Karakter |
|---|---|
| `HashSet` | Fast, unordered |
| `LinkedHashSet` | Unique + insertion order |
| `TreeSet` | Sorted |
| `EnumSet` | Efficient set untuk enum |

Contoh:

```java
Set<OrderStatus> terminalStatuses = EnumSet.of(
    OrderStatus.APPROVED,
    OrderStatus.REJECTED
);
```

---

## 4.4 Map

Map adalah key-value lookup.

```java
Map<UUID, User> usersById = new HashMap<>();

usersById.put(user.id(), user);
```

Access:

```java
User user = usersById.get(userId);
```

Implementasi:

| Implementation | Karakter |
|---|---|
| `HashMap` | Default choice |
| `LinkedHashMap` | Maintain insertion order |
| `TreeMap` | Sorted by key |
| `ConcurrentHashMap` | Concurrent access |
| `EnumMap` | Efficient map dengan enum key |

Contoh `EnumMap`:

```java
Map<OrderStatus, Integer> counts =
    new EnumMap<>(OrderStatus.class);

counts.put(OrderStatus.SUBMITTED, 10);
```

---

## 4.5 Queue

Queue cocok untuk processing order.

```java
Queue<String> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();

queue.offer("job-1");
queue.offer("job-2");

String job = queue.poll();
```

Method penting:

| Method | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `offer()` | Add, return boolean |
| `poll()` | Remove head, null if empty |
| `peek()` | Read head, null if empty |
| `add()` | Add, may throw |
| `remove()` | Remove, throws if empty |
| `element()` | Peek, throws if empty |

Prefer:

```java
offer()
poll()
peek()
```

Daripada:

```java
add()
remove()
element()
```

---

## 4.6 Deque

Deque bisa jadi stack atau queue.

```java
Deque<String> stack = new ArrayDeque<>();

stack.push("A");
stack.push("B");

String top = stack.pop();
```

Sebagai queue:

```java
Deque<String> deque = new ArrayDeque<>();

deque.addLast("A");
deque.addLast("B");

String first = deque.removeFirst();
```

Hindari `Stack` lama, prefer `ArrayDeque`.

---

## 4.7 Immutable Collection

Java factory methods:

```java
List<String> names = List.of("A", "B");
Set<String> roles = Set.of("ADMIN", "USER");
Map<String, Integer> score = Map.of(
    "A", 10,
    "B", 20
);
```

Immutable:

```java
names.add("C"); // UnsupportedOperationException
```

Copy immutable:

```java
List<String> safe = List.copyOf(input);
```

Rule:

```text
Use immutable collection at boundaries.
Use mutable collection inside local algorithm if needed.
```

Contoh record aman:

```java
public record Order(List<OrderLine> lines) {
    public Order {
        lines = List.copyOf(lines);
    }
}
```

---

## 4.8 Collection Selection Cheat Sheet

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Ordered list | `ArrayList` |
| Unique values | `HashSet` |
| Unique + stable order | `LinkedHashSet` |
| Sorted values | `TreeSet` |
| Lookup by key | `HashMap` |
| Lookup + stable order | `LinkedHashMap` |
| Sorted key lookup | `TreeMap` |
| Enum key lookup | `EnumMap` |
| Enum set | `EnumSet` |
| FIFO queue | `ArrayDeque` |
| Stack | `ArrayDeque` |
| Concurrent map | `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| Blocking producer-consumer | `BlockingQueue` |
| Read-heavy concurrent list | `CopyOnWriteArrayList` |

---

## 4.9 Big-O Practical Cheat Sheet

Typical average case:

| Structure | Get | Add | Remove | Contains |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `ArrayList` | O(1) by index | O(1) amortized | O(n) | O(n) |
| `LinkedList` | O(n) | O(1) if at node/end | O(1) if at node | O(n) |
| `HashSet` | N/A | O(1) | O(1) | O(1) |
| `TreeSet` | N/A | O(log n) | O(log n) | O(log n) |
| `HashMap` | O(1) | O(1) | O(1) | key O(1) |
| `TreeMap` | O(log n) | O(log n) | O(log n) | key O(log n) |

Practical rule:

```text
ArrayList for most lists.
HashMap for most lookup.
HashSet for most uniqueness.
TreeMap/TreeSet only when sorting is part of the requirement.
```

---

## 4.10 Equality and Hashing

Untuk `HashSet` dan `HashMap`, `equals()` dan `hashCode()` sangat penting.

```java
public record UserId(UUID value) {}
```

Record otomatis aman.

Manual:

```java
public final class UserId {
    private final UUID value;

    public UserId(UUID value) {
        this.value = Objects.requireNonNull(value);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (!(o instanceof UserId other)) return false;
        return Objects.equals(value, other.value);
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return Objects.hash(value);
    }
}
```

Kalau `equals/hashCode` salah:

```text
HashSet duplicate bisa kacau
HashMap lookup bisa gagal
Deduplication salah
Cache behavior salah
```

---

## 4.11 Null in Collections

Beberapa collection mengizinkan null, beberapa tidak.

```java
List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
list.add(null); // allowed
```

Tapi:

```java
List.of(null); // NullPointerException
```

Rule enterprise:

```text
Avoid null elements in collections.
Use empty collection instead of null collection.
```

Baik:

```java
return List.of();
```

Buruk:

```java
return null;
```

---

## 4.12 Concurrent Collections

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Concurrent key-value | `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| Producer-consumer blocking | `ArrayBlockingQueue`, `LinkedBlockingQueue` |
| High-throughput counter | `LongAdder` |
| Concurrent set | `ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet()` |
| Read-heavy list | `CopyOnWriteArrayList` |

Example:

```java
ConcurrentHashMap<String, LongAdder> counters =
    new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

counters.computeIfAbsent("orders", key -> new LongAdder())
        .increment();
```

Concurrent set:

```java
Set<String> activeUsers = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

activeUsers.add("user-1");
```

---

# 5. Processing Model & Stream

Ada beberapa model processing di Java:

```text
Imperative loop
Functional-style Stream
Iterator
Reactive stream
Parallel stream
Virtual-thread based processing
```

Fokus utama di Java SE: loop dan Stream.

---

## 5.1 Imperative Processing

```java
List<String> activeNames = new ArrayList<>();

for (User user : users) {
    if (user.active()) {
        activeNames.add(user.name());
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Mudah debug | Step-by-step jelas |
| Cocok untuk side effect | Mutation, logging, validation |
| Cocok untuk complex branching | Banyak rule |
| Familiar | Mudah dibaca semua engineer |

---

## 5.2 Stream Processing

```java
List<String> activeNames = users.stream()
    .filter(User::active)
    .map(User::name)
    .toList();
```

Stream cocok untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| Filter | Complex side-effect workflow |
| Map/transform | Transactional mutation rumit |
| Grouping | Debug step-by-step berat |
| Aggregation | Error handling bercabang kompleks |
| Projection | I/O call per item tanpa kontrol |

---

## 5.3 Stream Pipeline

Stream pipeline punya tiga bagian:

```text
Source
  ↓
Intermediate operations
  ↓
Terminal operation
```

Contoh:

```java
List<String> result = users.stream()       // source
    .filter(User::active)                  // intermediate
    .map(User::name)                       // intermediate
    .sorted()                              // intermediate
    .toList();                             // terminal
```

Source:

```java
users.stream()
Arrays.stream(array)
Stream.of("A", "B")
Files.lines(path)
```

Intermediate:

```java
filter
map
flatMap
distinct
sorted
limit
skip
peek
```

Terminal:

```java
toList
collect
forEach
reduce
count
anyMatch
allMatch
noneMatch
findFirst
findAny
min
max
```

---

## 5.4 Lazy Evaluation

Intermediate operation lazy.

```java
Stream<String> stream = names.stream()
    .filter(name -> {
        System.out.println("filter " + name);
        return name.startsWith("A");
    });
```

Belum jalan sampai terminal operation dipanggil:

```java
List<String> result = stream.toList();
```

Mental model:

```text
Stream pipeline is a recipe.
Terminal operation executes the recipe.
```

---

## 5.5 Common Stream Operations

### filter

```java
List<Order> submitted = orders.stream()
    .filter(order -> order.status() == OrderStatus.SUBMITTED)
    .toList();
```

### map

```java
List<UUID> orderIds = orders.stream()
    .map(Order::id)
    .toList();
```

### flatMap

```java
List<OrderLine> allLines = orders.stream()
    .flatMap(order -> order.lines().stream())
    .toList();
```

### distinct

```java
List<String> uniqueEmails = emails.stream()
    .distinct()
    .toList();
```

### sorted

```java
List<Order> sorted = orders.stream()
    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Order::createdAt))
    .toList();
```

Descending:

```java
List<Order> sorted = orders.stream()
    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Order::createdAt).reversed())
    .toList();
```

### limit

```java
List<Order> latestTen = orders.stream()
    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Order::createdAt).reversed())
    .limit(10)
    .toList();
```

---

## 5.6 `toList()` vs `Collectors.toList()`

Modern:

```java
List<String> names = users.stream()
    .map(User::name)
    .toList();
```

Collector:

```java
List<String> names = users.stream()
    .map(User::name)
    .collect(Collectors.toList());
```

Practical difference:

| Method | Result |
|---|---|
| `stream.toList()` | Unmodifiable list |
| `collect(Collectors.toList())` | Usually mutable, but not guaranteed by interface |
| `collect(Collectors.toCollection(ArrayList::new))` | Explicit mutable `ArrayList` |

Jika butuh mutable:

```java
List<String> names = users.stream()
    .map(User::name)
    .collect(Collectors.toCollection(ArrayList::new));
```

---

## 5.7 Collectors

### groupingBy

```java
Map<OrderStatus, List<Order>> byStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Order::status));
```

### counting

```java
Map<OrderStatus, Long> countByStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
        Order::status,
        Collectors.counting()
    ));
```

### mapping

```java
Map<OrderStatus, List<UUID>> orderIdsByStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
        Order::status,
        Collectors.mapping(Order::id, Collectors.toList())
    ));
```

### joining

```java
String csv = users.stream()
    .map(User::email)
    .collect(Collectors.joining(","));
```

### partitioningBy

```java
Map<Boolean, List<Order>> partitioned = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.partitioningBy(Order::isOverdue));
```

Result:

```text
true  -> overdue orders
false -> non-overdue orders
```

---

## 5.8 Reduce

Sum BigDecimal:

```java
BigDecimal total = lines.stream()
    .map(OrderLine::amount)
    .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
```

Max:

```java
Optional<Order> latest = orders.stream()
    .max(Comparator.comparing(Order::createdAt));
```

Count:

```java
long count = orders.stream()
    .filter(Order::active)
    .count();
```

Rule:

```text
Use reduce for aggregation into one value.
Use collect for aggregation into collection/map/object container.
```

---

## 5.9 Primitive Streams

Untuk avoid boxing overhead:

```java
int total = numbers.stream()
    .mapToInt(Integer::intValue)
    .sum();
```

Primitive streams:

| Type | Stream |
|---|---|
| `int` | `IntStream` |
| `long` | `LongStream` |
| `double` | `DoubleStream` |

Examples:

```java
IntStream.range(0, 10)
    .forEach(System.out::println);
```

Average:

```java
OptionalDouble average = users.stream()
    .mapToInt(User::age)
    .average();
```

---

## 5.10 Optional from Stream

Find first:

```java
Optional<User> user = users.stream()
    .filter(u -> u.id().equals(userId))
    .findFirst();
```

Use:

```java
User found = users.stream()
    .filter(u -> u.id().equals(userId))
    .findFirst()
    .orElseThrow(() -> new UserNotFoundException(userId));
```

Any match:

```java
boolean hasAdmin = users.stream()
    .anyMatch(user -> user.roles().contains(Role.ADMIN));
```

All match:

```java
boolean allActive = users.stream()
    .allMatch(User::active);
```

None match:

```java
boolean noBlocked = users.stream()
    .noneMatch(User::blocked);
```

---

## 5.11 Stream with Map

Convert list to map:

```java
Map<UUID, User> usersById = users.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        User::id,
        Function.identity()
    ));
```

Jika duplicate key mungkin terjadi:

```java
Map<String, User> usersByEmail = users.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        User::email,
        Function.identity(),
        (existing, replacement) -> existing
    ));
```

Maintain order:

```java
Map<UUID, User> usersById = users.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        User::id,
        Function.identity(),
        (a, b) -> a,
        LinkedHashMap::new
    ));
```

---

## 5.12 Stream Pitfalls

### 1. Side effect di `map`

Buruk:

```java
orders.stream()
    .map(order -> {
        order.submit();
        return order;
    })
    .toList();
```

Lebih jelas:

```java
for (Order order : orders) {
    order.submit();
}
```

### 2. `peek` untuk business logic

Buruk:

```java
orders.stream()
    .peek(order -> order.submit())
    .toList();
```

`peek` sebaiknya hanya untuk debugging ringan.

---

### 3. N+1 call dalam Stream

Buruk:

```java
List<Customer> customers = orders.stream()
    .map(order -> customerClient.getCustomer(order.customerId()))
    .toList();
```

Masalah:

```text
1000 orders = 1000 remote calls
slow
fragile
rate-limit risk
hard to retry properly
```

Lebih baik batch:

```java
Set<UUID> customerIds = orders.stream()
    .map(Order::customerId)
    .collect(Collectors.toSet());

Map<UUID, Customer> customers =
    customerClient.getCustomers(customerIds);
```

---

### 4. Parallel stream sembarangan

Buruk:

```java
orders.parallelStream()
    .map(this::process)
    .toList();
```

Risiko:

```text
uses common ForkJoinPool
uncontrolled concurrency
bad for blocking I/O
can starve other parallel tasks
harder debugging
transaction context issues
```

Gunakan parallel stream hanya jika:

```text
CPU-bound
large enough data
stateless operation
no blocking I/O
no shared mutable state
measured benefit
```

---

## 5.13 Sequential vs Parallel Stream

| Aspect | Sequential Stream | Parallel Stream |
|---|---|---|
| Execution | One thread | Multiple threads |
| Default safety | Easier | Harder |
| Good for | Normal processing | CPU-heavy pure computation |
| Bad for | Very large CPU task | Blocking I/O, DB call, HTTP call |
| Debugging | Easier | Harder |
| Ordering | Predictable | More complex |

Rule:

```text
Default to sequential stream.
Use parallel stream only after measurement.
```

---

## 5.14 Ordered vs Unordered Stream

List stream ordered:

```java
List<String> names = List.of("B", "A", "C");

names.stream()
    .forEach(System.out::println);
```

Set stream may be unordered:

```java
Set<String> names = Set.of("B", "A", "C");

names.stream()
    .forEach(System.out::println);
```

If order matters:

```java
names.stream()
    .sorted()
    .toList();
```

---

## 5.15 Stateful vs Stateless Operations

Stateless:

```java
filter
map
flatMap
```

Stateful:

```java
distinct
sorted
limit
skip
```

Stateful operations may need to remember data.

Example:

```java
orders.stream()
    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Order::createdAt))
    .limit(10)
    .toList();
```

This may require buffering/sorting before producing result.

---

# 6. Practical Enterprise Examples

## 6.1 Request DTO with Record

```java
public record CreateOrderRequest(
    UUID customerId,
    List<CreateOrderLineRequest> lines
) {
    public CreateOrderRequest {
        lines = List.copyOf(lines);
    }
}
```

Nested record:

```java
public record CreateOrderLineRequest(
    String productCode,
    int quantity,
    BigDecimal unitPrice
) {}
```

---

## 6.2 Domain ID as Strong Type

Buruk:

```java
void loadOrder(UUID id) {}
void loadCustomer(UUID id) {}
```

Mudah tertukar.

Lebih aman:

```java
public record OrderId(UUID value) {}
public record CustomerId(UUID value) {}
```

Use:

```java
void loadOrder(OrderId orderId) {}
void loadCustomer(CustomerId customerId) {}
```

---

## 6.3 Status Modeling

```java
public enum CaseStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    UNDER_REVIEW,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED
}
```

Transition:

```java
public boolean canTransitionTo(CaseStatus target) {
    return switch (this.status) {
        case DRAFT ->
            target == CaseStatus.SUBMITTED;

        case SUBMITTED ->
            target == CaseStatus.UNDER_REVIEW;

        case UNDER_REVIEW ->
            target == CaseStatus.APPROVED ||
            target == CaseStatus.REJECTED;

        case APPROVED, REJECTED ->
            false;
    };
}
```

---

## 6.4 Group Orders by Status

```java
Map<OrderStatus, List<Order>> byStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Order::status));
```

Count:

```java
Map<OrderStatus, Long> countByStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
        Order::status,
        Collectors.counting()
    ));
```

---

## 6.5 Convert List to Lookup Map

```java
Map<UUID, Order> ordersById = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        Order::id,
        Function.identity()
    ));
```

Use:

```java
Order order = ordersById.get(orderId);
```

---

## 6.6 Batch Join In Memory

```java
Map<UUID, Customer> customersById = customers.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        Customer::id,
        Function.identity()
    ));

List<OrderView> views = orders.stream()
    .map(order -> new OrderView(
        order.id(),
        customersById.get(order.customerId()).name(),
        order.status(),
        order.totalAmount()
    ))
    .toList();
```

---

# 7. Senior-Level Decision Rules

## 7.1 Data Type Decision

| Situation | Use |
|---|---|
| Required integer count | `int` |
| Large ID/counter | `long` |
| Database UUID | `UUID` |
| Money | `BigDecimal` |
| Timestamp event | `Instant` |
| Business date | `LocalDate` |
| API DTO | `record` |
| Finite status | `enum` |
| Finite result variants | `sealed interface` + `record` |
| Maybe missing return | `Optional<T>` |
| Multiple values | `List<T>` |
| Unique values | `Set<T>` |
| Lookup | `Map<K,V>` |

---

## 7.2 Type System Decision

| Problem | Better Model |
|---|---|
| `String status` | `enum Status` |
| `UUID id` everywhere | `record OrderId(UUID value)` |
| `Map<String,Object>` | typed DTO/record |
| nullable return | `Optional<T>` |
| finite result | sealed result type |
| dynamic casting | polymorphism / pattern matching |
| raw collection | generic collection |
| mutable DTO | record where possible |

---

## 7.3 Collection Decision

| Problem | Collection |
|---|---|
| Preserve order | `List` |
| Prevent duplicate | `Set` |
| Fast lookup by ID | `Map<ID, Entity>` |
| Preserve insertion order | `LinkedHashMap` / `LinkedHashSet` |
| Sort automatically | `TreeMap` / `TreeSet` |
| Enum keys | `EnumMap` |
| Enum values set | `EnumSet` |
| Stack | `ArrayDeque` |
| Queue | `ArrayDeque` |
| Concurrent lookup | `ConcurrentHashMap` |

---

## 7.4 Stream Decision

| Situation | Use Stream? |
|---|---|
| Simple filter/map | Yes |
| Grouping/counting | Yes |
| Convert list to map | Yes |
| Complex mutation | Prefer loop |
| Transactional workflow | Prefer explicit loop/service |
| Remote call per item | Avoid or batch |
| Debug-heavy logic | Prefer loop |
| CPU-heavy pure computation | Maybe parallel stream after measurement |
| Blocking I/O | Avoid parallel stream; consider executor/virtual threads |

---

# 8. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem |
|---|---|
| `String` for status | Invalid values possible |
| `double` for money | Precision bug |
| `null` collection | NPE risk |
| Raw `List` | Runtime ClassCastException |
| `Map<String,Object>` everywhere | No compile-time safety |
| `Optional.get()` blindly | Runtime failure |
| `parallelStream()` for DB/API calls | Uncontrolled concurrency |
| Side effect inside `map()` | Misleading code |
| Mutable object inside `HashSet` | Hash lookup corruption |
| Missing `equals/hashCode` | Set/Map bugs |
| `List` used for lookup repeatedly | O(n) repeated scanning |
| `Set` used when order matters | Unstable behavior |
| `LocalDateTime` for global timestamp | Timezone ambiguity |

---

# 9. Compact Cheat Sheet

```text
Primitive
- int, long, boolean, double, char
- no null
- fast
- use for required simple values

Wrapper
- Integer, Long, Boolean, Double
- nullable
- required for generics/collections/frameworks

String
- immutable
- use equals(), not ==
- use StringBuilder for loop concatenation

BigDecimal
- use for money
- construct from String
- compare with compareTo()

java.time
- Instant for timestamp
- LocalDate for date only
- ZonedDateTime for timezone-aware display

Enum
- finite fixed values
- great for status/type/category

Record
- immutable data carrier
- great for DTO/value object/command/result

Optional
- return type for maybe missing value
- avoid as field/parameter

List
- ordered, duplicates allowed
- default implementation: ArrayList

Set
- unique values
- default: HashSet
- ordered unique: LinkedHashSet
- sorted: TreeSet

Map
- key-value lookup
- default: HashMap
- ordered: LinkedHashMap
- sorted: TreeMap
- concurrent: ConcurrentHashMap

Stream
- source -> intermediate ops -> terminal op
- lazy until terminal operation
- good for filter/map/group/reduce
- avoid side effects and uncontrolled I/O

Generics
- List<String> gives compile-time safety
- generics are invariant
- PECS: Producer Extends, Consumer Super
- avoid raw types

Sealed Type
- finite subtype hierarchy
- excellent for result/state/event modeling
```

---

# 10. Most Important Onboarding Heuristics

Saat membaca codebase Java enterprise, tanyakan:

```text
1. Apakah data type-nya merepresentasikan domain dengan benar?
2. Apakah status masih String padahal harus enum?
3. Apakah ID raw UUID/String terlalu mudah tertukar?
4. Apakah money masih double?
5. Apakah timestamp punya timezone semantics jelas?
6. Apakah collection dipilih sesuai access pattern?
7. Apakah Map digunakan untuk lookup, bukan List scanning berulang?
8. Apakah equals/hashCode aman untuk Set/Map?
9. Apakah Stream dipakai untuk transformasi, bukan workflow mutation rumit?
10. Apakah parallelStream dipakai sembarangan?
11. Apakah Optional dipakai sebagai return, bukan field/parameter?
12. Apakah null diganti empty collection bila memungkinkan?
13. Apakah DTO bisa disederhanakan dengan record?
14. Apakah finite result/state bisa dimodelkan dengan sealed type?
```

Kesimpulan paling praktis: **Java yang kuat bukan hanya soal syntax, tapi soal memilih representasi data yang membuat invalid state sulit terjadi.** Data type yang benar, type system yang dimanfaatkan, collection yang sesuai access pattern, dan Stream yang dipakai hanya untuk processing yang cocok akan membuat codebase jauh lebih aman, mudah dibaca, dan lebih tahan terhadap bug enterprise.

## Response:
7/12/2026, 8:20:15 PM

Berikut **cheat sheet Java 17+ untuk Bit, Number, Text, dan Temporal**. Fokusnya untuk membaca dan menulis codebase enterprise dengan benar: data correctness, encoding, precision, timezone, dan representasi low-level.

# Java 17+ Cheat Sheet: Bit, Number, Text, Temporal

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text
Data Representation
├── Bit       -> representasi paling rendah: flag, mask, binary protocol, permission
├── Number    -> integer, decimal, money, counter, precision, overflow
├── Text      -> String, Unicode, charset, encoding, parsing, regex
└── Temporal  -> date, time, timezone, duration, timestamp, business calendar
```

Rule utama:

| Area | Risiko Terbesar |
|---|---|
| Bit | Salah shift, salah signed/unsigned, endian mismatch |
| Number | Overflow, precision loss, rounding salah |
| Text | Encoding rusak, Unicode salah, regex mahal |
| Temporal | Timezone ambiguity, DST bug, salah simpan timestamp |

---

# 2. Bit

## 2.1 Binary Literal

```java
int mask = 0b0000_0101;
long flags = 0b1000_0000L;
```

Underscore boleh untuk readability:

```java
int permissions = 0b0000_0001
                | 0b0000_0010
                | 0b0000_0100;
```

---

## 2.2 Integer Representation

Java integer primitive:

| Type | Size | Signed? | Range |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `byte` | 8-bit | signed | -128 to 127 |
| `short` | 16-bit | signed | -32,768 to 32,767 |
| `int` | 32-bit | signed | about -2.1B to 2.1B |
| `long` | 64-bit | signed | very large |
| `char` | 16-bit | unsigned-ish UTF-16 code unit | 0 to 65,535 |

Important:

```java
byte b = 127;
b++; // becomes -128 due to overflow
```

Java integer uses **two’s complement** representation.

---

## 2.3 Bitwise Operators

| Operator | Name | Example |
|---|---|---|
| `&` | AND | `a & b` |
| `|` | OR | `a | b` |
| `^` | XOR | `a ^ b` |
| `~` | NOT / complement | `~a` |
| `<<` | left shift | `a << 2` |
| `>>` | signed right shift | `a >> 2` |
| `>>>` | unsigned right shift | `a >>> 2` |

Example:

```java
int a = 0b1100;
int b = 0b1010;

int and = a & b; // 1000
int or  = a | b; // 1110
int xor = a ^ b; // 0110
```

---

## 2.4 Bit Flags

Cocok untuk permission, feature flag internal, compact state.

```java
public final class Permissions {
    public static final int READ   = 1 << 0; // 0001
    public static final int WRITE  = 1 << 1; // 0010
    public static final int DELETE = 1 << 2; // 0100
    public static final int ADMIN  = 1 << 3; // 1000
}
```

Set flag:

```java
int permission = 0;

permission |= Permissions.READ;
permission |= Permissions.WRITE;
```

Check flag:

```java
boolean canRead = (permission & Permissions.READ) != 0;
```

Remove flag:

```java
permission &= ~Permissions.WRITE;
```

Toggle flag:

```java
permission ^= Permissions.ADMIN;
```

---

## 2.5 Bit Mask Helper

```java
public final class BitFlags {
    private BitFlags() {}

    public static boolean has(int flags, int mask) {
        return (flags & mask) == mask;
    }

    public static int add(int flags, int mask) {
        return flags | mask;
    }

    public static int remove(int flags, int mask) {
        return flags & ~mask;
    }

    public static int toggle(int flags, int mask) {
        return flags ^ mask;
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
int flags = 0;

flags = BitFlags.add(flags, Permissions.READ);
flags = BitFlags.add(flags, Permissions.WRITE);

if (BitFlags.has(flags, Permissions.READ)) {
    // allowed
}
```

---

## 2.6 Shift Operators

```java
int x = 8;       // 00001000

x << 1;          // 16
x >> 1;          // 4
```

Signed right shift keeps sign:

```java
int negative = -8;

int signed = negative >> 1;
```

Unsigned right shift fills with zero:

```java
int unsigned = negative >>> 1;
```
