Use case:

| Operator | Use Case |
|---|---|
| `<<` | multiply by power of 2, bit positioning |
| `>>` | signed divide-ish by power of 2 |
| `>>>` | binary protocol, hash calculation, unsigned interpretation |

Practical warning:

```text
Do not replace normal arithmetic with shifts for readability unless you are doing actual bit-level work.
```

---

## 2.7 Byte and Unsigned Conversion

Java `byte` is signed.

```java
byte b = (byte) 255;
System.out.println(b); // -1
```

Interpret as unsigned:

```java
int unsigned = Byte.toUnsignedInt(b); // 255
```

For short:

```java
int value = Short.toUnsignedInt(s);
```

For int:

```java
long value = Integer.toUnsignedLong(i);
```

Useful for:

```text
binary protocol
network packet
file format
checksum
hashing
cryptography boundary
```

---

## 2.8 BitSet

`BitSet` cocok untuk banyak boolean flag.

```java
BitSet bits = new BitSet();

bits.set(0);
bits.set(5);

boolean enabled = bits.get(5);

bits.clear(0);
```

Operations:

```java
BitSet a = new BitSet();
BitSet b = new BitSet();

a.set(1);
a.set(2);

b.set(2);
b.set(3);

a.and(b); // intersection
a.or(b);  // union
a.xor(b); // difference-ish
```

Gunakan `BitSet` saat:

| Use Case | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Banyak boolean | Lebih compact daripada `boolean[]` |
| Set operasi bit | AND/OR/XOR cepat |
| Sparse flags | Bisa efisien |
| Permission matrix internal | Compact |

Hindari untuk API publik jika readability lebih penting daripada compactness.

---

## 2.9 ByteBuffer

Untuk binary data:

```java
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(16);

buffer.putInt(123);
buffer.putLong(999L);

buffer.flip();

int a = buffer.getInt();
long b = buffer.getLong();
```

Endianness:

```java
buffer.order(ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN);
buffer.order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
```

Default Java `ByteBuffer` adalah **big-endian**.

Use case:

```text
binary protocol
file format
network payload
serialization
off-heap/direct buffer
```

Direct buffer:

```java
ByteBuffer direct = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(1024);
```

Direct buffer cocok untuk I/O-heavy case, tapi lifecycle dan memory-nya lebih perlu perhatian.

---

## 2.10 Hex and Binary Display

Binary:

```java
String binary = Integer.toBinaryString(42);
```

Hex:

```java
String hex = Integer.toHexString(255); // ff
```

Format padded:

```java
String padded = String.format("%8s", Integer.toBinaryString(5))
    .replace(' ', '0');

// 00000101
```

Java 17 punya `HexFormat`:

```java
HexFormat hex = HexFormat.of();

byte[] bytes = {1, 2, 15, 16};

String text = hex.formatHex(bytes); // 01020f10
byte[] parsed = hex.parseHex(text);
```

---

## 2.11 Bit Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah |
|---|---|
| Magic bit number | Sulit dibaca |
| `byte` diperlakukan unsigned | Bug nilai negatif |
| Lupa endian | Binary data rusak |
| Flag integer untuk domain publik | Tidak self-documenting |
| Shift untuk optimisasi prematur | Menurunkan readability |
| Tidak pakai constant | Salah posisi bit |
| Tidak test boundary | Bug hanya muncul di edge case |

---

# 3. Number

## 3.1 Numeric Type Overview

| Type | Category | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| `byte` | integer | low-level binary |
| `short` | integer | jarang |
| `int` | integer | default integer |
| `long` | integer | large counter, ID-ish numeric |
| `float` | floating | jarang di enterprise |
| `double` | floating | measurement, ratio, approximation |
| `BigInteger` | arbitrary integer | very large exact integer |
| `BigDecimal` | arbitrary decimal | money, billing, tax |

Default practical choice:

```text
int         -> normal count
long        -> large count / epoch millis / generated numeric ID
BigDecimal  -> money / decimal exactness
double      -> approximate scientific/statistical value
```

---

## 3.2 Integer Overflow

Java primitive integer overflow tidak throw exception.

```java
int max = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
int overflow = max + 1;

System.out.println(overflow); // Integer.MIN_VALUE
```

Safe arithmetic:

```java
int result = Math.addExact(a, b);
```

Methods:

```java
Math.addExact(a, b);
Math.subtractExact(a, b);
Math.multiplyExact(a, b);
Math.incrementExact(a);
Math.decrementExact(a);
Math.negateExact(a);
```

Example:

```java
try {
    int result = Math.addExact(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 1);
} catch (ArithmeticException e) {
    // overflow detected
}
```

Use for:

```text
financial counters
quota calculation
pagination offset
billing quantity
capacity planning
```

---

## 3.3 Floating Point

```java
double value = 0.1 + 0.2;

System.out.println(value); // 0.30000000000000004
```

Floating point is approximate.

Gunakan `double` untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| ratio | money |
| percentage display | tax exact calculation |
| scientific measurement | billing |
| probability | currency |
| telemetry metrics | legal/compliance amount |

---

## 3.4 BigDecimal

Correct:

```java
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal("100.50");
```

Also OK:

```java
BigDecimal amount = BigDecimal.valueOf(100.50);
```

Avoid:

```java
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal(100.50);
```

Add:

```java
BigDecimal total = price.add(tax);
```

Subtract:

```java
BigDecimal remaining = balance.subtract(payment);
```

Multiply:

```java
BigDecimal subtotal = unitPrice.multiply(quantity);
```

Divide with rounding:

```java
BigDecimal result = amount.divide(
    divisor,
    2,
    RoundingMode.HALF_UP
);
```

Compare:

```java
if (amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0) {
    // positive
}
```

Avoid numeric equality using `equals()`:

```java
new BigDecimal("1.0").equals(new BigDecimal("1.00"));      // false
new BigDecimal("1.0").compareTo(new BigDecimal("1.00"));   // 0
```

---

## 3.5 BigDecimal Scale and Precision

```java
BigDecimal value = new BigDecimal("123.4500");

int scale = value.scale();         // 4
int precision = value.precision(); // total significant digits
```

Set scale:

```java
BigDecimal rounded = value.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
```

Normalize:

```java
BigDecimal normalized = value.stripTrailingZeros();
```

Money example:

```java
public record Money(BigDecimal amount, String currency) {
    public Money {
        if (amount == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("amount is required");
        }

        amount = amount.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);

        if (currency == null || currency.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency is required");
        }
    }
}
```

For serious money systems, rounding mode and scale must be defined by business/legal rules, not developer preference.

---

## 3.6 BigInteger

```java
BigInteger a = new BigInteger("999999999999999999999999");
BigInteger b = new BigInteger("2");

BigInteger result = a.multiply(b);
```

Use for:

```text
cryptography
very large exact integer
arbitrary precision numeric identifiers
mathematical computation
```

Common methods:

```java
a.add(b);
a.subtract(b);
a.multiply(b);
a.divide(b);
a.mod(b);
a.pow(10);
a.gcd(b);
```

---

## 3.7 Numeric Promotion

Java promotes smaller numeric types in arithmetic.

```java
byte a = 1;
byte b = 2;

// byte c = a + b; // compile error
int c = a + b;
```

Why? `byte + byte` becomes `int`.

Char arithmetic:

```java
char c = 'A';
int next = c + 1; // 66
```

Mixed types:

```java
int a = 10;
long b = 20L;

long result = a + b;
```

Promotion rough order:

```text
byte/short/char -> int -> long -> float -> double
```

---

## 3.8 Division

Integer division truncates:

```java
int result = 5 / 2; // 2
```

Floating division:

```java
double result = 5.0 / 2; // 2.5
```

BigDecimal division must handle non-terminating decimals:

```java
BigDecimal result = BigDecimal.ONE.divide(
    new BigDecimal("3"),
    4,
    RoundingMode.HALF_UP
);
```

Without rounding:

```java
BigDecimal.ONE.divide(new BigDecimal("3")); // ArithmeticException
```

---

## 3.9 Parsing Numbers

```java
int count = Integer.parseInt("123");
long id = Long.parseLong("999999");
double ratio = Double.parseDouble("0.75");
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal("123.45");
```

Safe parsing helper:

```java
public static OptionalInt parseIntSafe(String value) {
    try {
        return OptionalInt.of(Integer.parseInt(value));
    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
        return OptionalInt.empty();
    }
}
```

Unsigned parsing:

```java
int unsigned = Integer.parseUnsignedInt("4294967295");
```

---

## 3.10 Number Formatting

```java
NumberFormat formatter = NumberFormat.getNumberInstance(Locale.US);

String text = formatter.format(1234567.89);
```

Currency:

```java
NumberFormat currency = NumberFormat.getCurrencyInstance(Locale.US);

String text = currency.format(new BigDecimal("1234.50"));
```

DecimalFormat:

```java
DecimalFormat format = new DecimalFormat("#,##0.00");

String text = format.format(1234.5); // 1,234.50
```

Important: `NumberFormat` / `DecimalFormat` are generally not something you should casually share as mutable singleton across threads.

---

## 3.11 Atomic Numbers

```java
AtomicInteger counter = new AtomicInteger();

counter.incrementAndGet();
counter.addAndGet(5);
```

For high contention counter:

```java
LongAdder adder = new LongAdder();

adder.increment();
adder.add(10);

long total = adder.sum();
```

Use:

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| CAS-style numeric state | `AtomicInteger`, `AtomicLong` |
| High-throughput counter | `LongAdder` |
| Accumulate long values | `LongAccumulator` |
| Monetary value | Not atomic primitive; use lock/transaction/domain consistency |

---

## 3.12 Number Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah |
|---|---|
| `double` untuk money | Precision bug |
| Tidak handle overflow | Silent wrong number |
| `BigDecimal` dari `double` | Precision sudah rusak |
| `equals()` untuk numeric BigDecimal equality | Scale-sensitive |
| Division tanpa rounding | Runtime exception |
| Hardcoded rounding | Bisa salah secara bisnis/legal |
| Integer division tidak sengaja | Data ratio salah |
| Parse tanpa validation | Runtime failure |
| Mutable formatter singleton | Thread-safety risk |

---

# 4. Text

## 4.1 String Basics

`String` immutable.

```java
String name = "Fajar";

String upper = name.toUpperCase();
```

Original tidak berubah:

```java
String s = "hello";
s.toUpperCase();

System.out.println(s); // hello
```

Correct:

```java
s = s.toUpperCase();
```

---

## 4.2 String Equality

Use `equals()`:

```java
if ("ACTIVE".equals(status)) {
    // null-safe
}
```

Avoid:

```java
if (status == "ACTIVE") {
    // compares reference, not content
}
```

Case-insensitive:

```java
if ("ACTIVE".equalsIgnoreCase(status)) {
    // ok for simple cases
}
```

For locale-sensitive casing, be explicit:

```java
String normalized = text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
```

---

## 4.3 Blank vs Empty

```java
String empty = "";
String blank = "   ";
```

Check:

```java
empty.isEmpty(); // true
blank.isBlank(); // true
```

Common validation:

```java
if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
    throw new IllegalArgumentException("value is required");
}
```

---

## 4.4 StringBuilder

Use for repeated append.

```java
StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();

for (String item : items) {
    builder.append(item).append(",");
}

String result = builder.toString();
```

`StringBuffer` is synchronized legacy. Prefer `StringBuilder` unless you specifically need synchronized mutation.

---

## 4.5 Text Blocks

```java
String sql = """
    SELECT id, status, created_at
    FROM orders
    WHERE status = ?
    ORDER BY created_at DESC
    """;
```

Useful for:

```text
SQL
JSON fixture
XML fixture
HTML snippets
multi-line logs
test data
```

Be aware of trailing newline.

```java
String value = """
    hello
    """;
```

This includes a newline after `hello`.

---

## 4.6 Formatter

```java
String message = String.format(
    "Order %s has status %s",
    orderId,
    status
);
```

For simple string interpolation, Java does not have native template strings in Java 17. Use:

```java
"Order " + orderId + " has status " + status
```

or:

```java
String.format(...)
```

or logging placeholders:

```java
log.info("Order {} has status {}", orderId, status);
```

---

## 4.7 Charset and Encoding

Text in memory is `String`; bytes are encoded representation.

```text
String <-> byte[]
       Charset
```

Encode:

```java
byte[] bytes = text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

Decode:

```java
String text = new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

Always specify charset.

Avoid:

```java
byte[] bytes = text.getBytes(); // platform default
```

Prefer:

```java
byte[] bytes = text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

Common charsets:

| Charset | Use |
|---|---|
| `UTF_8` | default modern choice |
| `UTF_16` | JVM/internal-ish, some protocols |
| `ISO_8859_1` | legacy western systems |
| `US_ASCII` | strict ASCII protocol |

---

## 4.8 Unicode Model

Important distinction:

```text
char        -> 16-bit UTF-16 code unit
code point  -> actual Unicode scalar value
grapheme    -> what user perceives as one character
```

Problem:

```java
String emoji = "😊";

System.out.println(emoji.length()); // 2
```

Because emoji may use two UTF-16 code units.

Count code points:

```java
int count = emoji.codePointCount(0, emoji.length());
```

Iterate code points:

```java
text.codePoints()
    .forEach(cp -> System.out.println(Character.toChars(cp)));
```

Senior rule:

```text
Do not assume String.length() equals user-visible character count.
```

---

## 4.9 Normalization

Same-looking text can have different binary representation.

```java
String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(
    input,
    Normalizer.Form.NFC
);
```

Use for:

```text
search
deduplication
user input comparison
accented characters
external data integration
```

---

## 4.10 Locale-Sensitive Text

Bad:

```java
String lower = text.toLowerCase();
```

Better for system identifiers:

```java
String lower = text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
```

For user-facing locale:

```java
String lower = text.toLowerCase(Locale.forLanguageTag("tr-TR"));
```

Why it matters: some languages have special casing rules.

Rule:

| Use Case | Locale |
|---|---|
| System key, enum parsing, protocol | `Locale.ROOT` |
| User-facing display | User locale |
| Search normalization | Explicit chosen locale |

---

## 4.11 Collator

For locale-aware sorting:

```java
Collator collator = Collator.getInstance(Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID"));

List<String> sorted = names.stream()
    .sorted(collator)
    .toList();
```

Do not rely on raw Unicode order for user-facing alphabetical sorting.

---

## 4.12 Regex

```java
Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile("^[A-Z]{2}\\d{4}$");

Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher("AB1234");

boolean valid = matcher.matches();
```

For repeated use, compile once:

```java
private static final Pattern ORDER_CODE =
    Pattern.compile("^[A-Z]{2}\\d{4}$");
```

Extract:

```java
Pattern p = Pattern.compile("order-(\\d+)");
Matcher m = p.matcher("order-123");

if (m.matches()) {
    String id = m.group(1);
}
```

Split:

```java
String[] parts = text.split(",");
```

Beware: `split()` uses regex.

```java
"a.b".split(".");  // surprising, "." means any char
"a.b".split("\\."); // correct
```

---

## 4.13 Base64

Encode:

```java
String encoded = Base64.getEncoder()
    .encodeToString(bytes);
```

Decode:

```java
byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder()
    .decode(encoded);
```

URL-safe:

```java
String token = Base64.getUrlEncoder()
    .withoutPadding()
    .encodeToString(bytes);
```

Use for binary-to-text representation, not encryption.

---

## 4.14 Text Security

Avoid logging sensitive text:

```text
password
access token
refresh token
secret key
PII
full payload with personal data
authorization header
cookie
```

Masking example:

```java
public static String maskEmail(String email) {
    int at = email.indexOf('@');

    if (at <= 1) {
        return "***";
    }

    return email.charAt(0) + "***" + email.substring(at);
}
```

---

## 4.15 Text Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah |
|---|---|
| `==` untuk String | Reference comparison |
| Tidak specify charset | Platform-dependent bug |
| `String.length()` untuk jumlah karakter manusia | Unicode bug |
| Regex compile per request | Performance overhead |
| `split(".")` | Regex mistake |
| Lowercase tanpa Locale | Locale bug |
| Log full text payload | Security/privacy issue |
| Base64 dianggap encryption | Security misconception |
| String concatenation dalam loop besar | Allocation overhead |

---

# 5. Temporal

## 5.1 Java Time Type Map

| Type | Meaning | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| `Instant` | titik waktu global UTC | event timestamp, audit |
| `LocalDate` | tanggal tanpa waktu | birth date, due date |
| `LocalTime` | waktu tanpa tanggal | office hour |
| `LocalDateTime` | tanggal + waktu tanpa zone | local business schedule |
| `OffsetDateTime` | date-time + offset | API timestamp |
| `ZonedDateTime` | date-time + timezone rules | user display, calendar |
| `Duration` | machine time amount | timeout, SLA seconds |
| `Period` | human date amount | 1 month, 1 year |
| `YearMonth` | year + month | billing month |
| `MonthDay` | month + day | recurring birthday |
| `ZoneId` | timezone region | `Asia/Jakarta` |
| `ZoneOffset` | fixed offset | `+07:00` |

---

## 5.2 Instant

`Instant` adalah timestamp global.

```java
Instant now = Instant.now();
```

Use for:

```text
created_at
updated_at
audit timestamp
event occurrence
message timestamp
distributed system logs
```

Convert to user timezone:

```java
ZonedDateTime jakarta = now.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

---

## 5.3 LocalDate

```java
LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();

LocalDate dueDate = LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 12);
```

Use for:

```text
business date
birth date
expiry date without time
reporting date
calendar day
```

Add:

```java
LocalDate nextWeek = today.plusWeeks(1);
```

Compare:

```java
boolean overdue = dueDate.isBefore(LocalDate.now());
```

---

## 5.4 LocalDateTime

```java
LocalDateTime local = LocalDateTime.of(
    2026, 7, 12,
    9, 30
);
```

Important:

```text
LocalDateTime has no timezone.
It is not a global timestamp.
```

Danger:

```java
LocalDateTime createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
```

This is ambiguous in distributed systems.

Prefer:

```java
Instant createdAt = Instant.now();
```

Use `LocalDateTime` only when the business concept is intentionally local and timezone-independent or timezone is applied separately.

---

## 5.5 ZonedDateTime

```java
ZoneId zone = ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta");

ZonedDateTime nowJakarta = ZonedDateTime.now(zone);
```

Use for:

```text
calendar event
user-facing display
timezone-aware schedule
DST-sensitive calculation
```

Convert:

```java
Instant instant = Instant.now();

ZonedDateTime userTime = instant.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

---

## 5.6 OffsetDateTime

```java
OffsetDateTime now = OffsetDateTime.now();
```

Example:

```text
2026-07-12T19:30:00+07:00
```

OffsetDateTime has offset, but not full timezone rule identity.

| Type | Has Offset? | Has Zone Rules? |
|---|---|---|
| `OffsetDateTime` | Yes | No |
| `ZonedDateTime` | Yes | Yes |
| `Instant` | UTC point | No local rules |

---

## 5.7 Duration vs Period

`Duration` is machine time.

```java
Duration timeout = Duration.ofSeconds(30);
Duration sla = Duration.ofHours(4);
```

`Period` is human calendar date amount.

```java
Period oneMonth = Period.ofMonths(1);
Period oneYear = Period.ofYears(1);
```

Difference:

```java
Instant later = instant.plus(Duration.ofDays(1));
LocalDate nextMonth = date.plus(Period.ofMonths(1));
```

Use:

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Timeout | `Duration` |
| Cache TTL | `Duration` |
| SLA in hours/minutes | `Duration` |
| Age | `Period` |
| Billing cycle month | `Period` / `YearMonth` |
| Calendar date addition | `Period` |

---

## 5.8 Clock for Testability

Bad:

```java
Instant now = Instant.now();
```

Better:

```java
public class OrderService {
    private final Clock clock;

    public OrderService(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public void submit(Order order) {
        order.submittedAt(clock.instant());
    }
}
```

Production:

```java
Clock clock = Clock.systemUTC();
```

Test:

```java
Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
    Instant.parse("2026-07-12T00:00:00Z"),
    ZoneOffset.UTC
);
```

This makes temporal logic testable.

---

## 5.9 Formatting Temporal

Formatter:

```java
DateTimeFormatter formatter =
    DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
        .withLocale(Locale.US);

String text = LocalDateTime.now().format(formatter);
```

ISO format:

```java
String text = Instant.now().toString();
```

Parse:

```java
Instant instant = Instant.parse("2026-07-12T12:00:00Z");
LocalDate date = LocalDate.parse("2026-07-12");
```

Custom parse:

```java
DateTimeFormatter formatter =
    DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy");

LocalDate date = LocalDate.parse("12/07/2026", formatter);
```

---

## 5.10 Timezone

Use region zone:

```java
ZoneId jakarta = ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta");
ZoneId singapore = ZoneId.of("Asia/Singapore");
```

Avoid hardcoding offset as timezone unless truly fixed:

```java
ZoneOffset offset = ZoneOffset.of("+07:00");
```

Why?

```text
ZoneId has historical/future rules.
ZoneOffset is just fixed offset.
```

Good:

```java
ZonedDateTime userTime = instant.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

---

## 5.11 DST Issues

Some time does not exist in DST transition.

Example conceptually:

```text
02:30 may not exist on DST spring-forward day.
01:30 may occur twice on DST fall-back day.
```

Even if Indonesia does not currently use DST, enterprise systems with global users must not assume every day has exactly 24 hours.

Risky assumption:

```java
Duration.ofDays(1)
```

versus calendar day:

```java
Period.ofDays(1)
```

Different semantics.

---

## 5.12 Temporal Adjusters

```java
LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();

LocalDate firstDay = today.with(TemporalAdjusters.firstDayOfMonth());
LocalDate lastDay = today.with(TemporalAdjusters.lastDayOfMonth());
LocalDate nextMonday = today.with(TemporalAdjusters.next(DayOfWeek.MONDAY));
```

Useful for:

```text
billing cycle
reporting period
month-end processing
business schedule
calendar rule
```

---

## 5.13 YearMonth

```java
YearMonth billingMonth = YearMonth.of(2026, 7);

LocalDate firstDay = billingMonth.atDay(1);
LocalDate lastDay = billingMonth.atEndOfMonth();
```

Use for:

```text
invoice month
salary month
subscription period
reporting month
```

Better than manually handling `"2026-07"` as String.

---

## 5.14 Date Range Modeling

Prefer explicit range object.

```java
public record DateRange(LocalDate startInclusive, LocalDate endExclusive) {
    public DateRange {
        if (!startInclusive.isBefore(endExclusive)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid date range");
        }
    }

    public boolean contains(LocalDate date) {
        return !date.isBefore(startInclusive)
            && date.isBefore(endExclusive);
    }
}
```

Why end-exclusive?

```text
[start, end) is easier to compose.
No overlap ambiguity.
Works well for date/time queries.
```

Example SQL mindset:

```sql
WHERE created_at >= :start
  AND created_at <  :end
```

Avoid:

```sql
WHERE created_at BETWEEN :start AND :end
```

For timestamps, inclusive end often causes boundary bugs.

---

## 5.15 Temporal and Database Mapping

Common mapping:

| Java | PostgreSQL Concept |
|---|---|
| `Instant` | `timestamptz` |
| `OffsetDateTime` | `timestamptz` often |
| `LocalDate` | `date` |
| `LocalTime` | `time` |
| `LocalDateTime` | `timestamp without time zone` |
| `Duration` | `interval` or numeric seconds |
| `YearMonth` | often `yyyy-mm` string or date first day |

Practical rule:

```text
For event timestamp, prefer Instant in Java and timestamptz in DB.
For business date, use LocalDate and date.
For date range queries, use start inclusive and end exclusive.
```

---

## 5.16 Legacy Date/Calendar Interop

Old:

```java
Date date = new Date();
Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance();
```

Convert `Date` to `Instant`:

```java
Instant instant = date.toInstant();
```

Convert `Instant` to `Date`:

```java
Date date = Date.from(instant);
```

Avoid new business logic with `Date` / `Calendar`. Use `java.time`.

---

## 5.17 Temporal Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah |
|---|---|
| `LocalDateTime.now()` for audit timestamp | Timezone ambiguous |
| Store local time as global event time | Distributed bug |
| Hardcode offset as timezone | DST/history bug |
| Use `Date`/`Calendar` for new logic | Legacy complexity |
| Inclusive end timestamp | Boundary bug |
| Assume day = 24 hours | DST bug |
| No `Clock` abstraction | Hard to test |
| Format date for storage | Loses type semantics |
| Parse date without locale/format clarity | Inconsistent behavior |
| Compare date as string | Fragile |

---

# 6. Enterprise Modeling Examples

## 6.1 Permission with Bit Flags

```java
public final class AccessMask {
    public static final int VIEW_CASE   = 1 << 0;
    public static final int EDIT_CASE   = 1 << 1;
    public static final int APPROVE     = 1 << 2;
    public static final int REJECT      = 1 << 3;
    public static final int ADMIN       = 1 << 4;

    private AccessMask() {}
}
```

```java
public record PermissionSet(int mask) {
    public boolean canViewCase() {
        return (mask & AccessMask.VIEW_CASE) != 0;
    }

    public PermissionSet grant(int permission) {
        return new PermissionSet(mask | permission);
    }

    public PermissionSet revoke(int permission) {
        return new PermissionSet(mask & ~permission);
    }
}
```

---

## 6.2 Money

```java
public record Money(BigDecimal amount, Currency currency) {
    public Money {
        Objects.requireNonNull(amount, "amount is required");
        Objects.requireNonNull(currency, "currency is required");

        amount = amount.setScale(
            currency.getDefaultFractionDigits(),
            RoundingMode.HALF_UP
        );
    }

    public Money add(Money other) {
        requireSameCurrency(other);

        return new Money(amount.add(other.amount), currency);
    }

    private void requireSameCurrency(Money other) {
        if (!currency.equals(other.currency)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency mismatch");
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 6.3 Text Normalization for Search Key

```java
public static String normalizeSearchKey(String input) {
    if (input == null || input.isBlank()) {
        return "";
    }

    String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(input, Normalizer.Form.NFC);

    return normalized
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
}
```

---

## 6.4 Auditable Timestamp

```java
public record AuditStamp(
    Instant createdAt,
    Instant updatedAt
) {
    public static AuditStamp now(Clock clock) {
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        return new AuditStamp(now, now);
    }

    public AuditStamp touch(Clock clock) {
        return new AuditStamp(createdAt, clock.instant());
    }
}
```

---

## 6.5 SLA Deadline

```java
public record SlaPolicy(Duration responseTime) {
    public Instant deadlineFrom(Instant start) {
        return start.plus(responseTime);
    }
}
```

Use `Duration` because SLA is machine-time duration.

---

## 6.6 Billing Month

```java
public record BillingPeriod(YearMonth month) {
    public LocalDate startInclusive() {
        return month.atDay(1);
    }

    public LocalDate endExclusive() {
        return month.plusMonths(1).atDay(1);
    }
}
```

---

# 7. Quick Decision Matrix

## Bit

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Compact flags | `int` / `long` bit mask |
| Many booleans | `BitSet` |
| Binary payload | `byte[]` |
| Binary protocol | `ByteBuffer` |
| Hex encoding | `HexFormat` |
| Unsigned byte value | `Byte.toUnsignedInt()` |

## Number

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Count | `int` |
| Large count | `long` |
| Money | `BigDecimal` |
| Very large integer | `BigInteger` |
| Approx measurement | `double` |
| Overflow detection | `Math.addExact()` etc |
| High contention counter | `LongAdder` |
| Nullable numeric DTO field | Wrapper type |

## Text

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Normal text | `String` |
| Repeated append | `StringBuilder` |
| Binary/text conversion | `Charset`, usually UTF-8 |
| Regex reuse | static final `Pattern` |
| Locale casing for system keys | `Locale.ROOT` |
| Locale sorting | `Collator` |
| Unicode normalization | `Normalizer` |
| Binary as text | `Base64` / `HexFormat` |

## Temporal

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Event timestamp | `Instant` |
| Date only | `LocalDate` |
| User calendar time | `ZonedDateTime` |
| API timestamp with offset | `OffsetDateTime` |
| Timeout/cache TTL | `Duration` |
| Month billing | `YearMonth` |
| Date amount | `Period` |
| Testable current time | `Clock` |
| Date range | start inclusive, end exclusive |

---

# 8. Senior-Level Invariants

Saat review code Java, cek ini:

```text
Bit
[ ] Apakah signed/unsigned sudah jelas?
[ ] Apakah endian sudah eksplisit?
[ ] Apakah flag punya constant bernama?
[ ] Apakah binary conversion punya test boundary?

Number
[ ] Apakah money tidak pakai double?
[ ] Apakah overflow mungkin terjadi?
[ ] Apakah rounding rule eksplisit?
[ ] Apakah BigDecimal dibuat dari String/valueOf?
[ ] Apakah integer division disengaja?

Text
[ ] Apakah charset eksplisit?
[ ] Apakah String dibandingkan dengan equals?
[ ] Apakah Unicode length assumption aman?
[ ] Apakah regex dicompile ulang terus?
[ ] Apakah locale eksplisit untuk casing/sorting?
[ ] Apakah sensitive text tidak masuk log?

Temporal
[ ] Apakah event timestamp pakai Instant?
[ ] Apakah business date pakai LocalDate?
[ ] Apakah timezone semantics jelas?
[ ] Apakah range pakai end-exclusive?
[ ] Apakah current time injectable via Clock?
[ ] Apakah DST/global timezone dipertimbangkan?
```

---

# 9. Ringkasan Praktis

```text
Bit
- Pakai bit mask untuk compact internal flags.
- Selalu beri nama constant.
- Hati-hati signed byte dan endian.
- Gunakan ByteBuffer untuk binary protocol.
- Gunakan HexFormat/Base64 untuk representasi text.

Number
- int untuk default integer.
- long untuk counter besar.
- BigDecimal untuk money.
- BigInteger untuk integer sangat besar.
- double hanya untuk approximate value.
- Gunakan Math.*Exact untuk overflow-sensitive arithmetic.

Text
- String immutable.
- Pakai equals(), bukan ==.
- Selalu specify charset, biasanya UTF-8.
- String.length() bukan jumlah karakter manusia.
- Gunakan Locale.ROOT untuk system normalization.
- Compile regex sebagai Pattern jika sering dipakai.

Temporal
- Instant untuk event timestamp.
- LocalDate untuk business date.
- ZonedDateTime untuk user-facing calendar time.
- Duration untuk timeout/SLA.
- Period untuk kalender manusia.
- Clock untuk testing.
- Range sebaiknya start-inclusive, end-exclusive.
```

Kesimpulan paling penting: **Bit menjaga representasi low-level, Number menjaga presisi, Text menjaga encoding dan makna manusia, Temporal menjaga waktu dalam konteks global dan bisnis.** Banyak bug enterprise yang sulit dilacak berasal dari empat area ini, bukan dari algoritma yang rumit.

## Response:
7/12/2026, 8:24:01 PM

Saya akan buat versi yang langsung berguna untuk membaca dan menulis backend enterprise: mulai dari mental model thread/JVM, safety, executor, parallelism, virtual thread, sampai context propagation.

Intinya: cheat sheet ini membahas **bagaimana Java menjalankan banyak pekerjaan sekaligus**, bedanya **concurrency vs parallelism**, bagaimana **thread dan executor bekerja**, serta bagaimana **context seperti request id, security context, transaction context, dan logging context berpindah antar-thread**.

# Java 17+ Cheat Sheet: Concurrency, Parallelism, Thread, Context

---

# 1. Mental Model Utama

```text id="hxo86e"
Concurrency
= banyak pekerjaan hidup dalam periode waktu yang sama

Parallelism
= banyak pekerjaan benar-benar berjalan bersamaan di banyak CPU/core

Thread
= unit eksekusi

Context
= data implisit/eksplisit yang melekat pada eksekusi:
  request id, user id, tenant id, transaction, security, MDC, locale, classloader
```

Contoh sederhana:

| Konsep | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Concurrency | 1 server melayani 1.000 request dengan interleaving |
| Parallelism | 8 core CPU memproses 8 task CPU-heavy bersamaan |
| Thread | Jalur eksekusi untuk menjalankan kode |
| Context | `correlationId`, `userId`, `SecurityContext`, `MDC` |

Rule utama:

```text id="jmgpq9"
Concurrency is about structure.
Parallelism is about execution.
Thread is about scheduling.
Context is about continuity of meaning across execution.
```

---

# 2. Concurrency vs Parallelism

## 2.1 Concurrency

Concurrency berarti beberapa task bisa **berlangsung overlapping**, walaupun tidak selalu berjalan secara fisik bersamaan.

```text id="t7bw7e"
Task A: start ---- wait I/O ---- continue ---- finish
Task B:       start ---- continue ---- wait I/O ---- finish
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="x74vsf"
HTTP request handling
database call
external API call
file I/O
message consumer
scheduler
workflow engine
```

---

## 2.2 Parallelism

Parallelism berarti beberapa task benar-benar berjalan bersamaan di banyak core.

```text id="my3iu7"
Core 1 -> Task A
Core 2 -> Task B
Core 3 -> Task C
Core 4 -> Task D
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="xaprr9"
CPU-heavy calculation
batch transformation
compression
encryption
image/video processing
large in-memory computation
parallel search
```

---

## 2.3 Decision Rule

| Workload | Model |
|---|---|
| Banyak blocking I/O | Concurrency |
| CPU-heavy | Parallelism |
| Banyak HTTP/DB call | Concurrency + bounded resource |
| Banyak kalkulasi murni | Parallelism |
| Request-response backend | Concurrency |
| Batch CPU processing | Parallelism |
| Workflow orchestration | Concurrency |
| Stream data transformation | Bisa concurrency atau parallelism tergantung workload |

---

# 3. Java Thread Model

Di Java modern ada dua jenis thread besar:

```text id="xkldvb"
Java Thread
├── Platform Thread
└── Virtual Thread
```

Oracle JDK 21 docs menjelaskan bahwa `Thread` adalah unit processing yang bisa dijadwalkan, dan sejak virtual threads ada dua jenis thread: **platform threads** dan **virtual threads**. Virtual threads diperkenalkan final lewat JEP 444 di JDK 21 sebagai thread ringan untuk aplikasi concurrent high-throughput. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/core/virtual-threads.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. Platform Thread

Platform thread adalah Java thread tradisional yang umumnya dipetakan ke OS thread.

```java id="8qubbp"
Thread thread = new Thread(() -> {
    System.out.println("Running in platform thread");
});

thread.start();
```

Karakter:

| Aspek | Platform Thread |
|---|---|
| Relatif mahal | Ya |
| Jumlah praktis | Terbatas |
| Mapped ke OS thread | Ya |
| Cocok untuk | CPU-bound, worker pool terbatas |
| Stack memory | Lebih mahal dibanding virtual thread |
| Scheduling | OS + JVM |

Masalah jika membuat terlalu banyak platform thread:

```text id="vzlsri"
high memory usage
context switching overhead
thread exhaustion
scheduler overhead
unstable latency
```

---

# 5. Virtual Thread

Virtual thread adalah lightweight thread yang dikelola JVM. Code tetap terlihat blocking, tapi lebih scalable untuk I/O-bound concurrency. JDK 21 menjadikan virtual threads final; virtual threads biasanya memakai sekumpulan kecil platform threads sebagai carrier threads, dan kode yang berjalan di virtual thread tetap melihat `Thread.currentThread()` sebagai virtual thread tersebut. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/444?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="1rjtcp"
Thread thread = Thread.startVirtualThread(() -> {
    System.out.println("Running in virtual thread");
});
```

Executor virtual thread:

```java id="0gt6hz"
try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
    Future<String> future = executor.submit(() -> callExternalApi());

    String result = future.get();
}
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="9obdyp"
blocking HTTP call
blocking JDBC call
file I/O
request-per-thread server style
many independent I/O tasks
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text id="rgijz8"
CPU-heavy work
unbounded database pressure
unbounded downstream API call
synchronized lock contention
workload yang bottleneck-nya bukan thread
```

Rule penting:

```text id="pa7kfv"
Virtual thread solves thread scalability.
It does not solve database pool, rate limit, lock contention, or CPU saturation.
```

---

# 6. Thread Lifecycle

```text id="pvpwnm"
NEW
  ↓ start()
RUNNABLE
  ↓ waiting/blocking/timed waiting
WAITING / TIMED_WAITING / BLOCKED
  ↓ resumes
RUNNABLE
  ↓ done/error
TERMINATED
```

State penting:

| State | Makna |
|---|---|
| `NEW` | Thread dibuat tapi belum start |
| `RUNNABLE` | Siap/jalan |
| `BLOCKED` | Menunggu monitor lock |
| `WAITING` | Menunggu tanpa timeout |
| `TIMED_WAITING` | Menunggu dengan timeout |
| `TERMINATED` | Selesai |

Contoh inspect:

```java id="l2kgl3"
Thread thread = Thread.currentThread();

System.out.println(thread.getName());
System.out.println(thread.getState());
System.out.println(thread.isVirtual());
```

---

# 7. Jangan Membuat Thread Manual Sembarangan

Boleh untuk demo:

```java id="90eggu"
new Thread(() -> doWork()).start();
```

Tapi di production backend, lebih umum pakai:

```text id="43yxja"
ExecutorService
ScheduledExecutorService
CompletableFuture
virtual-thread executor
framework-managed executor
message listener container
application server worker pool
```

Kenapa?

```text id="q2jzpx"
lifecycle lebih jelas
shutdown bisa dikontrol
pool size bisa dibatasi
observability lebih mudah
error handling lebih terpusat
resource tidak liar
```

---

# 8. ExecutorService

Executor memisahkan **task submission** dari **thread management**.

```java id="ag4z5u"
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(10);

Future<String> future = executor.submit(() -> {
    return loadData();
});

String result = future.get();

executor.shutdown();
```

Java docs menyebut bahwa action sebelum task disubmit ke `ExecutorService` happen-before action dalam task tersebut, dan action dalam task happen-before hasilnya diambil lewat `Future.get()`. Ini penting untuk visibility antar-thread. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/util/concurrent/ExecutorService.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8.1 Executor Types

| Executor | Use Case | Warning |
|---|---|---|
| `newFixedThreadPool(n)` | Worker pool bounded | Queue default bisa unbounded |
| `newCachedThreadPool()` | Burst short-lived task | Bisa tumbuh terlalu besar |
| `newSingleThreadExecutor()` | Serial execution | Bottleneck |
| `newScheduledThreadPool(n)` | Scheduled task | Handle exception dengan benar |
| `newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()` | Banyak blocking task | Tetap perlu limit DB/API |
| Custom `ThreadPoolExecutor` | Production control | Perlu desain queue/rejection |

---

## 8.2 Fixed Thread Pool

```java id="3y6h4h"
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(8);
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="3g0kfl"
CPU-heavy work
bounded worker
controlled parallel processing
```

Tapi hati-hati: factory method ini bisa memakai queue yang sangat besar. Untuk production critical system, sering lebih aman pakai `ThreadPoolExecutor` eksplisit.

---

## 8.3 Custom ThreadPoolExecutor

```java id="pirr4r"
ExecutorService executor = new ThreadPoolExecutor(
    8,                                  // corePoolSize
    16,                                 // maximumPoolSize
    60, TimeUnit.SECONDS,               // keepAlive
    new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000),     // bounded queue
    new ThreadFactory() {
        private final AtomicInteger seq = new AtomicInteger();

        @Override
        public Thread newThread(Runnable r) {
            Thread t = new Thread(r);
            t.setName("order-worker-" + seq.incrementAndGet());
            return t;
        }
    },
    new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy()
);
```

Parameter penting:

| Parameter | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `corePoolSize` | Jumlah thread dasar |
| `maximumPoolSize` | Jumlah thread maksimum |
| `keepAliveTime` | Idle thread lifetime |
| `workQueue` | Buffer task |
| `ThreadFactory` | Nama, daemon, handler |
| `RejectedExecutionHandler` | Apa yang terjadi saat overload |

---

## 8.4 Rejection Policy

| Policy | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `AbortPolicy` | Throw exception |
| `CallerRunsPolicy` | Caller ikut menjalankan task |
| `DiscardPolicy` | Buang task diam-diam |
| `DiscardOldestPolicy` | Buang task paling lama |

Default production recommendation:

```text id="6z7892"
Avoid silent discard unless business explicitly accepts data loss.
Prefer bounded queue + explicit rejection handling.
```

---

# 9. Future

```java id="xmfj6g"
Future<Order> future = executor.submit(() -> loadOrder(orderId));

Order order = future.get();
```

Dengan timeout:

```java id="esmlmp"
Order order = future.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

Cancel:

```java id="6dnl3h"
future.cancel(true);
```

Problem dengan `Future`:

```text id="e6f390"
composition lemah
error handling verbose
manual blocking dengan get()
tidak enak untuk combine beberapa async task
```

Untuk komposisi async, pakai `CompletableFuture`.

---

# 10. CompletableFuture

## 10.1 Basic

```java id="n9u6io"
CompletableFuture<User> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> userClient.getUser(userId));
```

Get result:

```java id="wbjton"
User user = future.join();
```

`join()` membungkus exception sebagai `CompletionException`, sedangkan `get()` melempar checked exceptions.

---

## 10.2 Transform

```java id="u9fu6b"
CompletableFuture<String> emailFuture = userFuture
    .thenApply(User::email);
```

Async transform:

```java id="to37xw"
CompletableFuture<String> emailFuture = userFuture
    .thenApplyAsync(User::email, executor);
```

Rule:

```text id="wwplbq"
thenApply      -> lanjut di thread yang menyelesaikan stage sebelumnya
thenApplyAsync -> lanjut lewat executor
```

---

## 10.3 Compose

Kalau function mengembalikan `CompletableFuture`, pakai `thenCompose`.

```java id="yiw91p"
CompletableFuture<OrderView> viewFuture =
    loadOrder(orderId)
        .thenCompose(order ->
            loadCustomer(order.customerId())
                .thenApply(customer -> new OrderView(order, customer))
        );
```

Analogi:

| Method | Makna |
|---|---|
| `thenApply` | `T -> U` |
| `thenCompose` | `T -> CompletableFuture<U>` lalu flatten |
| `thenCombine` | combine dua future |

---

## 10.4 Combine

```java id="9lkl2e"
CompletableFuture<User> userFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadUser(userId), executor);

CompletableFuture<Order> orderFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadOrder(orderId), executor);

CompletableFuture<OrderView> viewFuture =
    userFuture.thenCombine(orderFuture, OrderView::new);
```

---

## 10.5 Error Handling

```java id="bssngb"
CompletableFuture<User> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadUser(userId), executor)
        .exceptionally(ex -> fallbackUser());
```

Dengan logging:

```java id="969ftm"
CompletableFuture<User> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadUser(userId), executor)
        .whenComplete((result, error) -> {
            if (error != null) {
                log.error("Failed to load user: userId={}", userId, error);
            }
        });
```

Recover berdasarkan exception:

```java id="lgf0pc"
CompletableFuture<User> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadUser(userId), executor)
        .handle((result, error) -> {
            if (error != null) {
                return fallbackUser();
            }
            return result;
        });
```

---

## 10.6 Timeout

```java id="f1fjdm"
CompletableFuture<User> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadUser(userId), executor)
        .orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

Fallback on timeout:

```java id="belbf2"
CompletableFuture<User> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> loadUser(userId), executor)
        .completeOnTimeout(fallbackUser(), 3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

---

# 11. Java Memory Model

Concurrency bug sering bukan karena “dua thread jalan bareng”, tapi karena:

```text id="ebk3q1"
visibility
atomicity
ordering
race condition
unsafe publication
```

JLS Chapter 17 mendefinisikan Java Memory Model. Secara praktis, hasil write oleh satu thread dijamin terlihat oleh thread lain hanya jika ada hubungan **happens-before** yang benar, misalnya lewat `synchronized`, `volatile`, `Thread.start()`, atau `Thread.join()`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/specs/jls/se8/html/jls-17.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11.1 Visibility Problem

```java id="t8g1g0"
class Worker {
    private boolean running = true;

    void stop() {
        running = false;
    }

    void run() {
        while (running) {
            // do work
        }
    }
}
```

Masalah: thread yang menjalankan `run()` belum tentu melihat update `running = false`.

Fix dengan `volatile`:

```java id="ez1nqg"
class Worker {
    private volatile boolean running = true;

    void stop() {
        running = false;
    }

    void run() {
        while (running) {
            // do work
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 11.2 Atomicity Problem

```java id="c31fuw"
count++;
```

Ini bukan satu operasi atomik. Kira-kira:

```text id="0bbiyf"
read count
add 1
write count
```

Jika dua thread melakukan bersamaan, update bisa hilang.

Fix dengan `AtomicInteger`:

```java id="1o2qa5"
AtomicInteger count = new AtomicInteger();

count.incrementAndGet();
```

Atau dengan lock:

```java id="b0mfm0"
synchronized void increment() {
    count++;
}
```

---

## 11.3 Ordering Problem

Compiler/JIT/CPU boleh melakukan reordering selama single-thread semantics tetap benar. Dalam multi-threading, ini bisa berbahaya jika tidak ada synchronization.

Rule praktis:

```text id="f8ntvp"
If data is shared across threads, protect it with:
- immutability
- volatile
- synchronized
- Lock
- atomic classes
- concurrent collections
- safe publication
```

---

# 12. `synchronized`

`synchronized` memberi:

```text id="0my4ak"
mutual exclusion
visibility
happens-before via monitor enter/exit
```

Method synchronized:

```java id="b15cv1"
public synchronized void increment() {
    count++;
}
```

Block synchronized:

```java id="jy6vpu"
private final Object lock = new Object();

public void increment() {
    synchronized (lock) {
        count++;
    }
}
```

Prefer private lock object:

```java id="rhufp5"
private final Object lock = new Object();
```

Hindari lock pada public object:

```java id="0m5aaq"
synchronized (this) {
    // external code can also lock this
}
```

---

# 13. `volatile`

`volatile` menjamin visibility dan ordering tertentu, tapi bukan compound atomicity.

Cocok:

```java id="lpg1ll"
private volatile boolean shutdown;
```

Tidak cukup:

```java id="uh3465"
volatile int count;

count++; // still not atomic
```

Gunakan `volatile` untuk:

| Cocok | Tidak Cocok |
|---|---|
| shutdown flag | counter increment |
| config reference swap | compound mutation |
| status visible antar-thread | check-then-act |
| single writer/many reader simple state | invariant multi-field |

---

# 14. Locks

## 14.1 ReentrantLock

```java id="kw2p88"
private final ReentrantLock lock = new ReentrantLock();

public void update() {
    lock.lock();
    try {
        // critical section
    } finally {
        lock.unlock();
    }
}
```

Kelebihan dibanding `synchronized`:

| Feature | ReentrantLock |
|---|---|
| Try lock | Ya |
| Timeout lock | Ya |
| Interruptible lock | Ya |
| Fairness option | Ya |
| Multiple condition | Ya |

Try lock:

```java id="o0oqq2"
if (lock.tryLock()) {
    try {
        // work
    } finally {
        lock.unlock();
    }
} else {
    // cannot acquire lock
}
```

---

## 14.2 ReadWriteLock

```java id="twm3q8"
private final ReadWriteLock rwLock = new ReentrantReadWriteLock();
private final Map<String, String> cache = new HashMap<>();

public String get(String key) {
    rwLock.readLock().lock();
    try {
        return cache.get(key);
    } finally {
        rwLock.readLock().unlock();
    }
}

public void put(String key, String value) {
    rwLock.writeLock().lock();
    try {
        cache.put(key, value);
    } finally {
        rwLock.writeLock().unlock();
    }
}
```

Cocok jika:

```text id="0c0h42"
read jauh lebih banyak daripada write
critical section cukup besar
contention nyata
```

Tidak selalu lebih cepat daripada simple lock.

---

## 14.3 StampedLock

Advanced lock untuk optimistic read.

```java id="samr1s"
private final StampedLock lock = new StampedLock();
private int x;
private int y;

public int distance() {
    long stamp = lock.tryOptimisticRead();

    int currentX = x;
    int currentY = y;

    if (!lock.validate(stamp)) {
        stamp = lock.readLock();
        try {
            currentX = x;
            currentY = y;
        } finally {
            lock.unlockRead(stamp);
        }
    }

    return currentX + currentY;
}
```

Gunakan hanya jika benar-benar perlu. Complexity-nya lebih tinggi.

---

# 15. Atomic Classes

```java id="mv60o0"
AtomicInteger counter = new AtomicInteger();

counter.incrementAndGet();
counter.compareAndSet(10, 20);
```

Common classes:

| Class | Use |
|---|---|
| `AtomicInteger` | atomic int |
| `AtomicLong` | atomic long |
| `AtomicBoolean` | atomic boolean |
| `AtomicReference<T>` | atomic reference |
| `LongAdder` | high-contention counter |
| `LongAccumulator` | custom accumulation |

High contention counter:

```java id="yz1hxh"
LongAdder counter = new LongAdder();

counter.increment();
counter.add(10);

long value = counter.sum();
```

Rule:

```text id="3dvkxk"
Atomic is good for simple independent state.
Lock is better for multi-field invariant.
```

---

# 16. Concurrent Collections

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Concurrent map | `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| Concurrent set | `ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet()` |
| Producer-consumer blocking | `BlockingQueue` |
| Read-heavy list | `CopyOnWriteArrayList` |
| Non-blocking queue | `ConcurrentLinkedQueue` |
| Delay task | `DelayQueue` |
| Priority blocking queue | `PriorityBlockingQueue` |

Example counter map:

```java id="u0lgpt"
ConcurrentHashMap<String, LongAdder> counters =
    new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

counters
    .computeIfAbsent("orders.submitted", key -> new LongAdder())
    .increment();
```

Concurrent set:

```java id="mts6y7"
Set<String> activeUsers = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

activeUsers.add(userId);
```

---

# 17. BlockingQueue

Producer-consumer pattern:

```java id="brqk5b"
BlockingQueue<Job> queue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000);
```

Producer:

```java id="uvmkjk"
queue.put(job); // waits if full
```

Consumer:

```java id="82h89d"
while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
    Job job = queue.take(); // waits if empty
    process(job);
}
```

Use timeout:

```java id="l5t96m"
Job job = queue.poll(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

if (job != null) {
    process(job);
}
```

BlockingQueue memberi natural backpressure.

---

# 18. CountDownLatch, Semaphore, Phaser

## 18.1 CountDownLatch

Menunggu beberapa task selesai.

```java id="j382or"
CountDownLatch latch = new CountDownLatch(3);

executor.submit(() -> {
    try {
        task1();
    } finally {
        latch.countDown();
    }
});

latch.await();
```

---

## 18.2 Semaphore

Membatasi concurrency.

```java id="zxn4v2"
Semaphore semaphore = new Semaphore(10);

public void callExternalApi() throws InterruptedException {
    semaphore.acquire();
    try {
        externalApi.call();
    } finally {
        semaphore.release();
    }
}
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="e4cnmv"
rate-sensitive downstream
limited expensive resource
parallelism cap
bulkhead
```

---

## 18.3 Phaser

Koordinasi multi-phase task.

```java id="80mtug"
Phaser phaser = new Phaser(3);

Runnable task = () -> {
    phaseOne();
    phaser.arriveAndAwaitAdvance();

    phaseTwo();
    phaser.arriveAndAwaitAdvance();
};
```

Jarang dipakai di backend CRUD biasa, tapi berguna untuk batch/parallel algorithms.

---

# 19. Parallel Stream

```java id="gzxczp"
List<Result> results = items.parallelStream()
    .map(this::cpuHeavyTransform)
    .toList();
```

Cocok jika:

```text id="nuaxoa"
CPU-bound
stateless
data cukup besar
tidak blocking I/O
tidak akses shared mutable state
sudah diukur benefit-nya
```

Tidak cocok:

```java id="1sbrqd"
orders.parallelStream()
    .map(order -> database.load(order.customerId()))
    .toList();
```

Masalah:

```text id="86nd7c"
uncontrolled concurrency
common ForkJoinPool contention
DB/API overload
transaction context issue
debugging sulit
```

Rule:

```text id="7xbqsy"
Default to sequential stream.
Use parallel stream only for measured CPU-bound pure computation.
```

---

# 20. ForkJoinPool

ForkJoin cocok untuk divide-and-conquer CPU workload.

```java id="4xb4xy"
class SumTask extends RecursiveTask<Long> {
    private final long[] numbers;
    private final int start;
    private final int end;

    SumTask(long[] numbers, int start, int end) {
        this.numbers = numbers;
        this.start = start;
        this.end = end;
    }

    @Override
    protected Long compute() {
        if (end - start <= 1_000) {
            long sum = 0;
            for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
                sum += numbers[i];
            }
            return sum;
        }

        int mid = (start + end) / 2;

        SumTask left = new SumTask(numbers, start, mid);
        SumTask right = new SumTask(numbers, mid, end);

        left.fork();

        long rightResult = right.compute();
        long leftResult = left.join();

        return leftResult + rightResult;
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="8szrcr"
ForkJoinPool pool = new ForkJoinPool();

long result = pool.invoke(new SumTask(numbers, 0, numbers.length));
```

---

# 21. Thread Context

Context adalah data yang membuat eksekusi punya makna.

Contoh context:

```text id="5nqb10"
correlationId
requestId
traceId
spanId
tenantId
userId
role/permission
locale
timezone
security principal
transaction
classloader
MDC logging context
```

Masalah besar:

```text id="xjs9h1"
Context sering hilang saat pindah thread.
```

Contoh:

```java id="kcbwj3"
log.info("requestId={}", requestId);

CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
    log.info("Where is my requestId?");
});
```

Jika `requestId` disimpan di `ThreadLocal` atau MDC thread lokal, task async belum tentu melihatnya.

---

# 22. ThreadLocal

`ThreadLocal<T>` menyimpan data per-thread.

```java id="qk7yp8"
private static final ThreadLocal<String> REQUEST_ID =
    new ThreadLocal<>();

public void handle(String requestId) {
    REQUEST_ID.set(requestId);

    try {
        process();
    } finally {
        REQUEST_ID.remove();
    }
}
```

Read:

```java id="ct8lft"
String requestId = REQUEST_ID.get();
```

Wajib `remove()`:

```java id="hb32r8"
finally {
    REQUEST_ID.remove();
}
```

Kalau lupa remove di thread pool:

```text id="l7prxu"
request context bocor ke request lain
memory leak
security bug
wrong tenant/user
wrong correlation id
```

---

## 22.1 ThreadLocal Anti-Pattern

Buruk:

```java id="dbrc66"
REQUEST_USER.set(user);

// no remove
```

Lebih aman:

```java id="1y7f2d"
REQUEST_USER.set(user);
try {
    service.process();
} finally {
    REQUEST_USER.remove();
}
```

---

# 23. InheritableThreadLocal

```java id="x43p0q"
private static final InheritableThreadLocal<String> CONTEXT =
    new InheritableThreadLocal<>();
```

Masalah:

```text id="xligj2"
tidak cocok untuk thread pool
context diwariskan saat thread dibuat, bukan saat task disubmit
bisa stale
bisa bocor
```

Rule:

```text id="ul0ktf"
Avoid InheritableThreadLocal for executor/thread pool context propagation.
```

---

# 24. ScopedValue

Scoped values adalah mekanisme modern untuk membagikan immutable context dalam scope tertentu. JEP 506 memperkenalkan Scoped Values sebagai cara yang aman dan efisien untuk membagikan data ke callees dalam thread yang sama dan child threads tanpa parameter method; fitur ini tercatat sebagai bagian dari JDK 25. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/506?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Conceptual example:

```java id="mdo1zu"
private static final ScopedValue<RequestContext> REQUEST_CONTEXT =
    ScopedValue.newInstance();

public void handle(RequestContext context) {
    ScopedValue.where(REQUEST_CONTEXT, context)
        .run(() -> service.process());
}

public void process() {
    RequestContext context = REQUEST_CONTEXT.get();
}
```

Kelebihan dibanding `ThreadLocal`:

| ThreadLocal | ScopedValue |
|---|---|
| Mutable per-thread slot | Immutable scoped binding |
| Bisa lupa remove | Scope otomatis selesai |
| Bisa bocor di thread pool | Lifetime lebih eksplisit |
| Mahal jika banyak virtual thread | Lebih cocok untuk virtual thread |
| Implicit global-ish | Structured context |

Gunakan untuk:

```text id="4k1l20"
request context
tenant context
security principal immutable
trace/correlation context
locale/timezone context
```

Jangan gunakan untuk mutable shared state.

---

# 25. Context Propagation

## 25.1 Explicit Context Passing

Paling jelas:

```java id="kb6fl6"
public record RequestContext(
    String requestId,
    String userId,
    String tenantId
) {}
```

```java id="l5fr3c"
service.process(context, command);
repository.save(context, entity);
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="siz8pa"
jelas
testable
tidak magic
aman antar-thread
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="tocbwb"
parameter bisa menyebar luas
method signature lebih ramai
```

---

## 25.2 Context Wrapper untuk Executor

```java id="5xjf48"
public final class ContextAwareExecutor implements Executor {
    private final Executor delegate;

    public ContextAwareExecutor(Executor delegate) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void execute(Runnable command) {
        RequestContext captured = RequestContextHolder.get();

        delegate.execute(() -> {
            RequestContext previous = RequestContextHolder.get();

            try {
                RequestContextHolder.set(captured);
                command.run();
            } finally {
                RequestContextHolder.set(previous);
            }
        });
    }
}
```

Tetap wajib hati-hati agar context tidak bocor.

---

## 25.3 MDC Logging Context

Dengan SLF4J MDC:

```java id="h5wntx"
MDC.put("requestId", requestId);
MDC.put("userId", userId);

try {
    service.process();
} finally {
    MDC.clear();
}
```

Di async task, MDC perlu dipropagasi manual atau lewat framework.

Wrapper:

```java id="iajfkz"
public static Runnable withMdc(Runnable task) {
    Map<String, String> contextMap = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

    return () -> {
        Map<String, String> previous = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

        try {
            if (contextMap != null) {
                MDC.setContextMap(contextMap);
            } else {
                MDC.clear();
            }

            task.run();
        } finally {
            if (previous != null) {
                MDC.setContextMap(previous);
            } else {
                MDC.clear();
            }
        }
    };
}
```

Use:

```java id="wp2x9k"
executor.submit(withMdc(() -> service.process()));
```

---

# 26. Security Context

Contoh context sensitif:

```text id="6hsp96"
authenticated user
roles
permissions
tenant
session
token claims
```

Bahaya jika salah propagation:

```text id="2xhsru"
request A memakai user request B
tenant leakage
audit trail salah
authorization bypass
```

Rule:

```text id="wq11o3"
Security context must be explicit, scoped, cleared, and tested.
```

Jangan mengandalkan global mutable static.

---

# 27. Transaction Context

Transaction context biasanya thread-bound di banyak framework.

Contoh konseptual:

```java id="gm6y6n"
@Transactional
public void submitOrder(OrderId orderId) {
    orderRepository.load(orderId);

    CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
        orderRepository.saveAudit(...);
    });
}
```

Bahaya:

```text id="e91nr0"
async task mungkin tidak ikut transaction
EntityManager/Session tidak thread-safe
lazy loading bisa gagal
commit/rollback boundary tidak jelas
```

Rule:

```text id="31uy8i"
Do not casually cross transaction boundary with async thread.
If async needed, define a new transaction boundary explicitly.
```

---

# 28. ClassLoader Context

Di enterprise runtime, ada juga:

```java id="ea2zic"
Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader();
```

Context classloader dipakai oleh:

```text id="a29wwj"
JPA
JAX-RS
CDI
ServiceLoader
application server
plugin architecture
JDBC driver loading
serialization framework
```

Jika membuat thread sendiri di application server, context classloader bisa salah.

Rule:

```text id="b9tl7r"
In managed runtimes, prefer framework-managed executors.
```

---

# 29. Structured Concurrency

Structured concurrency memperlakukan beberapa subtask yang terkait sebagai satu unit kerja: lifecycle, error, cancellation, dan observability jadi lebih jelas. OpenJDK JEP 533 masih menyebut Structured Concurrency sebagai preview API, sementara Oracle JDK 26 migration docs merujuk JEP 525 sebagai Sixth Preview. Jadi untuk Java 17/21 enterprise production, gunakan dengan sadar bahwa API ini belum final di semua baseline. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/533?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Problem yang ingin diselesaikan:

```java id="xsv0lp"
Future<User> userFuture = executor.submit(() -> loadUser(userId));
Future<Order> orderFuture = executor.submit(() -> loadOrder(orderId));

User user = userFuture.get();
Order order = orderFuture.get();
```

Masalah:

```text id="fih2q6"
kalau satu gagal, task lain bagaimana?
kalau request timeout, siapa cancel?
kalau parent selesai, child task masih jalan?
kalau observability, task ini milik request mana?
```

Structured concurrency mindset:

```text id="uwkecs"
Subtasks should not outlive their parent task.
Failure should propagate clearly.
Cancellation should be coordinated.
Related concurrent work should be visible as one unit.
```

---

# 30. Race Condition

Race condition terjadi saat hasil program bergantung pada timing interleaving antar-thread.

Buruk:

```java id="3pj8op"
class Counter {
    private int count;

    void increment() {
        count++;
    }

    int value() {
        return count;
    }
}
```

Fix:

```java id="k3oxrg"
class Counter {
    private final AtomicInteger count = new AtomicInteger();

    void increment() {
        count.incrementAndGet();
    }

    int value() {
        return count.get();
    }
}
```

Atau:

```java id="2ajzcw"
class Counter {
    private int count;

    synchronized void increment() {
        count++;
    }

    synchronized int value() {
        return count;
    }
}
```

---

# 31. Deadlock

Deadlock terjadi saat thread saling menunggu lock.

```java id="i3f6kh"
synchronized (lockA) {
    synchronized (lockB) {
        // work
    }
}
```

Thread lain:

```java id="npr460"
synchronized (lockB) {
    synchronized (lockA) {
        // work
    }
}
```

Fix:

```text id="vgcabc"
consistent lock ordering
smaller critical section
tryLock with timeout
avoid nested locks
use higher-level concurrency abstraction
```

Consistent ordering:

```java id="6znwz9"
Object first = order(lockA, lockB).first();
Object second = order(lockA, lockB).second();

synchronized (first) {
    synchronized (second) {
        // work
    }
}
```

---

# 32. Starvation, Livelock, Contention

| Problem | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Starvation | Thread tidak pernah mendapat kesempatan |
| Livelock | Thread aktif tapi tidak progress |
| Contention | Banyak thread rebutan resource |
| Convoying | Banyak thread menunggu satu lock lambat |
| Priority inversion | Thread penting tertahan thread prioritas rendah |

Contoh contention:

```text id="9p6qsq"
all requests lock same global object
all tasks wait for same DB connection pool
all threads update same AtomicLong
all virtual threads hit same rate-limited API
```

Fix:

```text id="tmir8h"
reduce shared state
partition lock
use LongAdder
use batching
use queue
use bulkhead
use connection pool sizing
use backpressure
```

---

# 33. Immutability as Concurrency Strategy

Immutable object aman dibagi antar-thread.

```java id="ovcpey"
public record RequestContext(
    String requestId,
    String userId,
    String tenantId
) {}
```

Immutable collection:

```java id="msjpjh"
public record OrderSnapshot(List<OrderLine> lines) {
    public OrderSnapshot {
        lines = List.copyOf(lines);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="dv93oh"
Prefer immutable data crossing thread boundaries.
Mutable data should have one owner or explicit synchronization.
```

---

# 34. Safe Publication

Object yang dibuat di satu thread harus dipublikasikan secara aman ke thread lain.

Safe ways:

```text id="4h7h9t"
final fields after constructor
volatile reference
synchronized block
thread-safe collection
Executor task submission
static initialization
CompletableFuture/Future completion
```

Example:

```java id="6dnew9"
public final class Config {
    private final String endpoint;
    private final Duration timeout;

    public Config(String endpoint, Duration timeout) {
        this.endpoint = endpoint;
        this.timeout = timeout;
    }
}
```

Final fields membantu object immutable dipublikasikan dengan aman.

---

# 35. Thread Pool Sizing

## 35.1 CPU-bound

Rule kasar:

```text id="tjxz6k"
threads ≈ number of CPU cores
```

Example:

```java id="300hgm"
int cores = Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors();

ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(cores);
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="f2l4o2"
calculation
compression
encryption
pure transformation
```

---

## 35.2 I/O-bound

Rule kasar:

```text id="u2tr8h"
threads can be larger than cores,
but must be limited by downstream capacity.
```

Faktor sizing:

```text id="71s94t"
DB connection pool
HTTP connection pool
API rate limit
latency
timeout
memory
queue size
SLA
```

Dengan virtual threads, jumlah thread bukan lagi bottleneck utama, tapi resource lain tetap harus dibatasi.

---

## 35.3 Bounded Resource Example

```java id="io1yw7"
Semaphore dbBulkhead = new Semaphore(50);

public Order loadOrder(OrderId id) throws InterruptedException {
    dbBulkhead.acquire();
    try {
        return orderRepository.load(id);
    } finally {
        dbBulkhead.release();
    }
}
```

---

# 36. Backpressure

Backpressure berarti sistem menolak/memperlambat input saat tidak mampu memproses.

Tanpa backpressure:

```text id="13l1jo"
request masuk terus
queue tumbuh terus
memory naik
latency naik
timeout naik
akhirnya crash
```

Dengan backpressure:

```text id="78hd52"
bounded queue
timeout
rejection
rate limit
bulkhead
caller-runs
load shedding
```

ThreadPoolExecutor dengan bounded queue:

```java id="c5a2v9"
ExecutorService executor = new ThreadPoolExecutor(
    8,
    8,
    0L,
    TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
    new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(500),
    new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy()
);
```

---

# 37. Cancellation and Interruption

## 37.1 Interrupt

```java id="qi04v1"
Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
```

Worker loop:

```java id="1d7t50"
while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
    doWork();
}
```

Blocking method biasanya throw `InterruptedException`:

```java id="88jo91"
try {
    queue.take();
} catch (InterruptedException e) {
    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
    return;
}
```

Rule:

```text id="8ksmei"
If you catch InterruptedException, either:
- stop and restore interrupt flag
- or propagate it
```

Buruk:

```java id="kilijq"
catch (InterruptedException e) {
    // ignore
}
```

Baik:

```java id="2xwiir"
catch (InterruptedException e) {
    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
    return;
}
```

---

# 38. Timeouts

Concurrency tanpa timeout adalah sumber hanging system.

HTTP call:

```java id="8mdyjd"
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
    .build();
```

Future:

```java id="gdy2yl"
future.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

CompletableFuture:

```java id="c5s29r"
future.orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

Lock:

```java id="sfxvqz"
if (lock.tryLock(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
    try {
        // work
    } finally {
        lock.unlock();
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="9bx97r"
Every remote call, blocking wait, lock wait, and queue wait should have a timeout strategy.
```

---

# 39. Context in REST/JAX-RS Backend

Typical request flow:

```text id="wpzlsk"
HTTP request
  ↓
JAX-RS resource
  ↓
security filter
  ↓
request context / correlation id
  ↓
application service
  ↓
transaction boundary
  ↓
repository / external client
  ↓
response
```

Context candidates:

```text id="l5v0n3"
correlationId -> logging/tracing
principal     -> authorization
tenantId      -> data isolation
locale        -> message formatting
timezone      -> temporal conversion
transaction   -> DB consistency
```

Do not pass huge framework objects everywhere. Prefer compact domain/application context.

```java id="8io8bd"
public record ExecutionContext(
    String correlationId,
    String userId,
    String tenantId,
    Locale locale,
    ZoneId zoneId
) {}
```

---

# 40. Context in Camunda/Workflow

Workflow systems add another kind of context:

```text id="afm1uz"
processInstanceId
businessKey
taskId
caseId
correlationId
tenantId
current activity
retry count
incident id
```

Important distinction:

| Context | Lifetime |
|---|---|
| Thread context | Current execution only |
| Request context | One HTTP request |
| Transaction context | One DB transaction |
| Workflow context | Long-running process |
| Business context | Case/order/customer lifecycle |

Rule:

```text id="kfglk1"
Never rely on thread context for long-running workflow state.
Persist workflow/business context explicitly.
```

---

# 41. Common Enterprise Failure Modes

| Failure | Root Cause |
|---|---|
| Random wrong user in logs | ThreadLocal/MDC not cleared |
| Tenant data leak | Context propagation bug |
| Async task sees no transaction | Transaction context is thread-bound |
| Lazy loading exception | Entity used outside original thread/session |
| Thread pool exhaustion | Blocking calls on bounded pool |
| Memory spike | Unbounded queue |
| DB overload | Too many concurrent tasks |
| API rate limit | No semaphore/rate limiter |
| Deadlock | Nested locks inconsistent order |
| Lost update | Non-atomic shared mutation |
| Stale config | Missing volatile/safe publication |
| Request hangs forever | Missing timeout |
| High CPU | Busy loop / spin / excessive contention |
| Virtual thread still slow | Bottleneck is DB/API/lock, not thread count |

---

# 42. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa Buruk |
|---|---|
| `new Thread()` per request | Lifecycle tidak terkendali |
| Unbounded executor queue | Memory risk |
| `parallelStream()` untuk DB/API call | Uncontrolled concurrency |
| Catch `InterruptedException` lalu ignore | Cancellation rusak |
| Shared mutable `HashMap` | Race/corruption |
| Static mutable context | Cross-request bug |
| ThreadLocal tanpa `remove()` | Context leak |
| Async dalam transaction tanpa desain | Inconsistent data |
| Lock global untuk semua request | Contention |
| Virtual thread tanpa bulkhead | Downstream overload |
| No timeout | Hanging system |
| Fire-and-forget task tanpa observability | Lost failure |
| Swallow exception dalam scheduled task | Silent broken job |

---

# 43. Practical Code Patterns

## 43.1 Bounded Async Fan-Out

```java id="6mspr0"
public List<Customer> loadCustomers(Set<CustomerId> customerIds)
        throws InterruptedException {

    Semaphore semaphore = new Semaphore(20);

    try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
        List<Future<Customer>> futures = new ArrayList<>();

        for (CustomerId id : customerIds) {
            futures.add(executor.submit(() -> {
                semaphore.acquire();
                try {
                    return customerClient.load(id);
                } finally {
                    semaphore.release();
                }
            }));
        }

        List<Customer> result = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Future<Customer> future : futures) {
            try {
                result.add(future.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS));
            } catch (ExecutionException e) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Failed to load customer", e);
            } catch (TimeoutException e) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Customer lookup timeout", e);
            }
        }

        return result;
    }
}
```

Key point:

```text id="mjqmj8"
Virtual threads give cheap concurrency.
Semaphore gives downstream protection.
Timeout gives failure boundary.
```

---

## 43.2 Safe Worker Loop

```java id="5hf1zo"
public final class Worker implements Runnable {
    private final BlockingQueue<Job> queue;

    public Worker(BlockingQueue<Job> queue) {
        this.queue = queue;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
            try {
                Job job = queue.poll(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

                if (job != null) {
                    process(job);
                }
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Worker failed to process job", e);
            }
        }
    }

    private void process(Job job) {
        // process job
    }
}
```

---

## 43.3 Context-Aware Task

```java id="j841gj"
public record RequestContext(
    String correlationId,
    String userId,
    String tenantId
) {}
```

```java id="wjx6jy"
public final class ContextualTask implements Runnable {
    private final RequestContext context;
    private final Runnable delegate;

    public ContextualTask(RequestContext context, Runnable delegate) {
        this.context = context;
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        MDC.put("correlationId", context.correlationId());
        MDC.put("userId", context.userId());
        MDC.put("tenantId", context.tenantId());

        try {
            delegate.run();
        } finally {
            MDC.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="rl3r0m"
executor.submit(new ContextualTask(context, () -> service.process(command)));
```

---

## 43.4 Atomic State Machine Guard

```java id="ewy4rr"
public enum JobStatus {
    NEW,
    RUNNING,
    COMPLETED,
    FAILED
}
```

```java id="8dwvm9"
public final class JobControl {
    private final AtomicReference<JobStatus> status =
        new AtomicReference<>(JobStatus.NEW);

    public boolean start() {
        return status.compareAndSet(JobStatus.NEW, JobStatus.RUNNING);
    }

    public void complete() {
        if (!status.compareAndSet(JobStatus.RUNNING, JobStatus.COMPLETED)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Job is not running");
        }
    }

    public void fail() {
        status.set(JobStatus.FAILED);
    }
}
```

Cocok untuk simple state transition. Untuk multi-field invariant, gunakan lock atau transaction.

# 44. Debugging Concurrency

## 44.1 Thread Dump

```bash id="il76vk"
jcmd <pid> Thread.print
```

Atau:

```bash id="fjk5uf"
jstack <pid>
```

Cari:

```text id="elprdi"
BLOCKED
WAITING
deadlock
same lock many threads
pool thread all busy
HTTP client wait
DB pool wait
ForkJoinPool commonPool saturation
```

---

## 44.2 JFR

```bash id="fo64qb"
java \
  -XX:StartFlightRecording=filename=app.jfr,duration=60s,settings=profile \
  -jar app.jar
```

Cari:

```text id="cmkrnv"
thread allocation
lock contention
blocked time
socket read
file I/O
CPU hotspot
GC pressure
virtual thread pinning
```

---

## 44.3 Logs

Good log fields:

```text id="sisaws"
timestamp
level
thread name
correlationId
traceId
spanId
userId if safe
tenantId
operation
duration
outcome
error type
```

Example:

```java id="lddtm3"
log.info(
    "Order submitted: correlationId={}, orderId={}, durationMs={}",
    context.correlationId(),
    orderId,
    duration.toMillis()
);
```

# 45. Onboarding Checklist

Saat baca codebase Java enterprise, cek ini:

```text id="oe4t5g"
Threading
[ ] Siapa yang membuat thread?
[ ] Apakah executor bounded?
[ ] Apakah shutdown dikelola?
[ ] Apakah task punya timeout?
[ ] Apakah interruption ditangani benar?

Concurrency Safety
[ ] Ada shared mutable state?
[ ] Apakah state dilindungi lock/atomic/concurrent collection?
[ ] Apakah volatile dipakai untuk visibility, bukan atomic increment?
[ ] Apakah object dipublish dengan aman?
[ ] Apakah collection mutable dibagi antar-thread?

Parallelism
[ ] Apakah parallelStream dipakai?
[ ] Apakah workload CPU-bound atau I/O-bound?
[ ] Apakah common ForkJoinPool dipakai tanpa sadar?
[ ] Apakah ada benchmark/measurement?

Context
[ ] Apakah requestId/correlationId selalu muncul di log?
[ ] Apakah MDC dibersihkan?
[ ] Apakah ThreadLocal di-remove?
[ ] Apakah security context bisa bocor?
[ ] Apakah async task kehilangan transaction?
[ ] Apakah tenant context eksplisit?

Resource
[ ] Apakah DB pool dibatasi?
[ ] Apakah external API punya rate limit/bulkhead?
[ ] Apakah queue bounded?
[ ] Apakah rejection policy jelas?
[ ] Apakah backpressure ada?

Failure
[ ] Apa yang terjadi kalau task gagal?
[ ] Apa yang terjadi kalau timeout?
[ ] Apa yang terjadi kalau parent request cancelled?
[ ] Apakah child task bisa hidup lebih lama dari parent?
[ ] Apakah error async terlihat di monitoring?
```

---

# 46. Decision Matrix

| Problem | Tool |
|---|---|
| Simple async task | `ExecutorService` |
| Compose async result | `CompletableFuture` |
| Many blocking I/O tasks | Virtual threads |
| CPU-heavy parallel work | Fixed pool / ForkJoin |
| Shared counter | `AtomicInteger` / `LongAdder` |
| Shared map | `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| Producer-consumer | `BlockingQueue` |
| Limit downstream concurrency | `Semaphore` |
| Protect critical section | `synchronized` / `ReentrantLock` |
| Many readers few writers | `ReadWriteLock` |
| Request context | Explicit context / ScopedValue |
| Logging context | MDC with propagation/cleanup |
| Transaction context | Framework boundary, avoid casual async |
| Cancellation | interrupt + timeout |
| Overload control | bounded queue + rejection + backpressure |
| Related subtasks lifecycle | structured concurrency mindset/API where available |

---

# 47. Most Important Rules

```text id="x6gquz"
1. Do not share mutable state unless you have a synchronization strategy.
2. Prefer immutable objects across thread boundaries.
3. Use ExecutorService, not random new Thread().
4. Every pool needs bounded capacity or explicit overload behavior.
5. Every blocking operation needs timeout/cancellation strategy.
6. volatile is for visibility, not compound atomicity.
7. Atomic is for simple independent state, not multi-field invariant.
8. Locks protect invariants, not just variables.
9. parallelStream is not for blocking I/O.
10. Virtual threads are excellent for blocking I/O, but still need bulkheads.
11. ThreadLocal must be cleaned with remove().
12. Context propagation must be designed, not assumed.
13. Transaction/security/request context should not accidentally cross threads.
14. Async failures must be observable.
15. Thread dumps and JFR are mandatory debugging tools.
```

---

# 48. Compact Summary

```text id="o7ftrm"
Concurrency
- Overlapping tasks.
- Useful for I/O, request handling, workflow, messaging.

Parallelism
- Actually simultaneous execution.
- Useful for CPU-heavy computation.

Platform Thread
- Traditional Java thread.
- More expensive.
- Use bounded pools.

Virtual Thread
- Lightweight Java thread, final since JDK 21.
- Great for blocking I/O.
- Not a replacement for DB/API limits.

Executor
- Manages task execution.
- Prefer bounded queue and explicit rejection.

CompletableFuture
- Async composition.
- Beware executor choice and context propagation.

Java Memory Model
- Think visibility, atomicity, ordering.
```
