```text id="o7ftrm"
- happens-before matters.

synchronized / Lock
- Protect critical sections and invariants.

volatile
- Visibility flag/reference.
- Not enough for count++.

Atomic
- Simple lock-free state.
- Use LongAdder for high-contention counter.

Concurrent Collections
- Use ConcurrentHashMap, BlockingQueue, CopyOnWriteArrayList where appropriate.

Context
- Request id, user, tenant, transaction, security, MDC.
- Does not automatically move across threads.
- ThreadLocal must be cleared.
- ScopedValue is the modern scoped immutable context model.

Production Mindset
- Bound everything.
- Timeout everything.
- Propagate context deliberately.
- Observe async failures.
- Debug with thread dump and JFR.
```

Kesimpulan praktisnya: **Java concurrency yang aman bukan sekadar membuat kode berjalan async. Yang penting adalah ownership state, resource bound, timeout, cancellation, context propagation, dan observability.** Virtual threads membuat model blocking jauh lebih scalable, tetapi correctness tetap ditentukan oleh transaction boundary, shared state, downstream capacity, dan context discipline.

---

**Sources:**

- [Virtual Threads - Java](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/core/virtual-threads.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [JEP 444: Virtual Threads](https://openjdk.org/jeps/444?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/12/2026, 8:28:53 PM

Saya akan fokus ke pattern, bukan teori ulang: kapan dipakai, bentuk kode, failure mode, dan trade-off production untuk Java 17+ sampai Java modern.

Maksudnya **Java concurrency pattern**: pola-pola praktis untuk mengatur thread, async task, parallel work, shared state, context propagation, timeout, dan resource protection.

Catatan versi: untuk Java 17, mayoritas pattern masih berbasis `ExecutorService`, `CompletableFuture`, `BlockingQueue`, `Lock`, atomic classes, dan concurrent collections. Sejak Java 21, **virtual threads** sudah final dan mengubah cara kita mendesain blocking I/O concurrency; sedangkan **structured concurrency** masih berstatus preview pada JEP 505, dan **ScopedValue** diperkenalkan untuk context immutable yang lebih aman daripada `ThreadLocal` dalam model modern Java. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/444?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# Java 17+ Cheat Sheet: Concurrency Patterns

---

# 1. Pattern Selection Matrix

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Banyak request blocking I/O | Thread-per-task, virtual thread |
| CPU-heavy batch | Fixed pool, ForkJoin, parallel stream hati-hati |
| Batasi downstream API/DB | Semaphore bulkhead |
| Banyak producer dan consumer | BlockingQueue producer-consumer |
| Async beberapa service lalu combine | Fan-out/fan-in |
| Async pipeline bertahap | CompletableFuture chain |
| Context hilang saat pindah thread | Context propagation wrapper |
| Logging correlation ID | MDC propagation |
| Shared counter | Atomic / LongAdder |
| Shared map | ConcurrentHashMap |
| Shared mutable object | Lock / synchronized / confinement |
| Banyak read sedikit write | ReadWriteLock / CopyOnWrite |
| Single ordered mutation | Actor / mailbox / single writer |
| Periodic task | ScheduledExecutorService |
| Task harus timeout/cancel | Timeout + interruption pattern |
| Async task tidak boleh hidup liar | Structured concurrency mindset |
| Transaksi DB + async side effect | Transactional outbox |
| Hindari duplicate concurrent call | In-flight deduplication |
| Retry aman | Idempotent retry pattern |

---

# 2. Core Principle

```text id="e1v5zw"
Concurrency pattern yang benar bukan sekadar membuat task async.

Yang harus dikendalikan:
1. ownership state
2. thread lifecycle
3. resource limit
4. timeout
5. cancellation
6. context propagation
7. failure visibility
8. transaction boundary
9. ordering guarantee
10. backpressure
```

---

# 3. Thread-per-Task Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan saat setiap task relatif independen dan lebih mudah dipahami sebagai satu jalur eksekusi.

Contoh:

```text id="31ys2u"
1 request = 1 task
1 external call = 1 task
1 file processing = 1 task
1 workflow delegate = 1 task
```

## Java 17 Platform Thread Version

```java id="xny1nl"
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(50);

Future<Result> future = executor.submit(() -> {
    return service.process(command);
});

Result result = future.get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

## Java 21+ Virtual Thread Version

```java id="31u0fq"
try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
    Future<Result> future = executor.submit(() -> {
        return service.process(command);
    });

    Result result = future.get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
}
```

Virtual threads cocok untuk blocking I/O high-concurrency karena ringan dan dikelola JVM; tetapi tetap perlu membatasi resource seperti DB pool, HTTP pool, API rate limit, dan lock contention. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/444?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Jangan Lakukan

```java id="hca6us"
for (Command command : commands) {
    new Thread(() -> service.process(command)).start();
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="7zezfa"
thread lifecycle tidak jelas
jumlah thread tidak dibatasi
shutdown susah
error bisa hilang
observability buruk
```

---

# 4. Fixed Worker Pool Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan untuk workload yang harus dibatasi jumlah parallel worker-nya.

Cocok untuk:

```text id="swi1en"
CPU-bound processing
batch job
limited worker background task
bounded parallelism
```

## Pattern

```java id="e5c5lt"
int workers = Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors();

ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(workers);

List<Future<Result>> futures = new ArrayList<>();

for (Job job : jobs) {
    futures.add(executor.submit(() -> process(job)));
}

for (Future<Result> future : futures) {
    Result result = future.get();
}
```

## Rule

```text id="4n0j3a"
CPU-bound:
thread count ≈ CPU cores

I/O-bound:
thread count boleh lebih besar, tetapi harus dibatasi oleh downstream capacity
```

---

# 5. Bounded Executor Pattern

## Problem

`Executors.newFixedThreadPool()` terlihat bounded, tapi queue internalnya bisa sangat besar. Pada production system, ini bisa menyebabkan memory naik sebelum sistem menolak load.

## Pattern

```java id="z1vpyk"
ExecutorService executor = new ThreadPoolExecutor(
    8,
    16,
    60,
    TimeUnit.SECONDS,
    new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000),
    namedThreadFactory("order-worker"),
    new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy()
);
```

Helper:

```java id="c8f6pv"
public static ThreadFactory namedThreadFactory(String prefix) {
    AtomicInteger sequence = new AtomicInteger();

    return runnable -> {
        Thread thread = new Thread(runnable);
        thread.setName(prefix + "-" + sequence.incrementAndGet());
        thread.setUncaughtExceptionHandler((t, e) -> {
            System.err.println("Uncaught error in " + t.getName() + ": " + e);
        });
        return thread;
    };
}
```

## Rejection Policy

| Policy | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `AbortPolicy` | Throw `RejectedExecutionException` |
| `CallerRunsPolicy` | Caller menjalankan task |
| `DiscardPolicy` | Buang task diam-diam |
| `DiscardOldestPolicy` | Buang task paling lama |

## Production Rule

```text id="4n4pou"
Prefer bounded queue.
Avoid silent discard unless data loss is explicitly acceptable.
Define overload behavior intentionally.
```

---

# 6. Backpressure Pattern

## Problem

Tanpa backpressure:

```text id="llgr0y"
request masuk terus
task queue membesar
memory naik
latency naik
timeout naik
service collapse
```

## Pattern

```java id="tl3va1"
public final class BoundedTaskSubmitter {
    private final ExecutorService executor;

    public BoundedTaskSubmitter(ExecutorService executor) {
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    public boolean submit(Runnable task) {
        try {
            executor.submit(task);
            return true;
        } catch (RejectedExecutionException e) {
            return false;
        }
    }
}
```

API layer:

```java id="loiqq0"
boolean accepted = submitter.submit(() -> process(command));

if (!accepted) {
    throw new ServiceUnavailableException("System is overloaded");
}
```

## HTTP Mapping

| Situation | Response |
|---|---|
| Queue penuh | `503 Service Unavailable` |
| Rate limit | `429 Too Many Requests` |
| Request timeout | `504 Gateway Timeout` |
| Downstream timeout | `502` / `504`, tergantung boundary |

---

# 7. Producer-Consumer Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan saat task diproduksi oleh satu komponen dan diproses oleh worker lain.

Contoh:

```text id="q1w0k2"
email sending
audit event processing
background job
file processing
notification dispatch
internal queue
```

## Pattern with BlockingQueue

```java id="9w0aq3"
BlockingQueue<Job> queue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000);
```

Producer:

```java id="il6jaa"
public void submit(Job job) throws InterruptedException {
    queue.put(job); // waits if full
}
```

Consumer:

```java id="cdvr7q"
public final class JobWorker implements Runnable {
    private final BlockingQueue<Job> queue;

    public JobWorker(BlockingQueue<Job> queue) {
        this.queue = queue;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
            try {
                Job job = queue.take();
                process(job);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Job processing failed", e);
            }
        }
    }

    private void process(Job job) {
        // process job
    }
}
```

Start workers:

```java id="q7q36t"
ExecutorService workers = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4);

for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    workers.submit(new JobWorker(queue));
}
```

## Key Invariant

```text id="gucpk9"
Queue capacity = memory protection.
Worker count = processing capacity.
put/offer policy = backpressure behavior.
```

---

# 8. Fan-Out / Fan-In Pattern

## Problem

Satu request perlu mengambil data dari beberapa sumber secara concurrent.

```text id="u9nco4"
load order
load customer
load payment
load entitlement
combine into response
```

## CompletableFuture Version

```java id="o0x0bd"
CompletableFuture<Order> orderFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> orderClient.load(orderId), executor);

CompletableFuture<Customer> customerFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> customerClient.load(customerId), executor);

CompletableFuture<Payment> paymentFuture =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> paymentClient.load(orderId), executor);

CompletableFuture<OrderView> result =
    orderFuture.thenCombine(customerFuture, (order, customer) -> {
        return new PartialView(order, customer);
    }).thenCombine(paymentFuture, (partial, payment) -> {
        return new OrderView(partial.order(), partial.customer(), payment);
    });

OrderView view = result.orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS).join();
```

## Simpler with Futures

```java id="h8x5ko"
Future<Order> orderFuture = executor.submit(() -> orderClient.load(orderId));
Future<Customer> customerFuture = executor.submit(() -> customerClient.load(customerId));

Order order = orderFuture.get(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
Customer customer = customerFuture.get(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

return new OrderView(order, customer);
```

## Failure Questions

```text id="eog673"
Kalau customer gagal, apakah order tetap dikembalikan?
Kalau payment timeout, apakah response partial boleh?
Kalau request cancelled, apakah semua child task ikut cancel?
Kalau salah satu gagal cepat, apakah task lain harus dihentikan?
```

---

# 9. Scatter-Gather Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Mirip fan-out/fan-in, tapi jumlah target dinamis.

Contoh:

```text id="f6l34x"
query beberapa provider
call beberapa downstream service
broadcast validation ke beberapa rule engine
ambil harga dari beberapa supplier
```

## Pattern

```java id="xw94xs"
public List<Response> scatterGather(
    List<Request> requests,
    Duration timeout
) throws InterruptedException {

    try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
        List<Future<Response>> futures = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Request request : requests) {
            futures.add(executor.submit(() -> callProvider(request)));
        }

        List<Response> responses = new ArrayList<>();
        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + timeout.toNanos();

        for (Future<Response> future : futures) {
            long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();

            if (remaining <= 0) {
                future.cancel(true);
                continue;
            }

            try {
                responses.add(future.get(remaining, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS));
            } catch (ExecutionException e) {
                log.warn("Provider call failed", e);
            } catch (TimeoutException e) {
                future.cancel(true);
            }
        }

        return responses;
    }
}
```

## Rule

```text id="8jwc3r"
Scatter-gather harus punya global deadline.
Jangan beri timeout per provider saja tanpa total request deadline.
```

---

# 10. Bulkhead Pattern

## Problem

Satu downstream lambat bisa menghabiskan semua thread/resource.

```text id="fe1e2d"
Payment API lambat
semua request thread menunggu payment
order API ikut lambat
seluruh service collapse
```

## Semaphore Bulkhead

```java id="to2dqn"
public final class DownstreamBulkhead {
    private final Semaphore permits;

    public DownstreamBulkhead(int maxConcurrentCalls) {
        this.permits = new Semaphore(maxConcurrentCalls);
    }

    public <T> T execute(Callable<T> task) {
        boolean acquired = false;

        try {
            acquired = permits.tryAcquire(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);

            if (!acquired) {
                throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Downstream bulkhead full");
            }

            return task.call();
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted while waiting for bulkhead", e);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new RuntimeException(e);
        } finally {
            if (acquired) {
                permits.release();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="k7ks0g"
Payment payment = paymentBulkhead.execute(() -> paymentClient.load(orderId));
```

## Good For

```text id="j0j93x"
DB protection
external API protection
expensive CPU operation
file processing limit
per-tenant concurrency cap
```

---

# 11. Rate Limiting Pattern

## Simple Semaphore Is Not Rate Limit

Semaphore membatasi concurrent call, bukan call per second.

| Pattern | Membatasi |
|---|---|
| Semaphore | concurrent in-flight |
| Rate limiter | request per time window |
| Queue | backlog |
| Timeout | waiting duration |

## Simple Token Bucket Sketch

```java id="wa67gz"
public final class SimpleRateLimiter {
    private final int maxTokens;
    private final AtomicInteger tokens;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler;

    public SimpleRateLimiter(int maxTokens, Duration refillInterval) {
        this.maxTokens = maxTokens;
        this.tokens = new AtomicInteger(maxTokens);
        this.scheduler = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor();

        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(
            () -> tokens.set(maxTokens),
            refillInterval.toMillis(),
            refillInterval.toMillis(),
            TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS
        );
    }

    public boolean tryAcquire() {
        while (true) {
            int current = tokens.get();

            if (current <= 0) {
                return false;
            }

            if (tokens.compareAndSet(current, current - 1)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="o3l1rr"
if (!rateLimiter.tryAcquire()) {
    throw new TooManyRequestsException();
}

externalApi.call();
```

For production, prefer proven libraries or gateway-level rate limiting.

---

# 12. Timeout Pattern

## Rule

```text id="03l3rg"
Setiap blocking boundary harus punya timeout.
```

## Future Timeout

```java id="yawuz5"
Future<Result> future = executor.submit(() -> service.call());

try {
    return future.get(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
} catch (TimeoutException e) {
    future.cancel(true);
    throw new RequestTimeoutException(e);
}
```

## CompletableFuture Timeout

```java id="1ksqs5"
CompletableFuture<Result> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> service.call(), executor)
        .orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

Fallback:

```java id="yglx4i"
CompletableFuture<Result> future =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> service.call(), executor)
        .completeOnTimeout(Result.fallback(), 3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

## Lock Timeout

```java id="sby2rr"
if (lock.tryLock(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
    try {
        updateState();
    } finally {
        lock.unlock();
    }
} else {
    throw new TimeoutException("Could not acquire lock");
}
```

---

# 13. Cancellation Pattern

## Correct Interruption Handling

```java id="n61ioe"
try {
    queue.take();
} catch (InterruptedException e) {
    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
    return;
}
```

## Cancellable Worker

```java id="d0eqth"
public final class CancellableWorker implements Runnable {
    private final BlockingQueue<Job> queue;

    public CancellableWorker(BlockingQueue<Job> queue) {
        this.queue = queue;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
            try {
                Job job = queue.poll(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

                if (job != null) {
                    process(job);
                }
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
    }

    private void process(Job job) {
        // periodically check interruption if long-running
        if (Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
            return;
        }

        // work
    }
}
```

## Anti-Pattern

```java id="qx7lit"
catch (InterruptedException e) {
    // ignore
}
```

This breaks cancellation.

---

# 14. Retry Pattern

## Kapan Aman?

Retry aman hanya jika operation:

```text id="t167yv"
idempotent
atau punya idempotency key
atau failure terjadi sebelum side effect
atau side effect bisa dideduplicate
```

## Simple Retry

```java id="f4xy20"
public <T> T retry(
    Callable<T> operation,
    int maxAttempts,
    Duration delay
) {
    RuntimeException lastError = null;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
        try {
            return operation.call();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError = new RuntimeException(e);

            if (attempt == maxAttempts) {
                throw lastError;
            }

            sleep(delay);
        }
    }

    throw lastError;
}

private void sleep(Duration delay) {
    try {
        Thread.sleep(delay);
    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted during retry delay", e);
    }
}
```

## Better with Backoff

```java id="d2i2vd"
Duration delay = Duration.ofMillis(100);

for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
    try {
        return operation.call();
    } catch (Exception e) {
        if (attempt == maxAttempts) {
            throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }

        sleep(delay);
        delay = delay.multipliedBy(2);
    }
}
```

## Warning

```text id="pcx5r8"
Retry tanpa timeout memperpanjang outage.
Retry tanpa idempotency membuat duplicate side effect.
Retry tanpa jitter bisa menciptakan retry storm.
```

---

# 15. Circuit Breaker Pattern

## Problem

Downstream sedang rusak, tapi service terus memanggilnya.

## State

```text id="z1a489"
CLOSED     -> normal
OPEN       -> fail fast
HALF_OPEN  -> test recovery
```

## Simple Sketch

```java id="jfd1t3"
public enum CircuitState {
    CLOSED,
    OPEN,
    HALF_OPEN
}
```

```java id="xtguer"
public final class SimpleCircuitBreaker {
    private final int failureThreshold;
    private final Duration openDuration;

    private final AtomicInteger failures = new AtomicInteger();
    private final AtomicReference<CircuitState> state =
        new AtomicReference<>(CircuitState.CLOSED);

    private volatile Instant openedAt;

    public SimpleCircuitBreaker(int failureThreshold, Duration openDuration) {
        this.failureThreshold = failureThreshold;
        this.openDuration = openDuration;
    }

    public <T> T execute(Callable<T> operation) {
        if (!allowRequest()) {
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Circuit is open");
        }

        try {
            T result = operation.call();
            onSuccess();
            return result;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            onFailure();
            throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }
    }

    private boolean allowRequest() {
        CircuitState current = state.get();

        if (current == CircuitState.CLOSED) {
            return true;
        }

        if (current == CircuitState.OPEN &&
            openedAt.plus(openDuration).isBefore(Instant.now())) {
            return state.compareAndSet(CircuitState.OPEN, CircuitState.HALF_OPEN);
        }

        return current == CircuitState.HALF_OPEN;
    }

    private void onSuccess() {
        failures.set(0);
        state.set(CircuitState.CLOSED);
    }

    private void onFailure() {
        int count = failures.incrementAndGet();

        if (count >= failureThreshold) {
            openedAt = Instant.now();
            state.set(CircuitState.OPEN);
        }
    }
}
```

Production biasanya pakai library resilience seperti Resilience4j, tapi memahami pattern-nya penting.

---

# 16. Atomic Counter Pattern

## Simple Counter

```java id="jtywte"
AtomicInteger counter = new AtomicInteger();

int value = counter.incrementAndGet();
```

## High-Contention Counter

```java id="xd5uh1"
LongAdder adder = new LongAdder();

adder.increment();
adder.add(10);

long value = adder.sum();
```

## Decision

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Exact atomic read/update | `AtomicInteger`, `AtomicLong` |
| High-throughput metric counter | `LongAdder` |
| Multi-field invariant | Lock, not atomic primitive |

---

# 17. CAS State Machine Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan untuk state transition sederhana dan thread-safe.

```java id="meityr"
public enum JobStatus {
    NEW,
    RUNNING,
    COMPLETED,
    FAILED,
    CANCELLED
}
```

```java id="jxtwdp"
public final class JobControl {
    private final AtomicReference<JobStatus> status =
        new AtomicReference<>(JobStatus.NEW);

    public boolean start() {
        return status.compareAndSet(JobStatus.NEW, JobStatus.RUNNING);
    }

    public boolean complete() {
        return status.compareAndSet(JobStatus.RUNNING, JobStatus.COMPLETED);
    }

    public boolean cancel() {
        while (true) {
            JobStatus current = status.get();

            if (current == JobStatus.COMPLETED || current == JobStatus.FAILED) {
                return false;
            }

            if (status.compareAndSet(current, JobStatus.CANCELLED)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }

    public JobStatus status() {
        return status.get();
    }
}
```

## Jangan Pakai CAS Jika

```text id="73jqop"
transition melibatkan banyak field
butuh DB transaction
butuh audit/event side effect
butuh complex invariant
```

Untuk itu gunakan lock atau transaction.

---

# 18. Lock Guard Pattern

## Basic

```java id="kpaijm"
private final Lock lock = new ReentrantLock();

public void update() {
    lock.lock();
    try {
        mutateState();
    } finally {
        lock.unlock();
    }
}
```

## Dengan Timeout

```java id="xfzrwl"
public void updateWithTimeout() {
    boolean acquired = false;

    try {
        acquired = lock.tryLock(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        if (!acquired) {
            throw new TimeoutException("Could not acquire lock");
        }

        mutateState();
    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new RuntimeException(e);
    } finally {
        if (acquired) {
            lock.unlock();
        }
    }
}
```

## Rule

```text id="b5q5d8"
Lock protects invariants, not variables.
Keep critical section small.
Never call slow external API while holding lock unless intentionally designed.
```

---

# 19. Read-Write Lock Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan saat:

```text id="zcvb8a"
read jauh lebih sering daripada write
read operation cukup mahal
state harus konsisten
contention nyata
```

## Pattern

```java id="x5g9cc"
public final class ConfigStore {
    private final ReadWriteLock lock = new ReentrantReadWriteLock();
    private Map<String, String> config = new HashMap<>();

    public String get(String key) {
        lock.readLock().lock();
        try {
            return config.get(key);
        } finally {
            lock.readLock().unlock();
        }
    }

    public void replaceAll(Map<String, String> newConfig) {
        lock.writeLock().lock();
        try {
            config = new HashMap<>(newConfig);
        } finally {
            lock.writeLock().unlock();
        }
    }
}
```

## Warning

```text id="kgkot3"
ReadWriteLock tidak otomatis lebih cepat.
Jika critical section kecil, synchronized/ReentrantLock mungkin lebih sederhana.
```

---

# 20. Copy-On-Write Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan untuk collection yang:

```text id="x6ov5n"
sangat sering dibaca
jarang diubah
ukuran tidak terlalu besar
butuh iterasi aman
```

## Pattern

```java id="ukg7pn"
CopyOnWriteArrayList<Listener> listeners = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

public void addListener(Listener listener) {
    listeners.add(listener);
}

public void notifyListeners(Event event) {
    for (Listener listener : listeners) {
        listener.onEvent(event);
    }
}
```

## Cocok Untuk

```text id="r41o2g"
event listeners
plugin hooks
configuration subscribers
read-mostly list
```

## Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text id="fh0rw2"
frequent writes
large list
high mutation rate
```

---

# 21. ConcurrentHashMap Compute Pattern

## Problem

Check-then-act pada map biasa tidak atomic.

Buruk:

```java id="vd6xe9"
if (!map.containsKey(key)) {
    map.put(key, new Counter());
}
map.get(key).increment();
```

## Pattern

```java id="xh9g76"
ConcurrentHashMap<String, LongAdder> counters = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

public void increment(String name) {
    counters.computeIfAbsent(name, key -> new LongAdder())
            .increment();
}
```

## Cache Example

```java id="e4cm50"
ConcurrentHashMap<String, Value> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

public Value get(String key) {
    return cache.computeIfAbsent(key, this::loadValue);
}
```

## Warning

```text id="fn3h27"
Function dalam computeIfAbsent sebaiknya tidak terlalu lambat.
Hindari side effect kompleks.
Hati-hati recursive update ke map yang sama.
```

---

# 22. In-Flight Deduplication Pattern

## Problem

Banyak request bersamaan meminta data sama, lalu semua call downstream.

```text id="rrcz99"
100 request for same customerId
100 downstream calls
padahal cukup 1 call
```

## Pattern

```java id="dxebcf"
public final class InFlightDeduplicator<K, V> {
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<K, CompletableFuture<V>> inFlight =
        new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private final Executor executor;

    public InFlightDeduplicator(Executor executor) {
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    public CompletableFuture<V> getOrStart(K key, Supplier<V> supplier) {
        return inFlight.computeIfAbsent(key, k ->
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(supplier, executor)
                .whenComplete((result, error) -> inFlight.remove(k))
        );
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="a6rn0w"
CompletableFuture<Customer> future =
    deduplicator.getOrStart(customerId, () -> customerClient.load(customerId));
```

## Cocok Untuk

```text id="mo7ijr"
cache miss protection
token refresh
profile lookup
configuration fetch
expensive external call
```

---

# 23. Single Writer Pattern

## Problem

Banyak thread mengubah state yang sama.

## Pattern

Semua mutation dikirim ke satu worker/thread.

```java id="icls8b"
public final class SingleWriterStore {
    private final BlockingQueue<Command> queue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>();
    private final Map<String, String> state = new HashMap<>();

    public void submit(Command command) throws InterruptedException {
        queue.put(command);
    }

    public void run() {
        while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
            try {
                Command command = queue.take();
                apply(command);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
    }

    private void apply(Command command) {
        switch (command) {
            case PutCommand put -> state.put(put.key(), put.value());
            case RemoveCommand remove -> state.remove(remove.key());
        }
    }
}
```

## Benefit

```text id="yylltk"
no lock for internal state
ordered mutation
easier reasoning
actor-like behavior
```

## Trade-off

```text id="lfk6ep"
single writer bisa jadi bottleneck
perlu queue monitoring
perlu backpressure
```

---

# 24. Actor / Mailbox Pattern

## Mental Model

```text id="6mdh2w"
Actor owns its state.
Other components do not mutate actor state directly.
They send messages to actor mailbox.
Actor processes messages sequentially.
```

## Sketch

```java id="6x30ms"
public sealed interface AccountMessage
    permits Deposit, Withdraw, GetBalance {
}

public record Deposit(BigDecimal amount) implements AccountMessage {}
public record Withdraw(BigDecimal amount) implements AccountMessage {}
public record GetBalance(CompletableFuture<BigDecimal> replyTo)
    implements AccountMessage {}
```

```java id="bb3go3"
public final class AccountActor implements Runnable {
    private final BlockingQueue<AccountMessage> mailbox =
        new LinkedBlockingQueue<>();

    private BigDecimal balance = BigDecimal.ZERO;

    public void send(AccountMessage message) {
        mailbox.offer(message);
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
            try {
                AccountMessage message = mailbox.take();
                handle(message);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
    }

    private void handle(AccountMessage message) {
        switch (message) {
            case Deposit deposit ->
                balance = balance.add(deposit.amount());

            case Withdraw withdraw ->
                balance = balance.subtract(withdraw.amount());

            case GetBalance getBalance ->
                getBalance.replyTo().complete(balance);
        }
    }
}
```

## Cocok Untuk

```text id="vqv8ld"
stateful component
ordered event processing
workflow state transition
per-aggregate command handling
```

---

# 25. Pipeline Pattern

## Problem

Task melewati beberapa tahap.

```text id="oeqssr"
read input
parse
validate
transform
persist
notify
```

## BlockingQueue Pipeline

```java id="kjtqjq"
BlockingQueue<RawMessage> rawQueue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000);
BlockingQueue<ParsedMessage> parsedQueue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000);
BlockingQueue<ValidatedMessage> validatedQueue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(1_000);
```

Stage 1:

```java id="e8vxzu"
executor.submit(() -> {
    while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
        RawMessage raw = rawQueue.take();
        parsedQueue.put(parse(raw));
    }
});
```

Stage 2:

```java id="tk0pgb"
executor.submit(() -> {
    while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
        ParsedMessage parsed = parsedQueue.take();
        validatedQueue.put(validate(parsed));
    }
});
```

Stage 3:

```java id="1kiph5"
executor.submit(() -> {
    while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
        ValidatedMessage validated = validatedQueue.take();
        persist(validated);
    }
});
```

## Key Design

| Concern | Decision |
|---|---|
| Queue size per stage | Backpressure |
| Worker count per stage | Throughput |
| Error handling | Dead letter / retry / skip |
| Ordering | Per-key or global |
| Shutdown | Poison pill / interruption |

---

# 26. Poison Pill Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Untuk memberi sinyal worker agar berhenti melalui queue.

```java id="ug42zq"
public sealed interface Job permits RealJob, StopJob {}

public record RealJob(String payload) implements Job {}

public enum StopJob implements Job {
    INSTANCE
}
```

Consumer:

```java id="lqgjsn"
while (true) {
    Job job = queue.take();

    if (job == StopJob.INSTANCE) {
        break;
    }

    process((RealJob) job);
}
```

Shutdown:

```java id="34w1ic"
for (int i = 0; i < workerCount; i++) {
    queue.put(StopJob.INSTANCE);
}
```

## Warning

```text id="7gouz1"
Jumlah poison pill harus sesuai jumlah consumer.
Untuk blocking operation lain, interruption sering lebih fleksibel.
```

---

# 27. Scheduled Task Pattern

## Pattern

```java id="lkr8uu"
ScheduledExecutorService scheduler =
    Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(2);

scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(
    () -> {
        try {
            runJob();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Scheduled job failed", e);
        }
    },
    0,
    1,
    TimeUnit.MINUTES
);
```

## `scheduleAtFixedRate` vs `scheduleWithFixedDelay`

| Method | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `scheduleAtFixedRate` | Coba jalan tiap interval tetap |
| `scheduleWithFixedDelay` | Delay dihitung setelah run sebelumnya selesai |

Fixed delay:

```java id="d14tu6"
scheduler.scheduleWithFixedDelay(
    this::safeRunJob,
    0,
    1,
    TimeUnit.MINUTES
);
```

## Rule

```text id="cr74fv"
Scheduled task harus catch exception.
Jika exception lolos, schedule bisa berhenti diam-diam.
```

---

# 28. Fork-Join Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Untuk CPU-bound recursive divide-and-conquer.

Contoh:

```text id="y8gluf"
large array sum
tree traversal
parallel search
large CPU computation
```

## Pattern

```java id="q45bi4"
public final class SumTask extends RecursiveTask<Long> {
    private static final int THRESHOLD = 10_000;

    private final long[] values;
    private final int start;
    private final int end;

    public SumTask(long[] values, int start, int end) {
        this.values = values;
        this.start = start;
        this.end = end;
    }

    @Override
    protected Long compute() {
        if (end - start <= THRESHOLD) {
            long sum = 0;
            for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
                sum += values[i];
            }
            return sum;
        }

        int mid = (start + end) / 2;

        SumTask left = new SumTask(values, start, mid);
        SumTask right = new SumTask(values, mid, end);

        left.fork();

        long rightResult = right.compute();
        long leftResult = left.join();

        return leftResult + rightResult;
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="obonqv"
ForkJoinPool pool = new ForkJoinPool();

long result = pool.invoke(new SumTask(values, 0, values.length));
```

## Rule

```text id="2uqd9t"
ForkJoin cocok untuk CPU-bound.
Jangan isi dengan blocking DB/API calls.
```

---

# 29. Parallel Stream Pattern

## Good Use

```java id="o6iqum"
List<Result> results = items.parallelStream()
    .map(this::cpuHeavyTransform)
    .toList();
```

## Bad Use

```java id="85fxdn"
List<Customer> customers = orders.parallelStream()
    .map(order -> customerClient.load(order.customerId()))
    .toList();
```

Masalah:

```text id="9l9swc"
uncontrolled concurrency
common ForkJoinPool contention
DB/API overload
context propagation issue
debugging sulit
```

## Rule

```text id="sze4z9"
Use parallelStream only for CPU-bound, stateless, side-effect-free processing after measurement.
```

---

# 30. CompletableFuture Pipeline Pattern

## Sequential Async Pipeline

```java id="tfakcc"
CompletableFuture<OrderResult> result =
    CompletableFuture
        .supplyAsync(() -> loadOrder(orderId), executor)
        .thenApply(this::validateOrder)
        .thenCompose(order -> reserveInventory(order))
        .thenCompose(reservation -> chargePayment(reservation))
        .thenApply(this::buildResult)
        .orTimeout(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

## Error Recovery

```java id="wnuwgq"
CompletableFuture<OrderResult> result =
    CompletableFuture
        .supplyAsync(() -> process(order), executor)
        .exceptionally(error -> {
            log.error("Order processing failed", error);
            return OrderResult.failed();
        });
```

## Rule

| Method | Use |
|---|---|
| `thenApply` | transform value |
| `thenCompose` | async call returning future |
| `thenCombine` | combine two futures |
| `allOf` | wait all |
| `anyOf` | first completed |
| `exceptionally` | fallback |
| `handle` | result/error mapping |
| `whenComplete` | side effect/logging |
| `orTimeout` | timeout failure |
| `completeOnTimeout` | timeout fallback |

---

# 31. CompletableFuture allOf Pattern

## Pattern

```java id="p0csq4"
List<CompletableFuture<Result>> futures = commands.stream()
    .map(command ->
        CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> process(command), executor)
    )
    .toList();

CompletableFuture<Void> all =
    CompletableFuture.allOf(futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0]));

List<Result> results = all.thenApply(ignored ->
    futures.stream()
        .map(CompletableFuture::join)
        .toList()
).join();
```

## With Individual Error Handling

```java id="sfp635"
List<CompletableFuture<Result>> futures = commands.stream()
    .map(command ->
        CompletableFuture
            .supplyAsync(() -> process(command), executor)
            .exceptionally(error -> Result.failed(command.id(), error))
    )
    .toList();
```

---

# 32. First Success Pattern

## Problem

Call multiple providers; use first successful response.

```java id="emrq7r"
public CompletableFuture<Quote> firstSuccess(List<Supplier<Quote>> providers) {
    CompletableFuture<Quote> result = new CompletableFuture<>();
    AtomicInteger remaining = new AtomicInteger(providers.size());

    for (Supplier<Quote> provider : providers) {
        CompletableFuture
            .supplyAsync(provider, executor)
            .whenComplete((quote, error) -> {
                if (error == null) {
                    result.complete(quote);
                } else if (remaining.decrementAndGet() == 0) {
                    result.completeExceptionally(error);
                }
            });
    }

    return result;
}
```

## Rule

```text id="793od5"
Setelah first success, cancel outstanding tasks if possible.
Pastikan provider call punya timeout.
```

---

# 33. Context Propagation Pattern

## Problem

Context seperti `requestId`, `tenantId`, `userId`, dan MDC sering hilang saat pindah thread.

## Explicit Context

```java id="9a17qs"
public record ExecutionContext(
    String correlationId,
    String userId,
    String tenantId,
    Locale locale,
    ZoneId zoneId
) {}
```

Pass explicitly:

```java id="b4cyk6"
service.process(context, command);
```

Ini paling jelas dan paling aman.

---

# 34. ThreadLocal Context Pattern

## Holder

```java id="1npmyf"
public final class RequestContextHolder {
    private static final ThreadLocal<ExecutionContext> CURRENT =
        new ThreadLocal<>();

    private RequestContextHolder() {}

    public static void set(ExecutionContext context) {
        CURRENT.set(context);
    }

    public static ExecutionContext get() {
        return CURRENT.get();
    }

    public static void clear() {
        CURRENT.remove();
    }
}
```

## Use Safely

```java id="d7a8b2"
RequestContextHolder.set(context);

try {
    service.process(command);
} finally {
    RequestContextHolder.clear();
}
```

## Anti-Pattern

```java id="d9n448"
RequestContextHolder.set(context);
service.process(command);
// no clear
```

Risiko:

```text id="u7vlw1"
context leak
wrong user
wrong tenant
wrong correlation id
security bug
```

---

# 35. MDC Propagation Pattern

## Capture and Restore

```java id="x0ndxp"
public static Runnable withMdc(Runnable task) {
    Map<String, String> captured = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

    return () -> {
        Map<String, String> previous = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

        try {
            if (captured != null) {
                MDC.setContextMap(captured);
            } else {
                MDC.clear();
            }

            task.run();
        } finally {
            if (previous != null) {
                MDC.setContextMap(previous);
            } else {
                MDC.clear();
            }
        }
    };
}
```

Use:

```java id="cwx8v4"
executor.submit(withMdc(() -> service.process(command)));
```

## Pattern Rule

```text id="f6v2cf"
Capture context at submit time.
Restore context at execution time.
Clear/restore previous context in finally.
```

---

# 36. Context-Aware Executor Pattern

```java id="wf1x7p"
public final class ContextAwareExecutor implements Executor {
    private final Executor delegate;

    public ContextAwareExecutor(Executor delegate) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void execute(Runnable command) {
        ExecutionContext capturedContext = RequestContextHolder.get();
        Map<String, String> capturedMdc = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

        delegate.execute(() -> {
            ExecutionContext previousContext = RequestContextHolder.get();
            Map<String, String> previousMdc = MDC.getCopyOfContextMap();

            try {
                RequestContextHolder.set(capturedContext);

                if (capturedMdc != null) {
                    MDC.setContextMap(capturedMdc);
                } else {
                    MDC.clear();
                }

                command.run();
            } finally {
                if (previousContext != null) {
                    RequestContextHolder.set(previousContext);
                } else {
                    RequestContextHolder.clear();
                }

                if (previousMdc != null) {
                    MDC.setContextMap(previousMdc);
                } else {
                    MDC.clear();
                }
            }
        });
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="zgye6y"
Executor contextAwareExecutor =
    new ContextAwareExecutor(Executors.newFixedThreadPool(10));
```

---

# 37. Scoped Context Pattern

`ScopedValue` adalah model modern untuk membagikan immutable context dalam scope tertentu, lebih mudah dikontrol daripada `ThreadLocal`, terutama bersama virtual threads dan structured concurrency. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/506?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Conceptual pattern:

```java id="98fwxb"
public final class ContextScope {
    public static final ScopedValue<ExecutionContext> CONTEXT =
        ScopedValue.newInstance();

    private ContextScope() {}
}
```

Use:

```java id="s3s2x5"
ScopedValue
    .where(ContextScope.CONTEXT, context)
    .run(() -> service.process(command));
```

Read:

```java id="fgyq0n"
ExecutionContext context = ContextScope.CONTEXT.get();
```

## Benefit

```text id="m7iwwm"
scope-bound
immutable
harder to leak
better fit for virtual threads
better than mutable ThreadLocal for request context
```

---

# 38. Structured Concurrency Pattern

Structured concurrency memperlakukan beberapa task terkait sebagai satu unit kerja: parent task mengatur lifecycle child tasks, failure, cancellation, dan observability. OpenJDK masih menandai structured concurrency sebagai preview dalam JEP 505. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/505?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Mindset

```text id="7zcoc0"
Child tasks should not outlive parent.
Failure should be propagated.
Cancellation should be coordinated.
Related concurrent work should be observable as one unit.
```

## Why It Matters

Manual future pattern:

```java id="av98ud"
Future<User> userFuture = executor.submit(() -> loadUser(userId));
Future<Order> orderFuture = executor.submit(() -> loadOrder(orderId));

User user = userFuture.get();
Order order = orderFuture.get();
```

Questions:

```text id="cmllbu"
Kalau loadUser gagal, apakah loadOrder harus dicancel?
Kalau request timeout, siapa cancel child task?
Kalau parent selesai, apakah child masih hidup?
```

## Java 17-Compatible Mindset

```java id="wwv04p"
List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>();

try {
    futures.add(executor.submit(() -> loadUser(userId)));
    futures.add(executor.submit(() -> loadOrder(orderId)));

    // get results with deadline
} catch (Exception e) {
    for (Future<?> future : futures) {
        future.cancel(true);
    }
    throw e;
}
```

---

# 39. Transactional Outbox Pattern

## Problem

Dalam transaction DB, kita ingin publish message/event.

Buruk:

```java id="txdzqy"
@Transactional
public void submitOrder(OrderId orderId) {
    orderRepository.submit(orderId);
    kafkaProducer.send("order-submitted", event); // side effect outside DB tx
}
```

Jika DB commit sukses tapi Kafka gagal, data inconsistent. Jika Kafka sukses tapi DB rollback, event palsu terkirim.

## Pattern

Dalam transaction:

```java id="lqxts4"
@Transactional
public void submitOrder(OrderId orderId) {
    Order order = orderRepository.load(orderId);

    order.submit();

    orderRepository.save(order);

    outboxRepository.insert(new OutboxEvent(
        UUID.randomUUID(),
        "ORDER_SUBMITTED",
        order.id().toString(),
        serialize(order)
    ));
}
```

Background publisher:

```java id="pf09mo"
public void publishOutbox() {
    List<OutboxEvent> events = outboxRepository.findPending(100);

    for (OutboxEvent event : events) {
        try {
            messagePublisher.publish(event);
            outboxRepository.markPublished(event.id());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            outboxRepository.markFailedAttempt(event.id(), e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}
```

## Rule

```text id="wcg6vd"
DB transaction writes business state and outbox event atomically.
Async publisher handles delivery later.
Consumer must be idempotent.
```

---

# 40. Async Boundary Pattern

## Problem

Async inside transaction is often wrong.

Buruk:

```java id="hvm5be"
@Transactional
public void process(Order order) {
    orderRepository.save(order);

    CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
        auditRepository.save(...);
    });
}
```

Risiko:

```text id="yskwzs"
async task tidak ikut transaction
EntityManager tidak thread-safe
lazy loading error
commit/rollback order tidak jelas
audit bisa tertulis walau transaction rollback
```

## Better Pattern

Option 1: outbox.

```java id="86mb4d"
@Transactional
public void process(Order order) {
    orderRepository.save(order);
    outboxRepository.insert(AuditEvent.from(order));
}
```

Option 2: explicit new transaction in async handler.

```java id="kilimr"
executor.submit(() -> {
    auditService.saveAuditInNewTransaction(auditCommand);
});
```

But only after deciding consistency semantics.

---

# 41. Idempotent Consumer Pattern

## Problem

Async delivery can duplicate messages.

```text id="v0eon5"
producer retry
consumer crash after processing before ack
broker redelivery
network timeout
```

## Pattern

```java id="gg2lcc"
@Transactional
public void consume(Message message) {
    if (processedMessageRepository.exists(message.id())) {
        return;
    }

    applyBusinessEffect(message);

    processedMessageRepository.insert(message.id());
}
```

## DB Constraint

```sql id="ecjvt8"
CREATE TABLE processed_message (
    message_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL
);
```

## Rule

```text id="lzfl3k"
At-least-once delivery requires idempotent consumer.
```

---

# 42. Double-Checked Locking Pattern

## Kapan Dipakai

Lazy initialization expensive singleton-ish object.

## Correct Version Requires `volatile`

```java id="fckfay"
public final class LazyClientHolder {
    private volatile Client client;

    public Client getClient() {
        Client local = client;

        if (local == null) {
            synchronized (this) {
                local = client;

                if (local == null) {
                    local = new Client();
                    client = local;
                }
            }
        }

        return local;
    }
}
```

## Better Simpler Alternative

Initialization-on-demand holder:

```java id="um9olp"
public final class ClientHolder {
    private ClientHolder() {}

    private static class Holder {
        private static final Client INSTANCE = new Client();
    }

    public static Client get() {
        return Holder.INSTANCE;
    }
}
```

## Rule

```text id="leytfp"
Prefer simple eager initialization or holder idiom.
Use double-checked locking only when you really need instance-level lazy init.
```

---

# 43. Guarded Suspension Pattern

## Low-Level wait/notify

```java id="w6me2f"
public final class GuardedQueue<T> {
    private final Queue<T> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();

    public synchronized void put(T value) {
        queue.add(value);
        notifyAll();
    }

    public synchronized T take() throws InterruptedException {
        while (queue.isEmpty()) {
            wait();
        }

        return queue.remove();
    }
}
```

## Important

Use `while`, not `if`.

```java id="e0iiz5"
while (conditionNotMet) {
    wait();
}
```

## Prefer Higher-Level API

```java id="6y5m47"
BlockingQueue<T> queue = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(100);
```

Rule:

```text id="eujq8p"
Use wait/notify only when building concurrency primitives.
For application code, prefer BlockingQueue, Lock, Condition, Semaphore, Executor.
```

---

# 44. Thread Confinement Pattern

## Meaning

State is only accessed by one thread.

```text id="cdvkax"
No sharing = no race condition.
```

## Example

```java id="8o4c2e"
public Result processBatch(List<Input> inputs) {
    Map<String, Integer> localCounts = new HashMap<>();

    for (Input input : inputs) {
        localCounts.merge(input.key(), 1, Integer::sum);
    }

    return new Result(localCounts);
}
```

`localCounts` is safe because it does not escape the method/thread.

## Rule

```text id="szn9n7"
Prefer local variables.
Avoid unnecessary shared mutable fields.
```

---

# 45. Immutable Snapshot Pattern

## Problem

Readers need consistent state while writer occasionally updates it.

## Pattern

```java id="ysvu31"
public final class RoutingTableHolder {
    private volatile RoutingTable current =
        new RoutingTable(Map.of());

    public RoutingTable current() {
        return current;
    }

    public void replace(RoutingTable newTable) {
        this.current = newTable;
    }
}
```

Immutable object:

```java id="ellqrp"
public record RoutingTable(Map<String, String> routes) {
    public RoutingTable {
        routes = Map.copyOf(routes);
    }
}
```

## Benefit

```text id="sw8ft3"
read tanpa lock
writer publish snapshot baru
reader selalu melihat object konsisten
```

Good for config, routing, feature flag snapshot, reference data.

---

# 46. Per-Key Lock Pattern

## Problem

Butuh serialize operation untuk key tertentu, tapi tidak ingin global lock.

```text id="7b4ybc"
same orderId must be sequential
different orderId can run concurrently
```

## Pattern

```java id="f7ig4q"
public final class KeyedLock<K> {
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<K, ReentrantLock> locks =
        new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public void withLock(K key, Runnable action) {
        ReentrantLock lock = locks.computeIfAbsent(key, ignored -> new ReentrantLock());

        lock.lock();
        try {
            action.run();
        } finally {
            lock.unlock();

            if (!lock.hasQueuedThreads()) {
                locks.remove(key, lock);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="h6dz20"
keyedLock.withLock(orderId, () -> orderService.process(orderId));
```

## Warning

```text id="8y6yg5"
Cleanup lock map carefully.
For cluster-wide locking, local lock is not enough.
```

---

# 47. Sharded Executor Pattern

## Problem

Need per-key ordering but also parallelism across keys.

## Pattern

```java id="y0m6xf"
public final class ShardedExecutor<K> {
    private final ExecutorService[] shards;

    public ShardedExecutor(int shardCount) {
        this.shards = new ExecutorService[shardCount];

        for (int i = 0; i < shardCount; i++) {
            shards[i] = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(
                namedThreadFactory("shard-" + i)
            );
        }
    }

    public void submit(K key, Runnable task) {
        int shard = Math.floorMod(key.hashCode(), shards.length);
        shards[shard].submit(task);
    }
}
```

## Guarantees

```text id="cptv8a"
Same key -> same shard -> ordered execution
Different keys -> possibly different shards -> parallel execution
```

Good for order/case/customer aggregate processing.

---

# 48. Debounce / Coalescing Pattern

## Problem

Many rapid events for same key should be collapsed.

```text id="fdw3vc"
config refresh
search indexing
UI notification
recalculate summary
```

## Simple Coalescing Queue

```java id="dp70fl"
public final class CoalescingQueue<K> {
    private final Set<K> pending = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private final BlockingQueue<K> queue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>();

    public void submit(K key) {
        if (pending.add(key)) {
            queue.offer(key);
        }
    }

    public K take() throws InterruptedException {
        K key = queue.take();
        pending.remove(key);
        return key;
    }
}
```

## Effect

```text id="lob3mi"
100 duplicate key events while pending -> 1 queued job
```

---

# 49. Cache Aside + Async Refresh Pattern

## Basic Cache Aside

```java id="7do632"
public Value get(String key) {
    Value cached = cache.get(key);

    if (cached != null) {
        return cached;
    }

    Value loaded = repository.load(key);
    cache.put(key, loaded);

    return loaded;
}
```

## Async Refresh

```java id="4kk3ac"
public Value get(String key) {
    CacheEntry entry = cache.get(key);

    if (entry == null) {
        return loadAndCache(key);
    }

    if (entry.isStale()) {
        executor.submit(() -> refresh(key));
    }

    return entry.value();
}
```

## Need Protection

Combine with in-flight deduplication to avoid cache stampede.

---

# 50. Context + Transaction + Async Rule

This is one of the most important enterprise rules:

```text id="y6dv9h"
Request context can be propagated.
Logging context can be propagated.
Security context must be propagated carefully.
Transaction context should usually NOT be propagated casually.
Persistence context should NOT be shared across threads.
Workflow/business context must be persisted explicitly.
```

---

# 51. Pattern Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya |
|---|---|
| `new Thread()` sembarangan | Lifecycle liar |
| Unbounded queue | Memory blow-up |
| Async tanpa timeout | Hanging task |
| Retry tanpa idempotency | Duplicate side effect |
| `parallelStream()` untuk DB/API | Downstream overload |
| Lock sambil call external API | Contention dan deadlock risk |
| ThreadLocal tanpa clear | Context leak |
| Swallow async exception | Silent failure |
| Scheduled task tanpa catch | Job bisa mati diam-diam |
| Fire-and-forget dalam transaction | Inconsistent side effect |
| Virtual thread tanpa bulkhead | DB/API tetap bisa jebol |
| Shared mutable HashMap | Race/corruption |
| Atomic untuk multi-field invariant | Consistency bug |
| Local lock untuk distributed guarantee | Salah scope |
| Infinite retry | Retry storm |

---

# 52. Production Checklist

```text id="o32jx3"
Executor
[ ] Pool bounded?
[ ] Queue bounded?
[ ] Thread name jelas?
[ ] Rejection policy jelas?
[ ] Shutdown jelas?

Timeout
[ ] Future get timeout?
[ ] HTTP timeout?
[ ] DB query timeout?
[ ] Lock wait timeout?
[ ] Queue offer/poll timeout?

Cancellation
[ ] InterruptedException ditangani benar?
[ ] Future.cancel dipakai saat timeout?
[ ] Long-running task cek interrupt?

Context
[ ] correlationId propagated?
[ ] MDC cleared?
[ ] ThreadLocal removed?
[ ] security context explicit?
[ ] tenant context safe?

Resource
[ ] DB pool tidak dioverrun?
[ ] downstream API protected by bulkhead/rate limit?
[ ] queue monitored?
[ ] backpressure defined?

State
[ ] shared mutable state minimized?
[ ] immutable snapshot used where possible?
[ ] lock protects invariant?
[ ] atomic only for simple state?
[ ] per-key ordering if needed?

Failure
[ ] async exception visible?
[ ] retry bounded?
[ ] idempotency key exists?
[ ] outbox used for transactional side effects?
[ ] dead-letter strategy exists?

Observability
[ ] thread name meaningful?
[ ] queue size metric?
[ ] active worker metric?
[ ] rejection count metric?
[ ] timeout count metric?
[ ] bulkhead saturation metric?
```

---

# 53. Practical Pattern Decision Guide

```text id="k7xql5"
Need to process many independent blocking calls?
-> virtual thread + semaphore bulkhead + timeout

Need CPU-heavy processing?
-> fixed pool sized around CPU cores / ForkJoin

Need ordered per-entity processing?
-> sharded executor or actor/mailbox

Need safe shared counter?
-> LongAdder for metrics, AtomicLong for exact CAS logic

Need shared map?
-> ConcurrentHashMap + compute/merge

Need multiple downstream calls in one request?
-> fan-out/fan-in with deadline

Need async side effect after DB write?
-> transactional outbox

Need propagate request ID?
-> explicit context or MDC propagation wrapper

Need prevent duplicate concurrent lookup?
-> in-flight deduplication

Need avoid context leak?
-> try/finally clear ThreadLocal/MDC

Need handle overload?
-> bounded queue + rejection + backpressure

Need parent-child task lifecycle?
-> structured concurrency mindset/API where available
```

---

# 54. Most Important Summary

```text id="tbk7po"
Concurrency patterns are mostly about control.

Control state:
- immutable object
- confinement
- lock
- atomic
- concurrent collection

Control work:
- executor
- queue
- worker pool
- virtual thread
- structured concurrency

Control resource:
- bounded queue
- semaphore
- rate limiter
- bulkhead
- timeout

Control meaning:
- explicit context
- MDC propagation
- scoped value
- security/tenant isolation

Control failure:
- cancellation
- retry with idempotency
- circuit breaker
- transactional outbox
- dead-letter handling
```

Kesimpulan praktisnya: **pattern terbaik bukan yang paling canggih, tetapi yang membuat ownership, ordering, resource limit, context, dan failure behavior paling eksplisit.** Untuk Java 17, kuasai `ExecutorService`, `CompletableFuture`, `BlockingQueue`, `Lock`, `Atomic`, dan `ConcurrentHashMap`. Untuk Java 21+, tambahkan virtual threads sebagai default kuat untuk blocking I/O, tetapi tetap pasang bulkhead, timeout, dan context discipline.

---

**Sources:**

- [JEP 444: Virtual Threads](https://openjdk.org/jeps/444?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/12/2026, 8:36:50 PM

Saya akan buat sebagai lanjutan dari collection sebelumnya: bukan sekadar `List/Set/Map`, tapi pola struktur data untuk lookup, indexing, hierarchy, graph, cache, ordering, aggregation, dan domain modelling enterprise.

Berikut **cheat sheet Java Data Structure Pattern** untuk Java 17+ dengan fokus enterprise backend: bagaimana memilih dan membentuk struktur data agar lookup, ordering, grouping, deduplication, state transition, hierarchy, cache, dan workflow lebih aman serta efisien.

# Java Data Structure Pattern Cheat Sheet

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text
Data Structure Pattern
= pola menyusun data agar operasi tertentu menjadi jelas, efisien, dan aman.

Pertanyaan utama:
1. Data dicari berdasarkan apa?
2. Apakah urutan penting?
3. Apakah duplicate boleh?
4. Apakah data berubah?
5. Apakah butuh lookup cepat?
6. Apakah butuh grouping?
7. Apakah relasi one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-many?
8. Apakah ada hierarchy atau graph?
9. Apakah perlu thread-safe?
10. Apakah data ini domain state, cache, index, atau temporary processing?
```

---

# 2. Pattern Selection Matrix

| Problem | Pattern | Java Structure |
|---|---|---|
| Ordered sequence | Sequence Pattern | `List<T>` |
| Unique values | Set Pattern | `Set<T>` |
| Lookup by ID | Index Map Pattern | `Map<ID, Entity>` |
| Group by category | Grouping Pattern | `Map<K, List<V>>` |
| One key to many values | MultiMap Pattern | `Map<K, List<V>>` / `Map<K, Set<V>>` |
| Count frequency | Frequency Map Pattern | `Map<T, Long>` / `LongAdder` |
| Preserve insertion order | Ordered Map/Set Pattern | `LinkedHashMap`, `LinkedHashSet` |
| Sorted access | Sorted Structure Pattern | `TreeMap`, `TreeSet`, `PriorityQueue` |
| Parent-child tree | Tree Pattern | node references / `Map<ParentId, List<Node>>` |
| Many-to-many relation | Bi-directional Index Pattern | two maps |
| Fast membership | Membership Pattern | `HashSet<T>` |
| Avoid duplicate in-flight call | In-flight Map Pattern | `Map<K, CompletableFuture<V>>` |
| Cache with eviction | LRU Cache Pattern | `LinkedHashMap` |
| State transition | Transition Table Pattern | `Map<State, Set<State>>` |
| Composite lookup | Composite Key Pattern | `record Key(a,b)` |
| Ordered task processing | Queue Pattern | `Queue`, `Deque`, `BlockingQueue` |
| Priority processing | Priority Queue Pattern | `PriorityQueue<T>` |
| Flags | Bit Set Pattern | `EnumSet`, `BitSet`, bitmask |
| Snapshot config | Immutable Snapshot Pattern | immutable record + volatile reference |
| Thread-safe lookup | Concurrent Map Pattern | `ConcurrentHashMap` |
| Per-key serialization | Keyed Queue / Sharded Map | `Map<K, Queue<T>>` |
| Graph traversal | Graph Pattern | adjacency list |
| Prefix search | Trie Pattern | custom tree map |
| Interval search | Range Pattern | `TreeMap`, custom interval model |

---

# 3. Sequence Pattern

## Use Case

Gunakan saat urutan data penting.

```java
List<Order> orders = new ArrayList<>();
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
ordered result
pagination result
audit log display
processing sequence
API response list
```

## Pattern

```java
List<OrderSummary> summaries = orders.stream()
    .map(order -> new OrderSummary(
        order.id(),
        order.status(),
        order.createdAt()
    ))
    .toList();
```

## Rule

```text
List is for order.
Do not use List for repeated lookup by ID.
```

Buruk:

```java
Order found = orders.stream()
    .filter(order -> order.id().equals(orderId))
    .findFirst()
    .orElseThrow();
```

Jika lookup dilakukan berulang, ubah ke index map.

---

# 4. Index Map Pattern

## Problem

Sering mencari entity berdasarkan ID.

## Pattern

```java
Map<OrderId, Order> ordersById = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        Order::id,
        Function.identity()
    ));
```

Use:

```java
Order order = ordersById.get(orderId);
```

## Benefit

| Approach | Complexity |
|---|---:|
| Scan `List` | O(n) per lookup |
| `Map` lookup | O(1) average |

## Enterprise Example

```java
Map<CustomerId, Customer> customersById = customers.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        Customer::id,
        Function.identity()
    ));

List<OrderView> views = orders.stream()
    .map(order -> {
        Customer customer = customersById.get(order.customerId());

        return new OrderView(
            order.id(),
            customer.name(),
            order.totalAmount()
        );
    })
    .toList();
```

## Pitfall

Duplicate key akan error:

```java
Collectors.toMap(Order::id, Function.identity());
```

Handle duplicate:

```java
Map<OrderId, Order> ordersById = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        Order::id,
        Function.identity(),
        (existing, replacement) -> existing
    ));
```

---

# 5. Strongly Typed Key Pattern

## Problem

Raw `UUID` atau `String` mudah tertukar.

Buruk:

```java
void loadOrder(UUID id) {}
void loadCustomer(UUID id) {}
```

Mudah salah:

```java
loadOrder(customerId);
```

## Pattern

```java
public record OrderId(UUID value) {}

public record CustomerId(UUID value) {}
```

Use:

```java
void loadOrder(OrderId orderId) {}

void loadCustomer(CustomerId customerId) {}
```

## Benefit

```text
compile-time safety
domain clarity
fewer accidental ID mixups
better API intent
```

---

# 6. Composite Key Pattern

## Problem

Lookup butuh lebih dari satu field.

Contoh:

```text
tenantId + orderId
country + productCode
customerId + subscriptionId
module + action
```

## Pattern

```java
public record TenantOrderKey(
    TenantId tenantId,
    OrderId orderId
) {}
```

Use:

```java
Map<TenantOrderKey, Order> orders = new HashMap<>();

orders.put(new TenantOrderKey(tenantId, orderId), order);

Order found = orders.get(new TenantOrderKey(tenantId, orderId));
```

## Jangan Pakai String Concatenation

Buruk:

```java
String key = tenantId + ":" + orderId;
```

Masalah:

```text
delimiter collision
harder refactor
no type safety
parsing needed
```

Lebih baik:

```java
record TenantOrderKey(TenantId tenantId, OrderId orderId) {}
```

---

# 7. Grouping Pattern

## Problem

Data perlu dikelompokkan berdasarkan kategori/status/owner.

## Pattern

```java
Map<OrderStatus, List<Order>> ordersByStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Order::status));
```

Count per group:

```java
Map<OrderStatus, Long> countByStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
        Order::status,
        Collectors.counting()
    ));
```

Group by owner:

```java
Map<UserId, List<Task>> tasksByAssignee = tasks.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Task::assigneeId));
```

## Use Case

```text
orders by status
tasks by assignee
cases by officer
events by aggregateId
products by category
errors by type
```

---

# 8. MultiMap Pattern

Java standard library tidak punya `Multimap` built-in seperti Guava, tapi bisa dibuat dengan `Map<K, List<V>>` atau `Map<K, Set<V>>`.

## One-to-Many dengan List

```java
Map<CustomerId, List<Order>> ordersByCustomer = new HashMap<>();

ordersByCustomer
    .computeIfAbsent(customerId, id -> new ArrayList<>())
    .add(order);
```

## One-to-Many dengan Set

```java
Map<RoleId, Set<Permission>> permissionsByRole = new HashMap<>();

permissionsByRole
    .computeIfAbsent(roleId, id -> new HashSet<>())
    .add(permission);
```

## Decision

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Preserve duplicates | `Map<K, List<V>>` |
| Prevent duplicates | `Map<K, Set<V>>` |
| Preserve insertion order | `Map<K, LinkedHashSet<V>>` |
| Sorted values | `Map<K, TreeSet<V>>` |

---

# 9. Frequency Map Pattern

## Problem

Menghitung kemunculan value.

## Simple Pattern

```java
Map<String, Long> frequency = words.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
        Function.identity(),
        Collectors.counting()
    ));
```

## Mutable Pattern

```java
Map<String, Integer> counts = new HashMap<>();

for (String word : words) {
    counts.merge(word, 1, Integer::sum);
}
```

## Concurrent High-Throughput Pattern

```java
ConcurrentHashMap<String, LongAdder> counters = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

public void increment(String key) {
    counters.computeIfAbsent(key, ignored -> new LongAdder())
            .increment();
}
```

Snapshot:

```java
Map<String, Long> snapshot = counters.entrySet().stream()
    .collect(Collectors.toMap(
        Map.Entry::getKey,
        entry -> entry.getValue().sum()
    ));
```

## Use Case

```text
metric counters
error count by type
event frequency
word count
request count by endpoint
status distribution
```

---

# 10. Membership Pattern

## Problem

Perlu cek apakah value termasuk dalam kumpulan.

Buruk:

```java
List<String> allowedStatuses = List.of("A", "B", "C");

if (allowedStatuses.contains(status)) {
    // O(n)
}
```

Baik:

```java
Set<String> allowedStatuses = Set.of("A", "B", "C");

if (allowedStatuses.contains(status)) {
    // O(1) average
}
```

## EnumSet untuk Enum

```java
Set<OrderStatus> terminalStatuses = EnumSet.of(
    OrderStatus.APPROVED,
    OrderStatus.REJECTED,
    OrderStatus.CANCELLED
);
```

Use:

```java
boolean terminal = terminalStatuses.contains(order.status());
```

## Rule

```text
Use Set for membership.
Use EnumSet for enum membership.
```

---

# 11. Ordered Deduplication Pattern

## Problem

Butuh remove duplicate tapi tetap menjaga urutan pertama.

## Pattern

```java
List<String> uniqueEmails = new ArrayList<>(
    new LinkedHashSet<>(emails)
);
```

Dengan stream:

```java
List<String> uniqueEmails = emails.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.collectingAndThen(
        Collectors.toCollection(LinkedHashSet::new),
        ArrayList::new
    ));
```

## Use Case

```text
API response unique but stable order
input normalization
ordered role list
ordered tags
```

---

# 12. Sorted Structure Pattern

## TreeSet

```java
Set<Order> sortedOrders = new TreeSet<>(
    Comparator.comparing(Order::createdAt)
);
```

## TreeMap

```java
TreeMap<Instant, Order> ordersByTime = new TreeMap<>();

ordersByTime.put(order.createdAt(), order);
```

Range query:

```java
NavigableMap<Instant, Order> recent =
    ordersByTime.tailMap(cutoffInstant, true);
```

## Use Case

```text
range lookup
time-based lookup
floor/ceiling query
ordered navigation
```

Example:

```java
Map.Entry<Instant, Order> latestBefore =
    ordersByTime.floorEntry(targetTime);
```

## Decision

| Need | Structure |
|---|---|
| Sort once for output | `stream().sorted()` |
| Maintain sorted data over time | `TreeMap` / `TreeSet` |
| Need min/max repeatedly | `PriorityQueue` |
| Need range query | `TreeMap` |

---

# 13. Priority Queue Pattern

## Problem

Selalu proses item dengan priority tertinggi/terendah.

## Pattern

```java
PriorityQueue<Job> queue = new PriorityQueue<>(
    Comparator.comparing(Job::priority)
);
```

Add:

```java
queue.offer(job);
```

Poll highest priority depending comparator:

```java
Job next = queue.poll();
```

## Higher Priority First

```java
PriorityQueue<Job> queue = new PriorityQueue<>(
    Comparator.comparing(Job::priority).reversed()
);
```

## Use Case

```text
retry job by nextAttemptAt
task priority
deadline scheduling
top-N processing
```

## Warning

`PriorityQueue` is not thread-safe. For concurrent use:

```java
PriorityBlockingQueue<Job> queue = new PriorityBlockingQueue<>();
```

---

# 14. Top-N Pattern

## Problem

Cari N item terbesar/terbaik tanpa sorting seluruh data.

## Pattern with Min Heap

```java
public static List<Order> topNByAmount(List<Order> orders, int n) {
    PriorityQueue<Order> heap = new PriorityQueue<>(
        Comparator.comparing(Order::totalAmount)
    );

    for (Order order : orders) {
        heap.offer(order);

        if (heap.size() > n) {
            heap.poll();
        }
    }

    List<Order> result = new ArrayList<>(heap);

    result.sort(Comparator.comparing(Order::totalAmount).reversed());

    return result;
}
```

## Complexity

| Approach | Complexity |
|---|---:|
| Sort all | O(n log n) |
| Heap top-N | O(n log k) |

Use when `n` is large and `k` is small.

---

# 15. Queue Pattern

## FIFO Queue

```java
Queue<Job> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();

queue.offer(job);

Job next = queue.poll();
```

## Deque as Stack

```java
Deque<String> stack = new ArrayDeque<>();

stack.push("A");
stack.push("B");

String top = stack.pop();
```

## Use Case

| Structure | Use |
|---|---|
| `ArrayDeque` | local queue/stack |
| `BlockingQueue` | producer-consumer |
| `PriorityQueue` | priority scheduling |
| `ConcurrentLinkedQueue` | non-blocking concurrent queue |

---

# 16. Ring Buffer Pattern

## Problem

Butuh fixed-size circular buffer.

Use case:

```text
recent events
last N errors
sliding window
small in-memory audit
```

## Pattern

```java
public final class RingBuffer<T> {
    private final Object[] elements;
    private int nextIndex;
    private int size;

    public RingBuffer(int capacity) {
        this.elements = new Object[capacity];
    }

    public void add(T value) {
        elements[nextIndex] = value;
        nextIndex = (nextIndex + 1) % elements.length;

        if (size < elements.length) {
            size++;
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public List<T> snapshot() {
        List<T> result = new ArrayList<>(size);

        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
            int index = (nextIndex - size + i + elements.length) % elements.length;
            result.add((T) elements[index]);
        }

        return result;
    }
}
```

## Warning

This version is not thread-safe. Add lock if shared across threads.

---

# 17. LRU Cache Pattern

## Problem

Cache dengan eviction berdasarkan least recently used.

## Pattern with LinkedHashMap

```java
public final class LruCache<K, V> extends LinkedHashMap<K, V> {
    private final int maxSize;

    public LruCache(int maxSize) {
        super(16, 0.75f, true);
        this.maxSize = maxSize;
    }

    @Override
    protected boolean removeEldestEntry(Map.Entry<K, V> eldest) {
        return size() > maxSize;
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
Map<String, UserProfile> cache = new LruCache<>(1_000);

cache.put(userId, profile);

UserProfile profile = cache.get(userId);
```

## Thread-Safe Wrapper

```java
Map<String, UserProfile> cache =
    Collections.synchronizedMap(new LruCache<>(1_000));
```

For serious production caching, prefer Caffeine/Redis, but this pattern is useful to understand.

---

# 18. Cache Aside Pattern

## Problem

Load from cache if exists, otherwise load from source.

## Pattern

```java
public Value get(String key) {
    Value cached = cache.get(key);

    if (cached != null) {
        return cached;
    }

    Value loaded = repository.load(key);

    cache.put(key, loaded);

    return loaded;
}
```

## Concurrent Safer Version

```java
ConcurrentHashMap<String, Value> cache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

public Value get(String key) {
    return cache.computeIfAbsent(key, repository::load);
}
```

## Warning

`computeIfAbsent` loader should not be too slow or recursively update same map.

---

# 19. In-Flight Deduplication Pattern

## Problem

Multiple threads request same key simultaneously; all trigger same expensive operation.

## Pattern

```java
public final class InFlightDeduplicator<K, V> {
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<K, CompletableFuture<V>> inFlight =
        new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private final Executor executor;

    public InFlightDeduplicator(Executor executor) {
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    public CompletableFuture<V> getOrStart(K key, Supplier<V> loader) {
        return inFlight.computeIfAbsent(key, ignored ->
            CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(loader, executor)
                .whenComplete((value, error) -> inFlight.remove(key))
        );
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
CompletableFuture<Customer> future =
    deduplicator.getOrStart(customerId, () -> customerClient.load(customerId));
```

## Use Case

```text
token refresh
cache miss protection
profile lookup
metadata loading
external API deduplication
```

---

# 20. Immutable Snapshot Pattern

## Problem

Banyak reader butuh data konsisten, writer jarang update.

## Pattern

```java
public record RoutingTable(Map<String, String> routes) {
    public RoutingTable {
        routes = Map.copyOf(routes);
    }
}
```

Holder:

```java
public final class RoutingTableHolder {
    private volatile RoutingTable current = new RoutingTable(Map.of());

    public RoutingTable current() {
        return current;
    }

    public void replace(Map<String, String> newRoutes) {
        current = new RoutingTable(newRoutes);
    }
}
```

## Benefit

```text
read without lock
safe publication via volatile
immutable state
reader sees consistent snapshot
```

## Use Case

```text
configuration
feature flags
routing table
reference data
permission matrix
country/currency mapping
```

---

# 21. Copy-on-Write List Pattern

## Problem

List sering dibaca, jarang diubah.

## Pattern

```java
CopyOnWriteArrayList<Listener> listeners = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

public void addListener(Listener listener) {
    listeners.add(listener);
}

public void notifyListeners(Event event) {
    for (Listener listener : listeners) {
        listener.onEvent(event);
    }
}
```

## Cocok Untuk

```text
listeners
plugins
subscribers
read-mostly collections
```

## Tidak Cocok Untuk

```text
frequent writes
large list
high mutation rate
```

---

# 22. Bidirectional Index Pattern

## Problem

Butuh lookup dua arah.

Contoh:

```text
user -> roles
role -> users
order -> invoice
invoice -> order
```

## Pattern

```java
public final class UserRoleIndex {
    private final Map<UserId, Set<RoleId>> rolesByUser = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<RoleId, Set<UserId>> usersByRole = new HashMap<>();

    public void add(UserId userId, RoleId roleId) {
        rolesByUser
            .computeIfAbsent(userId, ignored -> new HashSet<>())
            .add(roleId);

        usersByRole
            .computeIfAbsent(roleId, ignored -> new HashSet<>())
            .add(userId);
    }

    public Set<RoleId> rolesOf(UserId userId) {
        return rolesByUser.getOrDefault(userId, Set.of());
    }

    public Set<UserId> usersIn(RoleId roleId) {
        return usersByRole.getOrDefault(roleId, Set.of());
    }
}
```

## Invariant

```text
Both indexes must be updated together.
```

If this represents persistent business data, the DB should own the authoritative relation. In-memory index is usually a read model/cache.

---

# 23. Parent-Child Tree Pattern

## Problem

Flat data has parent ID and needs hierarchy.

```java
public record Category(
    CategoryId id,
    CategoryId parentId,
    String name
) {}
```

## Build Children Index

```java
Map<CategoryId, List<Category>> childrenByParent = categories.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Category::parentId));
```

Find root:

```java
List<Category> roots = childrenByParent.getOrDefault(null, List.of());
```

## Recursive Tree Node

```java
public record CategoryNode(
    Category category,
    List<CategoryNode> children
) {}
```

Build:

```java
public CategoryNode buildTree(
    Category category,
    Map<CategoryId, List<Category>> childrenByParent
) {
    List<CategoryNode> children = childrenByParent
        .getOrDefault(category.id(), List.of())
        .stream()
        .map(child -> buildTree(child, childrenByParent))
        .toList();

    return new CategoryNode(category, children);
}
```

## Use Case

```text
product catalog category
organization hierarchy
menu tree
case folder tree
document hierarchy
```

## Warning

Check for cycles. Parent-child data can be corrupt.

---

# 24. Tree Traversal Pattern

## Depth-First Search

```java
public void dfs(CategoryNode node, Consumer<CategoryNode> visitor) {
    visitor.accept(node);

    for (CategoryNode child : node.children()) {
        dfs(child, visitor);
    }
}
```

## Breadth-First Search

```java
public void bfs(CategoryNode root, Consumer<CategoryNode> visitor) {
    Queue<CategoryNode> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();

    queue.offer(root);

    while (!queue.isEmpty()) {
        CategoryNode node = queue.poll();

        visitor.accept(node);

        for (CategoryNode child : node.children()) {
            queue.offer(child);
        }
    }
}
```

## Decision

| Need | Traversal |
|---|---|
| Process parent before descendants | DFS preorder |
| Process leaves first | DFS postorder |
| Level-by-level | BFS |
| Find shortest unweighted path | BFS |

---

# 25. Graph Adjacency List Pattern

## Problem

Data has arbitrary relations, not strict tree.

Examples:

```text
workflow transitions
dependency graph
service dependency
permission inheritance
case relation
order dependency
BPMN-like nodes
```

## Pattern

```java
Map<NodeId, List<NodeId>> adjacency = new HashMap<>();

adjacency
    .computeIfAbsent(from, ignored -> new ArrayList<>())
    .add(to);
```

## DFS

```java
public void dfs(
    NodeId start,
    Map<NodeId, List<NodeId>> graph,
    Set<NodeId> visited
) {
```
