# 33. NIO SocketChannel

Non-blocking capable socket channel.

```java
try (SocketChannel channel = SocketChannel.open()) {
    channel.connect(new InetSocketAddress("example.com", 80));

    ByteBuffer request = ByteBuffer.wrap(
        "GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: example.com\r\n\r\n"
            .getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
    );

    while (request.hasRemaining()) {
        channel.write(request);
    }

    ByteBuffer response = ByteBuffer.allocate(8192);

    int n = channel.read(response);

    response.flip();

    byte[] bytes = new byte[response.remaining()];
    response.get(bytes);

    System.out.println(new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
}
```

---

# 34. Non-Blocking SocketChannel

```java
SocketChannel channel = SocketChannel.open();
channel.configureBlocking(false);

channel.connect(new InetSocketAddress("example.com", 80));

while (!channel.finishConnect()) {
    // do other work
}
```

Read:

```java
ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(1024);

int n = channel.read(buffer);

if (n > 0) {
    buffer.flip();
    // process
} else if (n == 0) {
    // no data currently
} else {
    // end of stream
}
```

---

# 35. Selector Pattern

Selector lets one thread monitor many channels.

```java
Selector selector = Selector.open();

ServerSocketChannel server = ServerSocketChannel.open();
server.bind(new InetSocketAddress(8080));
server.configureBlocking(false);

server.register(selector, SelectionKey.OP_ACCEPT);

while (true) {
    selector.select();

    Iterator<SelectionKey> keys = selector.selectedKeys().iterator();

    while (keys.hasNext()) {
        SelectionKey key = keys.next();
        keys.remove();

        if (key.isAcceptable()) {
            ServerSocketChannel serverChannel = (ServerSocketChannel) key.channel();
            SocketChannel client = serverChannel.accept();

            client.configureBlocking(false);
            client.register(selector, SelectionKey.OP_READ);
        }

        if (key.isReadable()) {
            SocketChannel client = (SocketChannel) key.channel();

            ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(1024);

            int n = client.read(buffer);

            if (n == -1) {
                client.close();
            } else if (n > 0) {
                buffer.flip();
                client.write(buffer); // echo
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Use NIO selector when:

```text
many connections
few threads
custom network server
event-loop style architecture
```

But for normal enterprise backend, use proven frameworks unless you are building infrastructure.

---

# 36. Blocking vs Non-Blocking I/O

| Model | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| Blocking socket per thread | simple, readable | many platform threads expensive |
| Blocking + virtual threads | simple + scalable for I/O | Java 21+, still need resource limits |
| Non-blocking NIO selector | scalable, few threads | complex state machines |
| Async framework | scalable, production-ready | framework complexity |

Rule:

```text
For business services, prefer HTTP/framework clients.
For custom protocols, prefer blocking + virtual threads if Java 21+.
Use raw NIO selectors only when you need event-loop-level control.
```

---

# 37. TLS / HTTPS

HTTP client HTTPS works by default with JVM trust store:

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://example.com"))
    .GET()
    .build();

HttpResponse<String> response =
    client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
```

Custom SSLContext:

```java
SSLContext sslContext = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS");
sslContext.init(null, null, null);

HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .sslContext(sslContext)
    .build();
```

Rule:

```text
Do not disable certificate validation in production.
Do not trust all certificates.
Do not disable hostname verification.
```

---

# 38. SSLSocket

```java
SSLSocketFactory factory =
    (SSLSocketFactory) SSLSocketFactory.getDefault();

try (SSLSocket socket =
         (SSLSocket) factory.createSocket("example.com", 443)) {

    socket.startHandshake();

    BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(
        new OutputStreamWriter(socket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
    );

    writer.write("GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n");
    writer.write("Host: example.com\r\n");
    writer.write("\r\n");
    writer.flush();
}
```

Use only when you need raw TLS socket. For HTTP, prefer `HttpClient`.

---

# 39. Unix Domain Sockets

Java supports Unix domain sockets via NIO channels.

Server address:

```java
UnixDomainSocketAddress address =
    UnixDomainSocketAddress.of(Path.of("/tmp/app.sock"));
```

Server:

```java
try (ServerSocketChannel server =
         ServerSocketChannel.open(StandardProtocolFamily.UNIX)) {

    server.bind(address);

    try (SocketChannel client = server.accept()) {
        ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(1024);

        int n = client.read(buffer);

        buffer.flip();

        client.write(ByteBuffer.wrap("ok\n".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
    }
}
```

Client:

```java
try (SocketChannel channel =
         SocketChannel.open(StandardProtocolFamily.UNIX)) {

    channel.connect(UnixDomainSocketAddress.of(Path.of("/tmp/app.sock")));

    channel.write(ByteBuffer.wrap("hello\n".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
}
```

Use case:

```text
local IPC
sidecar communication
local daemon protocol
avoid TCP port exposure
better local security boundary than public TCP port
```

Operational notes:

```text
Unix domain sockets are OS-dependent.
Clean up old socket file if needed.
Check filesystem permissions.
```

---

# 40. IPC Overview

IPC means communication between processes.

Common IPC choices:

| IPC Method | Java Support | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| stdin/stdout pipe | `ProcessBuilder` | child process communication |
| loopback TCP | `Socket` / HTTP | local services |
| Unix domain socket | NIO channels | local daemon / sidecar |
| file exchange | `Files` | simple batch handoff |
| file lock | `FileChannel.lock()` | coordination |
| memory-mapped file | `MappedByteBuffer` | shared local data |
| named pipe/FIFO | OS-specific via files | Unix integration |
| message broker | external | distributed IPC |
| database table | JDBC | durable handoff |

---

# 41. ProcessBuilder IPC

Start process:

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("python3", "script.py")
    .redirectErrorStream(true)
    .start();
```

Write to child stdin:

```java
try (BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(
    new OutputStreamWriter(process.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
)) {
    writer.write("hello");
    writer.newLine();
    writer.flush();
}
```

Read child stdout:

```java
try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(
    new InputStreamReader(process.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
)) {
    String line;

    while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
        System.out.println(line);
    }
}
```

Wait:

```java
int exitCode = process.waitFor();
```

---

# 42. Process I/O Deadlock Warning

Problem:

```text
Parent waits for process exit.
Child writes lots of stdout/stderr.
Pipe buffer fills.
Child blocks.
Parent waits forever.
```

Bad:

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("cmd").start();
int exitCode = process.waitFor();
```

Better: consume stdout/stderr.

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("cmd").start();

CompletableFuture<String> stdout =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> readAll(process.getInputStream()));

CompletableFuture<String> stderr =
    CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> readAll(process.getErrorStream()));

boolean finished = process.waitFor(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

if (!finished) {
    process.destroyForcibly();
    throw new TimeoutException("Process timeout");
}

String out = stdout.join();
String err = stderr.join();
```

Helper:

```java
static String readAll(InputStream input) {
    try {
        return new String(input.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
    }
}
```

---

# 43. Redirect Process I/O

To inherit parent console:

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("ls", "-la")
    .inheritIO()
    .start();
```

Redirect to file:

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("command")
    .redirectOutput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.to(Path.of("out.log").toFile()))
    .redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.to(Path.of("err.log").toFile()))
    .start();
```

Merge stderr into stdout:

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("command")
    .redirectErrorStream(true)
    .start();
```

---

# 44. ProcessHandle

Current process:

```java
ProcessHandle current = ProcessHandle.current();

System.out.println(current.pid());
```

Child process:

```java
Process process = new ProcessBuilder("sleep", "10").start();

ProcessHandle handle = process.toHandle();

System.out.println(handle.pid());
System.out.println(handle.isAlive());
```

Destroy:

```java
handle.destroy();
```

Force destroy:

```java
handle.destroyForcibly();
```

---

# 45. Loopback TCP IPC

Use loopback TCP for local service communication.

Server binds to localhost only:

```java
ServerSocket server = new ServerSocket();
server.bind(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 9090));
```

Client:

```java
try (Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", 9090)) {
    // communicate
}
```

Rule:

```text
Bind to 127.0.0.1 or ::1 for local-only IPC.
Do not bind to 0.0.0.0 unless remote access is intended.
```

---

# 46. File-Based IPC

Simple handoff:

```java
Path inbox = Path.of("/app/inbox");
Path file = inbox.resolve("job-123.json");

Files.writeString(file, json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

Better with atomic move:

```java
Path temp = Files.createTempFile(inbox, "job-", ".tmp");
Path finalFile = inbox.resolve("job-123.json");

Files.writeString(temp, json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

Files.move(
    temp,
    finalFile,
    StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE
);
```

Consumer scans:

```java
try (Stream<Path> files = Files.list(inbox)) {
    files
        .filter(path -> path.toString().endsWith(".json"))
        .forEach(this::process);
}
```

Rule:

```text
Producer writes temp file then atomically moves to final filename.
Consumer only processes final filenames.
```

---

# 47. File Lock IPC

```java
try (
    FileChannel channel = FileChannel.open(
        Path.of("app.lock"),
        StandardOpenOption.CREATE,
        StandardOpenOption.WRITE
    );
    FileLock lock = channel.tryLock()
) {
    if (lock == null) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Another process is running");
    }

    runExclusiveJob();
}
```

Warning:

```text
File locks can be OS/filesystem dependent.
Be careful on NFS/network volumes.
```

---

# 48. Memory-Mapped IPC

Two processes can coordinate via file-backed mapped memory, but this is advanced.

```java
try (FileChannel channel = FileChannel.open(
    Path.of("shared.dat"),
    StandardOpenOption.CREATE,
    StandardOpenOption.READ,
    StandardOpenOption.WRITE
)) {
    channel.truncate(1024);

    MappedByteBuffer buffer = channel.map(
        FileChannel.MapMode.READ_WRITE,
        0,
        1024
    );

    buffer.putInt(42);
    buffer.force();
}
```

Use case:

```text
high-performance local IPC
shared status block
low-latency local data sharing
```

Warning:

```text
You must design synchronization yourself.
Memory visibility, locking, file lifecycle, and corruption handling are complex.
```

---

# 49. Named Pipe / FIFO

Java can interact with OS named pipes as files, but creation is OS-specific.

Unix example outside Java:

```bash
mkfifo /tmp/app.pipe
```

Java read:

```java
try (BufferedReader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(
    Path.of("/tmp/app.pipe"),
    StandardCharsets.UTF_8
)) {
    String line = reader.readLine();
}
```

Java write:

```java
try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(
    Path.of("/tmp/app.pipe"),
    StandardCharsets.UTF_8
)) {
    writer.write("hello");
    writer.newLine();
}
```

Warning:

```text
Named pipe behavior is OS-specific.
Open/read/write can block depending on peer behavior.
```

---

# 50. Network Security Checklist

```text
[ ] Are timeouts configured?
[ ] Is TLS certificate validation enabled?
[ ] Is hostname verification enabled?
[ ] Are secrets excluded from logs?
[ ] Are Authorization/Cookie headers masked?
[ ] Are redirects controlled?
[ ] Is SSRF risk considered for user-supplied URLs?
[ ] Is request body size limited?
[ ] Is response body size limited?
[ ] Is retry idempotent?
[ ] Is connection target allowlisted if user-controlled?
[ ] Is local server bound to localhost if not public?
[ ] Are socket protocols framed safely?
[ ] Are length prefixes validated?
```

---

# 51. SSRF-Safe URI Handling

Problem:

```text
User submits URL.
Server fetches it.
User points to internal metadata/service.
```

Dangerous targets:

```text
localhost
127.0.0.1
0.0.0.0
::1
169.254.169.254
private IP ranges
internal DNS names
file:// URI
jar:// URI
```

Basic allowlist pattern:

```java
public URI validateExternalUri(String raw) {
    URI uri = URI.create(raw);

    if (!"https".equalsIgnoreCase(uri.getScheme())) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Only HTTPS is allowed");
    }

    String host = uri.getHost();

    if (host == null || !host.endsWith(".trusted-example.com")) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Host not allowed");
    }

    return uri;
}
```

Rule:

```text
Prefer allowlist over blocklist for outbound fetches controlled by user input.
```

---

# 52. Retry Rules

Retry only if operation is safe.

| Operation | Retry? |
|---|---|
| GET idempotent | usually yes |
| PUT idempotent | often yes |
| DELETE idempotent | maybe |
| POST create payment/order | only with idempotency key |
| raw socket send | depends on protocol |
| UDP send | app-level decision |

Bad:

```text
Retry POST payment without idempotency key.
```

Better:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Idempotency-Key", idempotencyKey)
    .POST(...)
    .build();
```

---

# 53. Backpressure / Bulkhead

Do not allow unlimited outbound calls.

```java
Semaphore bulkhead = new Semaphore(50);

public HttpResponse<String> call(HttpRequest request) {
    boolean acquired = false;

    try {
        acquired = bulkhead.tryAcquire(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);

        if (!acquired) {
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("Outbound bulkhead full");
        }

        return client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new RuntimeException(e);
    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
    } finally {
        if (acquired) {
            bulkhead.release();
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Virtual threads make waiting cheaper.
They do not make downstream systems unlimited.
```

---

# 54. Common Networking Exceptions

| Exception | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `UnknownHostException` | DNS/hostname failed |
| `ConnectException` | connection refused/unreachable |
| `SocketTimeoutException` | read/connect timeout |
| `NoRouteToHostException` | routing/network issue |
| `SSLHandshakeException` | TLS handshake/cert problem |
| `EOFException` | peer closed unexpectedly |
| `ProtocolException` | protocol-level issue |
| `IOException` | generic network I/O failure |
| `InterruptedException` | thread interrupted while waiting |

Example:

```java
try {
    return client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
} catch (HttpTimeoutException e) {
    throw new DownstreamTimeoutException(e);
} catch (ConnectException e) {
    throw new DownstreamUnavailableException(e);
} catch (IOException e) {
    throw new DownstreamIOException(e);
} catch (InterruptedException e) {
    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
    throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted during HTTP call", e);
}
```

---

# 55. Observability Fields

For outbound calls, log:

```text
correlationId
method
scheme
host
path template, not full sensitive URL
status code
duration
timeout
retry attempt
error type
```

Avoid logging:

```text
Authorization
Cookie
Set-Cookie
access_token query param
password query param
full PII payload
client secret
API key
```

Example:

```java
long start = System.nanoTime();

try {
    HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString());

    long durationMs = Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start).toMillis();

    log.info(
        "Outbound HTTP completed: method={}, host={}, status={}, durationMs={}",
        request.method(),
        request.uri().getHost(),
        response.statusCode(),
        durationMs
    );

    return response;
} catch (Exception e) {
    long durationMs = Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start).toMillis();

    log.warn(
        "Outbound HTTP failed: method={}, host={}, durationMs={}, errorType={}",
        request.method(),
        request.uri().getHost(),
        durationMs,
        e.getClass().getSimpleName()
    );

    throw e;
}
```

---

# 56. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem |
|---|---|
| No timeout | hanging request/thread |
| Logging full URL with token | secret leak |
| Using `URL` for equality/comparison | surprising DNS/network behavior risk |
| String concat query params | broken encoding |
| Retry POST without idempotency | duplicate side effects |
| Assuming TCP read equals message | protocol bug |
| No length validation | memory attack |
| Infinite socket accept with no shutdown plan | operational issue |
| Binding local service to `0.0.0.0` accidentally | exposure |
| Ignoring `InterruptedException` | broken cancellation |
| Not consuming process stdout/stderr | process deadlock |
| File lock as distributed lock on NFS | unreliable |
| Trust-all SSL context | critical security bug |
| Disabling hostname verification | MITM risk |
| User-controlled URL fetch without allowlist | SSRF risk |

---

# 57. Decision Guide

```text
Need call REST/HTTP service?
-> HttpClient or framework client

Need custom reliable protocol?
-> TCP Socket / SocketChannel

Need low-latency lossy datagrams?
-> UDP DatagramSocket

Need many connections with few threads?
-> NIO Selector or framework

Need simple local process communication?
-> ProcessBuilder stdin/stdout

Need local daemon IPC?
-> Unix domain socket or loopback TCP

Need simple batch handoff?
-> file-based IPC with atomic move

Need single-instance process lock?
-> FileChannel lock, with OS/filesystem caveats

Need high-performance shared local data?
-> memory-mapped file, but design synchronization carefully

Need user-supplied external URL?
-> allowlist, HTTPS-only, SSRF protection
```

---

# 58. Production Checklist

```text
HTTP
[ ] connect timeout set?
[ ] request timeout set?
[ ] status code handled?
[ ] retry policy idempotent?
[ ] headers sanitized in logs?
[ ] redirects intentional?
[ ] response size bounded?

Socket
[ ] connect timeout set?
[ ] read timeout set?
[ ] protocol framing defined?
[ ] max frame size validated?
[ ] socket closed with try-with-resources?
[ ] server bind address intentional?
[ ] thread/concurrency model bounded?

URI/URL
[ ] URI used instead of URL where possible?
[ ] query parameters encoded?
[ ] user URLs allowlisted?
[ ] path traversal / SSRF considered?
[ ] no secrets in query string?

IPC
[ ] child stdout/stderr consumed?
[ ] process timeout enforced?
[ ] exit code checked?
[ ] file handoff uses temp + atomic move?
[ ] lock behavior valid on target filesystem?
[ ] Unix socket permissions checked?

Security
[ ] TLS validation enabled?
[ ] hostname verification enabled?
[ ] no trust-all certs?
[ ] credentials not logged?
[ ] local-only service bound to localhost?
```

---

# 59. Compact Summary

```text
URI
- syntax and identifier
- prefer for parsing/building addresses
- encode query values
- validate user-controlled URI

URL
- legacy locator with protocol handler
- avoid for equality/comparison
- use when old API requires it

HttpClient
- modern Java HTTP client
- supports sync/async
- set connect and request timeout
- handle status codes explicitly

TCP Socket
- reliable byte stream
- not message-oriented
- define framing
- set connect/read timeout

ServerSocket
- listens for TCP clients
- use executor or virtual threads
- bind address intentionally

UDP
- datagram-oriented
- unreliable/unordered
- good for lossy low-latency cases

NIO SocketChannel
- channel/buffer-based
- can be non-blocking
- useful for custom scalable servers

Selector
- multiplex many channels with one thread
- powerful but complex

TLS
- use HTTPS/SSLContext
- never disable cert validation in production

IPC
- ProcessBuilder pipes
- loopback TCP
- Unix domain socket
- files with atomic move
- file locks
- memory-mapped files

Production rules
- timeout everything
- bound concurrency
- validate remote targets
- frame protocols
- mask secrets
- observe latency/status/error
```

Kesimpulan praktis: **networking code yang benar bukan hanya bisa connect.** Yang paling penting adalah **timeout, framing, encoding URI, TLS correctness, retry idempotency, resource cleanup, bounded concurrency, dan security boundary**. Untuk enterprise backend, gunakan `HttpClient` atau framework client untuk HTTP, gunakan raw socket hanya saat membangun custom protocol/IPC, dan selalu desain failure mode sejak awal.

## Response:
7/12/2026, 9:09:12 PM

Saya akan buat khusus untuk `java.net.http.HttpClient`: request/response, sync/async, timeout, headers, body publisher/handler, error handling, retry, security, observability, dan pola production.

Berikut **cheat sheet Java HTTP Client** untuk **Java 17+** menggunakan `java.net.http.HttpClient`.

`HttpClient` adalah HTTP client standard Java sejak Java 11 melalui JEP 321. API utamanya berada di package `java.net.http`, mendukung HTTP/1.1 dan HTTP/2, menyediakan mode sync dan async berbasis `CompletableFuture`, dan client yang sudah dibuat bersifat immutable serta bisa dipakai untuk banyak request. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/321))

# Java HTTP Client Cheat Sheet

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text
HttpClient
  = reusable client object
  = menyimpan konfigurasi koneksi, proxy, redirect, SSL, authenticator, executor

HttpRequest
  = immutable request
  = method, URI, headers, timeout, body

HttpResponse<T>
  = response
  = status code, headers, body typed as T

BodyPublisher
  = request body producer

BodyHandler / BodySubscriber
  = response body consumer
```

Core classes:

```java
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
```

---

# 2. Minimal GET

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/orders"))
    .GET()
    .build();

HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
);

System.out.println(response.statusCode());
System.out.println(response.body());
```

---

# 3. Reuse `HttpClient`

Baik:

```java
public final class OrderApiClient {
    private final HttpClient client;

    public OrderApiClient() {
        this.client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
            .build();
    }
}
```

Buruk:

```java
public String call() {
    HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient(); // jangan buat terus-menerus
    // send request
}
```

Kenapa? `HttpClient` menyimpan konfigurasi dan resource sharing untuk request yang dikirim melaluinya, termasuk connection pool yang bisa dipakai ulang; membuat client baru untuk setiap operasi biasanya menghilangkan manfaat reuse koneksi. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpClient.html))

---

# 4. Client Builder

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
    .followRedirects(HttpClient.Redirect.NORMAL)
    .version(HttpClient.Version.HTTP_2)
    .build();
```

Common options:

| Option | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `connectTimeout()` | timeout saat connect |
| `followRedirects()` | policy redirect |
| `version()` | prefer HTTP/1.1 atau HTTP/2 |
| `proxy()` | proxy selector |
| `authenticator()` | HTTP auth |
| `cookieHandler()` | cookie management |
| `sslContext()` | TLS config |
| `executor()` | executor untuk async/dependent tasks |

Builder memang dipakai untuk konfigurasi state client seperti preferred protocol version, redirect, proxy, authenticator, dan lainnya. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpClient.html))

---

# 5. Request Builder

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/orders/123"))
    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
    .header("Accept", "application/json")
    .GET()
    .build();
```

`HttpRequest` dibuat lewat builder. Builder dapat mengatur URI, headers, body, dan request body diberikan melalui `BodyPublisher` pada `POST`, `PUT`, atau `method`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs//api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpRequest.html))

---

# 6. Timeout

Ada dua level timeout:

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
    .build();

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
    .GET()
    .build();
```

| Timeout | Makna |
|---|---|
| `connectTimeout` | batas waktu membuat koneksi |
| request `timeout` | batas waktu request selesai |

Rule production:

```text
Every outbound HTTP call must have timeout.
No timeout = risk request menggantung.
```

---

# 7. GET with Query Parameter

Minimal:

```java
String status = URLEncoder.encode("UNDER REVIEW", StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

URI uri = URI.create(
    "https://api.example.com/orders?status=" + status
);

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .GET()
    .build();
```

Helper sederhana:

```java
public static String queryParam(String value) {
    return URLEncoder.encode(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
}
```

Penting:

```text
URLEncoder cocok untuk query/form value.
Jangan pakai string concat sembarangan untuk URL user-controlled.
```

---

# 8. POST JSON

```java
String json = """
    {
      "customerId": "c-123",
      "amount": 100.50
    }
    """;

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/orders"))
    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
    .header("Accept", "application/json")
    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
    .build();

HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
);
```

---

# 9. PUT JSON

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/orders/123"))
    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
    .PUT(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
    .build();
```

---

# 10. DELETE

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/orders/123"))
    .DELETE()
    .build();
```

DELETE with body jika API mengharuskan:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .method("DELETE", HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json))
    .build();
```

---

# 11. PATCH

Tidak ada shortcut `.PATCH()`, gunakan `.method()`:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
    .method("PATCH", HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json))
    .build();
```

---

# 12. Headers

Set single header:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Accept", "application/json")
    .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + token)
    .build();
```

Set multiple headers:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .headers(
        "Accept", "application/json",
        "Content-Type", "application/json"
    )
    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json))
    .build();
```

Read response headers:

```java
HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString());

Optional<String> contentType =
    response.headers().firstValue("Content-Type");

Map<String, List<String>> headers =
    response.headers().map();
```

Jangan log:

```text
Authorization
Cookie
Set-Cookie
X-Api-Key
access_token
refresh_token
client_secret
```

---

# 13. BodyPublishers

Common request body publishers:

| Publisher | Use |
|---|---|
| `noBody()` | body kosong |
| `ofString()` | JSON/text |
| `ofByteArray()` | binary dari byte array |
| `ofFile()` | upload file |
| `ofInputStream()` | streaming upload |
| `fromPublisher()` | custom reactive publisher |

Contoh:

```java
HttpRequest.BodyPublisher body =
    HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

`BodyPublishers` memang menyediakan publisher praktis seperti body dari `String` atau file. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/jp/21/docs/api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 14. Upload File

```java
Path file = Path.of("data/report.pdf");

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/upload"))
    .header("Content-Type", "application/pdf")
    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofFile(file))
    .build();

HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
);
```

---

# 15. Upload Binary Byte Array

```java
byte[] payload = Files.readAllBytes(Path.of("data.bin"));

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofByteArray(payload))
    .build();
```

Untuk file besar, lebih baik `ofFile()` daripada membaca semua ke memory.

---

# 16. BodyHandlers

Common response handlers:

| Handler | Body Type | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `ofString()` | `String` | JSON/text kecil |
| `ofByteArray()` | `byte[]` | binary kecil |
| `ofFile(path)` | `Path` | download file |
| `ofInputStream()` | `InputStream` | streaming response |
| `ofLines()` | `Stream<String>` | streaming lines |
| `discarding()` | `Void` | ignore body |

`ofString()` membaca body lengkap menjadi `String`; jika response `Content-Type` tidak punya charset atau charset tidak didukung, default-nya UTF-8. `ofInputStream()` dan `ofLines()` mengembalikan body yang mungkin belum seluruhnya diterima saat `HttpResponse` dikembalikan. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.html))

---

# 17. Download to File

```java
Path target = Path.of("download/report.pdf");

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(URI.create("https://api.example.com/report.pdf"))
    .GET()
    .build();

HttpResponse<Path> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofFile(target)
);

Path downloaded = response.body();
```

---

# 18. Stream Response Body

```java
HttpResponse<InputStream> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofInputStream()
);

try (InputStream input = response.body()) {
    input.transferTo(Files.newOutputStream(Path.of("output.bin")));
}
```

Rule:

```text
Jika pakai ofInputStream(), close stream.
Jika pakai ofLines(), close Stream<String>.
```

Failing to close, cancel, or fully consume returned streams from handlers such as `ofInputStream()` or `ofLines()` can interfere with orderly completion/shutdown behavior. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/22/docs//api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpClient.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. Stream Lines

```java
HttpResponse<Stream<String>> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofLines()
);

try (Stream<String> lines = response.body()) {
    lines
        .filter(line -> !line.isBlank())
        .forEach(System.out::println);
}
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
NDJSON
large text response
server log stream
line-based protocol over HTTP
```

---

# 20. Sync vs Async

## Sync

```java
HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
);
```

Use when:

```text
simple flow
blocking codebase
virtual thread model
straightforward error handling
```

## Async

```java
CompletableFuture<HttpResponse<String>> future =
    client.sendAsync(
        request,
        HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
    );

future
    .thenApply(HttpResponse::body)
    .thenAccept(System.out::println)
    .join();
```

Use when:

```text
fan-out/fan-in
non-blocking orchestration
parallel downstream calls
composition dengan CompletableFuture
```

JEP 321 menjelaskan API ini mendukung request/response non-blocking melalui `CompletableFuture`. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/321))

---

# 21. Async with Timeout

```java
CompletableFuture<HttpResponse<String>> future =
    client.sendAsync(request, BodyHandlers.ofString())
        .orTimeout(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

Fallback:

```java
CompletableFuture<HttpResponse<String>> future =
    client.sendAsync(request, BodyHandlers.ofString())
        .completeOnTimeout(fallbackResponse, 5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
```

Biasanya lebih jelas set timeout di `HttpRequest` juga:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
    .GET()
    .build();
```

---

# 22. Status Code Handling

```java
HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    BodyHandlers.ofString()
);

int status = response.statusCode();

if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
    return response.body();
}

if (status == 404) {
    throw new NotFoundException("Resource not found");
}

if (status == 409) {
    throw new ConflictException(response.body());
}

if (status >= 500) {
    throw new DownstreamServerException(status, response.body());
}

throw new DownstreamClientException(status, response.body());
```

Cheat sheet:

| Status | Meaning | Typical Handling |
|---|---|---|
| `200` | OK | success |
| `201` | Created | success + location/body |
| `204` | No Content | success tanpa body |
| `400` | Bad Request | caller/request bug |
| `401` | Unauthorized | auth missing/invalid |
| `403` | Forbidden | auth ok, access denied |
| `404` | Not Found | absent resource |
| `409` | Conflict | domain conflict |
| `422` | Validation error | business validation |
| `429` | Rate limited | backoff/retry later |
| `500` | Server error | retry if safe |
| `502/503/504` | gateway/unavailable/timeout | retry if safe |

---

# 23. Response Wrapper Pattern

```java
public record ApiResponse<T>(
    int statusCode,
    HttpHeaders headers,
    T body
) {
    public boolean isSuccess() {
        return statusCode >= 200 && statusCode < 300;
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString());

ApiResponse<String> apiResponse = new ApiResponse<>(
    response.statusCode(),
    response.headers(),
    response.body()
);
```

---

# 24. Generic Send Helper

```java
public final class HttpJsonClient {
    private final HttpClient client;

    public HttpJsonClient(HttpClient client) {
        this.client = client;
    }

    public String sendJson(HttpRequest request) {
        try {
            HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
                request,
                HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            );

            int status = response.statusCode();

            if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
                return response.body();
            }

            throw new DownstreamHttpException(
                status,
                "HTTP call failed: status=" + status
            );
        } catch (HttpTimeoutException e) {
            throw new DownstreamTimeoutException("HTTP request timeout", e);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new DownstreamIOException("HTTP I/O failure", e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted during HTTP call", e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 25. Exception Handling

Common exceptions:

| Exception | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `HttpTimeoutException` | request timeout |
| `ConnectException` | connection refused/unavailable |
| `UnknownHostException` | DNS failed |
| `SSLHandshakeException` | TLS/certificate issue |
| `IOException` | network I/O failure |
| `InterruptedException` | thread interrupted during sync send |
| `CompletionException` | async failure wrapper |

Sync pattern:

```java
try {
    return client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString());
} catch (HttpTimeoutException e) {
    throw new DownstreamTimeoutException(e);
} catch (IOException e) {
    throw new DownstreamIOException(e);
} catch (InterruptedException e) {
    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
    throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted", e);
}
```

Async pattern:

```java
client.sendAsync(request, BodyHandlers.ofString())
    .exceptionally(error -> {
        Throwable cause = unwrapCompletionException(error);
        throw new DownstreamException(cause);
    });
```

Helper:

```java
private static Throwable unwrapCompletionException(Throwable error) {
    if (error instanceof CompletionException && error.getCause() != null) {
        return error.getCause();
    }
    return error;
}
```

---

# 26. Retry Pattern

Retry hanya aman jika operasi idempotent atau punya idempotency key.

```java
public HttpResponse<String> sendWithRetry(
    HttpRequest request,
    int maxAttempts
) {
    RuntimeException last = null;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
        try {
            HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
                request,
                BodyHandlers.ofString()
            );

            if (response.statusCode() < 500 && response.statusCode() != 429) {
                return response;
            }

            last = new RuntimeException(
                "Retryable HTTP status: " + response.statusCode()
            );

        } catch (IOException e) {
            last = new UncheckedIOException(e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted", e);
        }

        sleep(backoff(attempt));
    }

    throw last;
}

private Duration backoff(int attempt) {
    return Duration.ofMillis(100L * (1L << Math.min(attempt - 1, 5)));
}

private void sleep(Duration duration) {
    try {
        Thread.sleep(duration);
    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted during backoff", e);
    }
}
```

Rule:

| Method | Retry? |
|---|---|
| `GET` | biasanya aman |
| `PUT` | sering aman jika idempotent |
| `DELETE` | tergantung API |
| `POST create` | hanya dengan idempotency key |
| `POST payment` | wajib idempotency key |

Idempotency key:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
    .POST(BodyPublishers.ofString(json))
    .build();
```

---

# 27. Bulkhead / Limit Concurrency

Virtual thread atau async bukan alasan untuk menghajar downstream tanpa batas.

```java
public final class BulkheadedHttpClient {
    private final HttpClient client;
    private final Semaphore semaphore;

    public BulkheadedHttpClient(HttpClient client, int maxConcurrentCalls) {
        this.client = client;
        this.semaphore = new Semaphore(maxConcurrentCalls);
    }

    public HttpResponse<String> send(HttpRequest request) {
        boolean acquired = false;

        try {
            acquired = semaphore.tryAcquire(500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);

            if (!acquired) {
                throw new ServiceUnavailableException("HTTP bulkhead full");
            }

            return client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString());

        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted", e);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        } finally {
            if (acquired) {
                semaphore.release();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Use for:

```text
database-backed API
rate-limited external API
payment service
government/regulatory API
legacy on-prem service
```

---

# 28. Fan-Out / Fan-In

```java
CompletableFuture<HttpResponse<String>> customerFuture =
    client.sendAsync(customerRequest, BodyHandlers.ofString());

CompletableFuture<HttpResponse<String>> orderFuture =
    client.sendAsync(orderRequest, BodyHandlers.ofString());

CompletableFuture<View> viewFuture =
    customerFuture.thenCombine(orderFuture, (customerResponse, orderResponse) -> {
        if (customerResponse.statusCode() != 200 || orderResponse.statusCode() != 200) {
            throw new DownstreamException("Failed to build view");
        }

        return buildView(customerResponse.body(), orderResponse.body());
    });

View view = viewFuture
    .orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
    .join();
```

Rule:

```text
Fan-out harus punya deadline global.
Jangan hanya timeout per request tanpa total request budget.
```

---

# 29. Authentication: Bearer Token

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + accessToken)
    .GET()
    .build();
```

Security:

```text
Jangan log token.
Jangan taruh token di query string.
Prefer Authorization header.
```

---

# 30. Basic Auth

Manual:

```java
String raw = username + ":" + password;

String encoded = Base64.getEncoder()
    .encodeToString(raw.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .header("Authorization", "Basic " + encoded)
    .GET()
    .build();
```

Atau via `Authenticator`:

```java
Authenticator authenticator = new Authenticator() {
    @Override
    protected PasswordAuthentication getPasswordAuthentication() {
        return new PasswordAuthentication(
            username,
            password.toCharArray()
        );
    }
};

HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .authenticator(authenticator)
    .build();
```

---

# 31. Cookies

```java
CookieManager cookieManager = new CookieManager();

HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .cookieHandler(cookieManager)
    .build();
```

Use case:

```text
session-based legacy service
stateful internal app
test automation
```

For backend service-to-service APIs, token/header auth is usually clearer than cookie session.

---

# 32. Proxy

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .proxy(ProxySelector.of(
        new InetSocketAddress("proxy.company.local", 8080)
    ))
    .build();
```

No proxy:

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .proxy(HttpClient.Builder.NO_PROXY)
    .build();
```

---

# 33. TLS / SSLContext

Default HTTPS biasanya memakai JVM trust store.

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
```

Custom SSL context:

```java
SSLContext sslContext = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS");

// init with custom key/trust managers if needed
sslContext.init(keyManagers, trustManagers, new SecureRandom());

HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .sslContext(sslContext)
    .build();
```

Rule keras:

```text
Jangan disable certificate validation di production.
Jangan pakai trust-all TrustManager.
Jangan disable hostname verification.
```

---

# 34. Redirect Handling

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .followRedirects(HttpClient.Redirect.NORMAL)
    .build();
```

Options:

| Option | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `NEVER` | tidak follow redirect |
| `NORMAL` | follow redirect normal |
| `ALWAYS` | follow semua redirect |

Security concern:

```text
Hati-hati redirect saat membawa Authorization header.
Pastikan redirect policy sesuai boundary trust.
```

---

# 35. HTTP Version

```java
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .version(HttpClient.Version.HTTP_2)
    .build();
```

Per request:

```java
HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
    .uri(uri)
    .version(HttpClient.Version.HTTP_2)
    .GET()
    .build();
```

Rule:

```text
Set HTTP_2 as preference if server mendukung.
Tetap handle fallback/behavior sesuai client implementation.
```

---

# 36. Custom Executor

```java
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(20);

HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
    .executor(executor)
    .build();
```

Use when:

```text
ingin kontrol thread async
ingin nama thread jelas
ingin limit async callback execution
ingin observability lebih baik
```

Shutdown:

```java
executor.shutdown();
```

Caution:

```text
Jangan block sembarangan di executor kecil.
Jangan pakai unbounded executor tanpa alasan.
```

---

# 37. Virtual Threads + Sync HttpClient

Di Java 21+, model ini sering sederhana untuk blocking I/O:

```java
try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
    Future<HttpResponse<String>> future = executor.submit(() ->
        client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString())
    );

    HttpResponse<String> response = future.get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
}
```

Mental model:

```text
Virtual thread makes blocking cheaper.
It does not remove need for timeout, bulkhead, retry discipline, or downstream rate limit.
```

---

# 38. Logging / Observability Pattern

```java
long start = System.nanoTime();

try {
    HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
        request,
        BodyHandlers.ofString()
    );

    long durationMs =
        Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start).toMillis();

    log.info(
        "HTTP outbound completed: method={}, host={}, path={}, status={}, durationMs={}",
        request.method(),
        request.uri().getHost(),
        request.uri().getPath(),
        response.statusCode(),
        durationMs
    );

    return response;

} catch (Exception e) {
    long durationMs =
        Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start).toMillis();

    log.warn(
        "HTTP outbound failed: method={}, host={}, path={}, durationMs={}, errorType={}",
        request.method(),
        request.uri().getHost(),
        request.uri().getPath(),
        durationMs,
        e.getClass().getSimpleName()
    );

    throw e;
}
```

Log fields:

```text
method
scheme
host
path template
status
duration
timeout
retry attempt
error type
correlation id
```

Do not log:

```text
full Authorization header
cookies
full query string with secrets
full request/response body with PII
```

---

# 39. Safe URI Handling

Bad:

```java
URI uri = URI.create("https://api.example.com/search?q=" + query);
```

Better:

```java
String encoded = URLEncoder.encode(query, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

URI uri = URI.create("https://api.example.com/search?q=" + encoded);
```

For user-controlled target URLs, use allowlist:

```java
public URI validateAllowedUri(String raw) {
    URI uri = URI.create(raw);

    if (!"https".equalsIgnoreCase(uri.getScheme())) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Only HTTPS is allowed");
    }

    String host = uri.getHost();

    if (host == null || !host.endsWith(".trusted.example.com")) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Host is not allowed");
    }

    return uri;
}
```

SSRF rule:

```text
Jika user bisa menentukan URL yang server fetch,
gunakan allowlist host/scheme.
Jangan cuma blocklist localhost/private IP.
```

---

# 40. Handling 204 No Content

```java
HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
    request,
    BodyHandlers.ofString()
);

if (response.statusCode() == 204) {
    return Optional.empty();
}

return Optional.of(response.body());
```

Atau:

```java
HttpResponse<Void> response = client.send(
    request,
    BodyHandlers.discarding()
);
```

---

# 41. Large Response Safety

Bad:

```java
HttpResponse<String> response =
    client.send(request, BodyHandlers.ofString());
```

Untuk response besar, ini bisa memory spike.

Better:

```java
HttpResponse<Path> response = client.send(
    request,
    BodyHandlers.ofFile(Path.of("large-output.dat"))
);
```

Atau streaming:

```java
HttpResponse<InputStream> response = client.send(
    request,
    BodyHandlers.ofInputStream()
);

try (InputStream in = response.body()) {
    processStream(in);
}
```

---

# 42. Minimal JSON Client Wrapper

Tanpa library JSON, wrapper hanya mengirim/menerima string:

```java
public final class SimpleJsonHttpClient {
    private final HttpClient client;
    private final URI baseUri;

    public SimpleJsonHttpClient(URI baseUri) {
        this.baseUri = baseUri;
        this.client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
            .version(HttpClient.Version.HTTP_2)
            .build();
    }

    public String get(String path) {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(baseUri.resolve(path))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
            .header("Accept", "application/json")
            .GET()
            .build();

        return send(request);
    }

    public String postJson(String path, String json) {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(baseUri.resolve(path))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
            .header("Accept", "application/json")
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(BodyPublishers.ofString(json, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
            .build();

        return send(request);
    }

    private String send(HttpRequest request) {
        try {
            HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
                request,
                BodyHandlers.ofString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            );

            if (response.statusCode() >= 200 && response.statusCode() < 300) {
                return response.body();
            }

            throw new DownstreamHttpException(
                response.statusCode(),
                response.body()
            );

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new RuntimeException("Interrupted", e);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 43. Testing Pattern with Local Stub

Dengan `com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer` untuk test sederhana:

```java
HttpServer server = HttpServer.create(
    new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 0),
    0
);

server.createContext("/orders", exchange -> {
    byte[] body = """
        {"id":"o-123","status":"SUBMITTED"}
        """.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

    exchange.getResponseHeaders()
        .add("Content-Type", "application/json");

    exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, body.length);

    try (OutputStream os = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
        os.write(body);
    }
});

server.start();

int port = server.getAddress().getPort();

URI baseUri = URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/");

try {
    // run client test
} finally {
    server.stop(0);
}
```

Use case:

```text
unit/integration test ringan
no external dependency
simulate status code
simulate timeout/error
```

---

# 44. Maven Module Info

Jika pakai JPMS:

```java
module com.example.client {
    requires java.net.http;
}
```

Package `java.net.http` berada dalam module `java.net.http`, yang mendefinisikan HTTP Client dan WebSocket APIs. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/25/docs/api/java.net.http/module-summary.html))

---

# 45. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Masalah |
|---|---|
| Buat `HttpClient` baru per request | connection reuse buruk |
| Tidak set timeout | request bisa menggantung |
| Retry semua POST | duplicate side effect |
| Log full Authorization header | secret leak |
| Log full query string | token/PII leak |
| `ofString()` untuk response besar | memory spike |
| Tidak close `InputStream` dari `ofInputStream()` | resource leak |
| String concat query param | encoding bug |
| Follow redirect sembarangan | auth/security boundary leak |
| Trust-all SSL | critical security issue |
| Async tanpa error handling | failure hilang |
| Unlimited fan-out | downstream overload |
| Swallow `InterruptedException` | cancellation rusak |
| Treat all non-2xx same | poor domain error handling |

---

# 46. Production Checklist

```text
Client
[ ] HttpClient reused?
[ ] connectTimeout set?
[ ] HTTP version intentional?
[ ] redirect policy intentional?
[ ] proxy/auth/cookie config clear?

Request
[ ] URI built safely?
[ ] query params encoded?
[ ] request timeout set?
[ ] Accept/Content-Type correct?
[ ] Authorization not logged?
[ ] idempotency key for unsafe retry?

Response
[ ] status code mapped explicitly?
[ ] large body not loaded into memory?
[ ] streaming body closed?
[ ] error body handled safely?
[ ] response headers inspected if needed?

Reliability
[ ] retry bounded?
[ ] retry only idempotent/safe operations?
[ ] backoff/jitter considered?
[ ] bulkhead/concurrency limit exists?
[ ] async failures observed?
[ ] InterruptedException restores interrupt flag?

Security
[ ] TLS validation enabled?
[ ] hostname verification enabled?
[ ] no trust-all certs?
[ ] SSRF protection for user-controlled URLs?
[ ] secrets masked in logs?

Observability
[ ] method/host/path/status/duration logged?
[ ] correlationId propagated?
[ ] timeout/retry metrics exist?
[ ] downstream error type captured?
```

---

# 47. Compact Summary

```text
HttpClient
- Reusable, immutable client.
- Create once per config/downstream.
- Set connect timeout.
- Configure redirect/proxy/auth/SSL as needed.

HttpRequest
- Immutable request.
- Built with builder.
- Set URI, method, headers, timeout, body.

BodyPublisher
- Sends request body.
- ofString for JSON/text.
- ofFile for file upload.
- ofByteArray for binary.

HttpResponse
- Has statusCode, headers, body.
- Body type depends on BodyHandler.

BodyHandler
- ofString for small text.
- ofByteArray for small binary.
- ofFile for download.
- ofInputStream for streaming.
- discarding for no body.

Sync
- client.send()
- simple and readable.
- good with virtual threads.

Async
- client.sendAsync()
- returns CompletableFuture.
- good for fan-out/fan-in.

Production Rules
- Reuse client.
- Timeout every request.
- Encode query params.
- Handle status codes explicitly.
- Retry only safe/idempotent operations.
- Limit concurrency.
- Mask secrets.
- Close streaming bodies.
```

Kesimpulan praktis: **`HttpClient` cocok sebagai default Java SE HTTP client untuk service-to-service call, CLI tooling, batch job, dan integration utility.** Untuk production, correctness-nya bukan di syntax request, tetapi di **timeout, status mapping, retry idempotency, bulkhead, TLS, URI safety, resource closing, dan observability**.

---

**Sources:**

- [JEP 321: HTTP Client API](https://openjdk.org/jeps/321)
- [HttpClient (Java SE 21 & JDK 21)](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.net.http/java/net/http/HttpClient.html)

## Response:
7/12/2026, 9:12:51 PM

Berikut **cheat sheet Java HTTP Webserver Standard Library** untuk **Java 17+**.

Fokusnya adalah HTTP server bawaan JDK: `jdk.httpserver`, `HttpServer`, `HttpsServer`, `HttpHandler`, `HttpExchange`, filter, authenticator, static file server, dan batasan production-nya.

# Java HTTP Webserver Standard Library Cheat Sheet

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text id="9tqqgn"
Java Standard HTTP Server
├── Module
│   └── jdk.httpserver
│
├── Package
│   └── com.sun.net.httpserver
│
├── Core Classes
│   ├── HttpServer
│   ├── HttpsServer
│   ├── HttpHandler
│   ├── HttpExchange
│   ├── HttpContext
│   ├── Filter
│   ├── Authenticator
│   ├── BasicAuthenticator
│   ├── Headers
│   ├── SimpleFileServer
│   └── HttpHandlers
│
└── Tool
    └── jwebserver
```

`jdk.httpserver` mendefinisikan API HTTP server bawaan JDK dan menyediakan tool `jwebserver` untuk menjalankan minimal HTTP server. Package `com.sun.net.httpserver` menyediakan API high-level untuk embedded HTTP server dan mendukung HTTP serta HTTPS. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/jdk.httpserver/module-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan standard HTTP server untuk:

```text id="8m1hlu"
local dev server
mock server
test stub
health/debug endpoint internal
small admin endpoint
CLI tool exposing HTTP temporarily
demo/prototype
simple file serving
embedded lightweight tool
```

Jangan jadikan default untuk:

```text id="mcjbtn"
large production REST API
complex routing
Jakarta REST replacement
Spring/Quarkus/Micronaut replacement
high-security public API
advanced observability stack
advanced middleware/filter chain
complex auth/authz
large-scale static content delivery
```

JEP 408 menjelaskan Simple Web Server sebagai minimal web server untuk static files, prototyping, ad-hoc coding, testing, dan pendidikan; tidak menyediakan CGI atau servlet-like functionality. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/408?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Module Dependency

Kalau pakai JPMS:

```java id="66vi1x"
module com.example.server {
    requires jdk.httpserver;
}
```

Kalau pakai Maven tanpa JPMS, biasanya tidak perlu dependency eksternal karena ini bagian dari JDK.

---

# 4. Minimal HTTP Server

```java id="v1228y"
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

public class MinimalServer {
    public static void main(String[] args) throws IOException {
        HttpServer server = HttpServer.create(
            new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8080),
            0
        );

        server.createContext("/hello", exchange -> {
            byte[] response = "Hello from Java HttpServer\n"
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

            exchange.getResponseHeaders()
                .add("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");

            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, response.length);

            try (OutputStream body = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
                body.write(response);
            }
        });

        server.start();

        System.out.println("Server started at http://localhost:8080/hello");
    }
}
```

`HttpServer` adalah simple HTTP server yang di-bind ke IP address dan port, lalu listen untuk TCP connection dari client. `HttpsServer` adalah subclass untuk HTTPS. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/jdk.httpserver/com/sun/net/httpserver/HttpServer.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Binding Address

Localhost only:

```java id="yf9xvb"
new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8080)
```

Specific loopback IPv4:

```java id="j0jp0d"
new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 8080)
```

All interfaces:

```java id="4ktlgi"
new InetSocketAddress("0.0.0.0", 8080)
```

Decision:

| Bind Address | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `localhost` | hanya local machine |
| `127.0.0.1` | local IPv4 |
| `0.0.0.0` | semua network interface |
| specific IP | bind ke interface tertentu |

Rule:

```text id="q6p7n3"
Untuk tool lokal/test, bind ke localhost.
Jangan bind ke 0.0.0.0 kecuali memang ingin diakses dari luar.
```

---

# 6. Backlog

```java id="2ggppw"
HttpServer server = HttpServer.create(
    new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8080),
    50
);
```

Backlog adalah queue koneksi TCP yang belum diterima server.

| Value | Meaning |
|---:|---|
| `0` | system default |
| small | lebih cepat reject under burst |
| large | bisa menahan lebih banyak pending connection |

Rule:

```text id="1bf1fa"
Untuk mock/local server, 0 cukup.
Untuk server internal dengan burst kecil, set explicit backlog.
```

---

# 7. Context / Route

```java id="thqurh"
server.createContext("/orders", exchange -> {
    // handle /orders and matching path
});
```

Multiple contexts:

```java id="g6imz7"
server.createContext("/health", new HealthHandler());
server.createContext("/orders", new OrdersHandler());
server.createContext("/metrics", new MetricsHandler());
```

Handler class:

```java id="psl81k"
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange;
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpHandler;

import java.io.IOException;

public final class HealthHandler implements HttpHandler {
    @Override
    public void handle(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        Response.text(exchange, 200, "OK\n");
    }
}
```

---

# 8. HttpExchange

`HttpExchange` mewakili satu request-response exchange.

Common methods:

```java id="p7h50z"
exchange.getRequestMethod();
exchange.getRequestURI();
exchange.getRequestHeaders();
exchange.getRequestBody();

exchange.getResponseHeaders();
exchange.sendResponseHeaders(statusCode, contentLength);
exchange.getResponseBody();

exchange.getRemoteAddress();
exchange.getLocalAddress();
```

Example:

```java id="1dmlv4"
String method = exchange.getRequestMethod();
URI uri = exchange.getRequestURI();
Headers headers = exchange.getRequestHeaders();
InputStream requestBody = exchange.getRequestBody();
```

---

# 9. Response Helper

```java id="u7suog"
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

public final class Response {
    private Response() {}

    public static void text(
        HttpExchange exchange,
        int status,
        String body
    ) throws IOException {
        byte[] bytes = body.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        exchange.getResponseHeaders()
            .set("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");

        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(status, bytes.length);

        try (var output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
            output.write(bytes);
        }
    }

    public static void json(
        HttpExchange exchange,
        int status,
        String json
    ) throws IOException {
        byte[] bytes = json.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        exchange.getResponseHeaders()
            .set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");

        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(status, bytes.length);

        try (var output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
            output.write(bytes);
        }
    }

    public static void noContent(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(204, -1);
        exchange.close();
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Method Routing

```java id="nq46ww"
server.createContext("/orders", exchange -> {
    String method = exchange.getRequestMethod();

    switch (method) {
        case "GET" -> handleGetOrders(exchange);
        case "POST" -> handleCreateOrder(exchange);
        default -> Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
    }
});
```

Set `Allow` header:

```java id="sov1o2"
exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET, POST");
Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
```

---

# 11. Path Handling

```java id="om4c9c"
URI uri = exchange.getRequestURI();

String path = uri.getPath();
```

Example:

```text id="53mf64"
/orders/123
```

Manual path extraction:

```java id="b1pwk0"
private static Optional<String> extractOrderId(String path) {
    String prefix = "/orders/";

    if (!path.startsWith(prefix)) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    String id = path.substring(prefix.length());

    if (id.isBlank() || id.contains("/")) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    return Optional.of(id);
}
```

Use:

```java id="u7205d"
String path = exchange.getRequestURI().getPath();

Optional<String> orderId = extractOrderId(path);
```

---

# 12. Query Parameter Parsing

```java id="se5r7v"
public static Map<String, List<String>> parseQuery(String rawQuery) {
    Map<String, List<String>> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    if (rawQuery == null || rawQuery.isBlank()) {
        return result;
    }

    for (String pair : rawQuery.split("&")) {
        int idx = pair.indexOf('=');

        String rawKey = idx >= 0 ? pair.substring(0, idx) : pair;
        String rawValue = idx >= 0 ? pair.substring(idx + 1) : "";

        String key = URLDecoder.decode(rawKey, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        String value = URLDecoder.decode(rawValue, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        result.computeIfAbsent(key, ignored -> new ArrayList<>())
            .add(value);
    }

    return result;
}
```

Use:

```java id="5yfsmy"
String rawQuery = exchange.getRequestURI().getRawQuery();

Map<String, List<String>> query = parseQuery(rawQuery);

String status = query.getOrDefault("status", List.of())
    .stream()
    .findFirst()
    .orElse("ALL");
```

# 13. Request Headers

```java id="k272vk"
Headers headers = exchange.getRequestHeaders();

String contentType = headers.getFirst("Content-Type");
String authorization = headers.getFirst("Authorization");
```

Headers are multi-value:

```java id="1p71bn"
List<String> values = headers.get("Accept");
```

Security rule:

```text id="4mkcfs"
Jangan log Authorization, Cookie, token, secret, API key.
```

---

# 14. Response Headers

```java id="137gom"
Headers responseHeaders = exchange.getResponseHeaders();

responseHeaders.set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");
responseHeaders.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
responseHeaders.add("X-Request-Id", requestId);
```

Common headers:

| Header | Use |
|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | response media type |
| `Content-Length` | body length |
| `Cache-Control` | caching policy |
| `Location` | created/redirect resource |
| `Allow` | allowed methods |
| `WWW-Authenticate` | auth challenge |
| `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` | CORS |
| `X-Request-Id` | correlation |

---

# 15. Reading Request Body

```java id="mm30pc"
String body;

try (InputStream input = exchange.getRequestBody()) {
    body = new String(input.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
}
```

Warning:

```text id="84bj9z"
readAllBytes() tidak cocok untuk body besar.
Batasi body size untuk keamanan.
```

Safer limited read:

```java id="wuaamu"
public static byte[] readLimited(InputStream input, int maxBytes)
        throws IOException {
    ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

    byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
    int total = 0;
    int read;

    while ((read = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
        total += read;

        if (total > maxBytes) {
            throw new IOException("Request body too large");
        }

        output.write(buffer, 0, read);
    }

    return output.toByteArray();
}
```

Use:

```java id="rtvffd"
byte[] bytes = readLimited(exchange.getRequestBody(), 1_000_000);
String json = new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
```

---

# 16. JSON Response Without External Library

```java id="fkbkk6"
String json = """
    {
      "status": "UP"
    }
    """;

Response.json(exchange, 200, json);
```

Manual escape helper for small utilities:

```java id="hm8u57"
public static String jsonString(String value) {
    return "\"" + value
        .replace("\\", "\\\\")
        .replace("\"", "\\\"")
        .replace("\n", "\\n")
        .replace("\r", "\\r")
        .replace("\t", "\\t")
        + "\"";
}
```

For real apps, use Jackson/JSON-B. Standard library does not include a full JSON binding library.

# 17. Static JSON Endpoint

```java id="2pfu27"
server.createContext("/health", exchange -> {
    if (!"GET".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET");
        Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
        return;
    }

    Response.json(exchange, 200, """
        {"status":"UP"}
        """);
});
```

```java id="ecv622"
exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Location", "/orders/123");

Response.json(exchange, 201, """
    {"id":"123","status":"CREATED"}
    """);
```

# 19. Error Response Pattern

```java id="p4i4a5"
public record ErrorResponse(
    String code,
    String message
) {
    public String toJson() {
        return """
            {"code":%s,"message":%s}
            """.formatted(
                jsonString(code),
                jsonString(message)
            );
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="3v1vpj"
Response.json(
    exchange,
    400,
    new ErrorResponse("INVALID_REQUEST", "Invalid request body").toJson()
);
```

# 20. Global Safe Handler Wrapper

```java id="526m19"
@FunctionalInterface
public interface ThrowingHandler {
    void handle(HttpExchange exchange) throws Exception;
}
```

Wrapper:

```java id="3fqbx1"
public static HttpHandler safe(ThrowingHandler handler) {
    return exchange -> {
        try {
            handler.handle(exchange);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            Response.json(
                exchange,
                400,
                new ErrorResponse("BAD_REQUEST", e.getMessage()).toJson()
            );
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();

            Response.json(
                exchange,
                500,
                new ErrorResponse("INTERNAL_ERROR", "Internal server error").toJson()
            );
        } finally {
            exchange.close();
        }
    };
}
```

Use:

```java id="aigltp"
server.createContext("/orders", safe(this::handleOrders));
```

---

# 21. Exchange Close Rule

Always complete/close exchange.

Good:

```java id="xnzadx"
try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
    output.write(bytes);
}
```

Or:

```java id="xq542m"
exchange.close();
```

Rule:

```text id="wmk9a6"
Set headers.
sendResponseHeaders().
Write body if any.
Close response body or exchange.
```

---

# 22. Content Length

Known length:

```java id="g4b2vp"
exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, bytes.length);
```

No body:

```java id="1d0jtm"
exchange.sendResponseHeaders(204, -1);
```

Chunked / unknown length:

```java id="2rznl5"
exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, 0);
```

Then write stream:

```java id="iaqmk6"
try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
    output.write(data);
}
```

Practical rule:

```text id="z5237k"
Jika body kecil dan sudah dalam byte[], gunakan exact content length.
Jika streaming/unknown, gunakan 0.
Jika no body, gunakan -1.
```

---

# 23. Executor

Default executor behavior is implementation-specific enough that you should configure your own for any non-trivial server.

Platform thread pool:

```java id="rynswk"
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(20);

server.setExecutor(executor);
```

Java 21+ virtual thread executor:

```java id="k8o3n6"
server.setExecutor(Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor());
```

Full example:

```java id="jdqzzs"
HttpServer server = HttpServer.create(
    new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8080),
    0
);

server.setExecutor(Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor());

server.createContext("/health", safe(exchange -> {
    Response.json(exchange, 200, "{\"status\":\"UP\"}");
}));

server.start();
```

Rule:

```text id="68j55x"
Set executor explicitly.
For blocking handlers, virtual threads are attractive on Java 21+.
Still add bulkhead/limits for DB/downstream calls.
```

---

# 24. Graceful Stop

```java id="6891dy"
server.stop(5);
```

`delay` adalah jumlah detik maksimum untuk menunggu exchange berjalan sebelum server berhenti.

Add shutdown hook:

```java id="9f4vjq"
Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(() -> {
    System.out.println("Stopping HTTP server...");
    server.stop(5);
    executor.shutdown();
}));
```

Pattern:

```java id="g7qgqg"
ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(20);

server.setExecutor(executor);

Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(() -> {
    server.stop(5);
    executor.shutdown();
}));
```

---

# 25. Filter

Filter bisa dipakai untuk cross-cutting concern.

```java id="jt1fqf"
public final class LoggingFilter extends Filter {
    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        long start = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            chain.doFilter(exchange);
        } finally {
            long durationMs =
                Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start).toMillis();

            System.out.printf(
                "%s %s durationMs=%d%n",
                exchange.getRequestMethod(),
                exchange.getRequestURI(),
                durationMs
            );
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Request logging filter";
    }
}
```

Attach:

```java id="dx9e09"
HttpContext context = server.createContext("/orders", safe(this::handleOrders));

context.getFilters().add(new LoggingFilter());
```

Use filters for:

```text id="dx9ot8"
logging
request id
basic CORS
auth pre-check
body size check
security headers
```

---

# 26. Request ID Filter

```java id="ifoq7t"
public final class RequestIdFilter extends Filter {
    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        String requestId = Optional
            .ofNullable(exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("X-Request-Id"))
            .filter(id -> !id.isBlank())
            .orElse(UUID.randomUUID().toString());

        exchange.setAttribute("requestId", requestId);
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("X-Request-Id", requestId);

        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Adds request id";
    }
}
```

Read in handler:

```java id="ea7qab"
String requestId = (String) exchange.getAttribute("requestId");
```

---

# 27. CORS Filter

Simple CORS:

```java id="8sq0q3"
public final class CorsFilter extends Filter {
    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        Headers headers = exchange.getResponseHeaders();

        headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "http://localhost:3000");
        headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS");
        headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, Authorization");

        if ("OPTIONS".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(204, -1);
            exchange.close();
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Simple CORS filter";
    }
}
```

Production warning:

```text id="sxrvsb"
Jangan asal pakai Access-Control-Allow-Origin: * untuk endpoint ber-auth.
```

---

# 28. Basic Authentication

```java id="h9skcy"
HttpContext context = server.createContext("/admin", safe(this::handleAdmin));

context.setAuthenticator(new BasicAuthenticator("admin-area") {
    @Override
    public boolean checkCredentials(String username, String password) {
        return "admin".equals(username)
            && constantTimeEquals(password, System.getenv("ADMIN_PASSWORD"));
    }
});
```

Simple constant-time-ish helper:

```java id="0pus6c"
private static boolean constantTimeEquals(String a, String b) {
    if (a == null || b == null) {
        return false;
    }

    byte[] x = a.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    byte[] y = b.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

    return MessageDigest.isEqual(x, y);
}
```

Warning:

```text id="klwzab"
Basic auth harus pakai HTTPS jika melewati network.
Untuk local-only dev tool, localhost bisa diterima tergantung risiko.
```

---

# 29. Manual Bearer Token Auth Filter

```java id="fag4vw"
public final class BearerAuthFilter extends Filter {
    private final String expectedToken;

    public BearerAuthFilter(String expectedToken) {
        this.expectedToken = expectedToken;
    }

    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        String authorization =
            exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("Authorization");

        if (authorization == null || !authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            unauthorized(exchange);
            return;
        }

        String token = authorization.substring("Bearer ".length());

        if (!MessageDigest.isEqual(
            token.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
            expectedToken.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
        )) {
            unauthorized(exchange);
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    private void unauthorized(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        exchange.getResponseHeaders()
            .set("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer");

        Response.json(
            exchange,
            401,
            "{\"code\":\"UNAUTHORIZED\",\"message\":\"Unauthorized\"}"
        );
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Bearer token authentication";
    }
}
```
