# 30. Static File Server: SimpleFileServer

Java menyediakan `SimpleFileServer` untuk static file serving. Dokumentasi JDK 21 menjelaskan simple file server terdiri dari `HttpServer`, `HttpHandler` yang melayani file dari directory, dan output filter. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/jdk.httpserver/com/sun/net/httpserver/SimpleFileServer.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="94z61u"
import com.sun.net.httpserver.SimpleFileServer;

import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.nio.file.Path;

public class StaticServer {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        var server = SimpleFileServer.createFileServer(
            new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8080),
            Path.of("public"),
            SimpleFileServer.OutputLevel.INFO
        );

        server.start();
    }
}
```

Access:

```text id="kfbomv"
http://localhost:8080/
```

---

# 31. `jwebserver` Tool

Run static server from command line:

```bash id="xb4twj"
jwebserver
```

Specify port and directory:

```bash id="tcv287"
jwebserver -p 8080 -d public
```

Use case:

```text id="5gmzzm"
quick static file server
local testing
share generated docs locally
frontend static preview
ad-hoc file serving
```

JEP 408 describes `jwebserver` as a command-line tool for a minimal web server that serves static files only, with no CGI or servlet-like functionality. ([OpenJDK](https://openjdk.org/jeps/408?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 32. Static Handler via SimpleFileServer

Instead of creating full file server:

```java id="ojq73c"
HttpHandler fileHandler = SimpleFileServer.createFileHandler(
    Path.of("public")
);

server.createContext("/", fileHandler);
```

This is useful when mixing static and dynamic endpoints:

```java id="a9w9qh"
server.createContext("/api/health", safe(this::health));
server.createContext("/", SimpleFileServer.createFileHandler(Path.of("public")));
```

---

# 33. HttpHandlers Utility

`HttpHandlers` provides useful handler implementations such as static response handler and conditional handler. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/jdk.httpserver/com/sun/net/httpserver/HttpHandlers.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Static response example conceptually:

```java id="ojxayp"
HttpHandler handler = HttpHandlers.of(
    200,
    Headers.of("Content-Type", "text/plain"),
    "OK\n"
);
```

If your exact JDK version supports the API shape above, this is convenient for simple static responses. Otherwise, a small `Response.text(...)` helper is enough.

---

# 34. HTTPS Server

`HttpsServer` supports HTTPS and requires `HttpsConfigurator` for SSL configuration. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/jdk.httpserver/com/sun/net/httpserver/HttpsServer.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Skeleton:

```java id="a8j621"
HttpsServer server = HttpsServer.create(
    new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8443),
    0
);

SSLContext sslContext = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS");

// initialize sslContext with KeyManager[] and TrustManager[]
sslContext.init(keyManagers, trustManagers, new SecureRandom());

server.setHttpsConfigurator(new HttpsConfigurator(sslContext));

server.createContext("/health", safe(exchange -> {
    Response.json(exchange, 200, "{\"status\":\"UP\"}");
}));

server.setExecutor(Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor());
server.start();
```

Practical note:

```text id="pk25cs"
HTTPS setup butuh keystore/certificate.
Untuk production/public-facing service, biasanya lebih baik TLS termination dilakukan oleh reverse proxy/load balancer.
```

```java id="r42y49"
server.createContext("/health", safe(exchange -> {
    if (!"GET".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET");
        Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
        return;
    }

    Response.json(exchange, 200, """
        {"status":"UP"}
        """);
}));
```

# 36. Readiness Endpoint

```java id="5v3bv2"
server.createContext("/ready", safe(exchange -> {
    boolean databaseOk = checkDatabase();
    boolean downstreamOk = checkDownstream();

    if (databaseOk && downstreamOk) {
        Response.json(exchange, 200, "{\"status\":\"READY\"}");
    } else {
        Response.json(exchange, 503, "{\"status\":\"NOT_READY\"}");
    }
}));
```

Rule:

```text id="e90iox"
Health = process hidup.
Readiness = siap menerima traffic.
```

# 37. Minimal JSON API Example

```java id="hxegp9"
public final class OrderServer {
    private final Map<String, String> orders = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        new OrderServer().start();
    }

    public void start() throws IOException {
        HttpServer server = HttpServer.create(
            new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 8080),
            0
        );

        server.setExecutor(Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor());

        server.createContext("/orders", safe(this::orders));

        server.start();

        System.out.println("Order server running on http://localhost:8080");
    }

    private void orders(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        switch (exchange.getRequestMethod()) {
            case "GET" -> listOrders(exchange);
            case "POST" -> createOrder(exchange);
            default -> {
                exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET, POST");
                Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
            }
        }
    }

    private void listOrders(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        String json = orders.entrySet().stream()
            .map(entry -> """
                {"id":%s,"status":%s}
                """.formatted(
                    jsonString(entry.getKey()),
                    jsonString(entry.getValue())
                ))
            .collect(Collectors.joining(",", "[", "]"));

        Response.json(exchange, 200, json);
    }

    private void createOrder(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        byte[] body = readLimited(exchange.getRequestBody(), 1_000_000);

        String id = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

        orders.put(id, "CREATED");

        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Location", "/orders/" + id);

        Response.json(
            exchange,
            201,
            """
            {"id":%s,"status":"CREATED"}
            """.formatted(jsonString(id))
        );
    }
}
```

# 38. Testing with Embedded Server

```java id="d3w2td"
HttpServer server = HttpServer.create(
    new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 0),
    0
);

server.createContext("/test", exchange -> {
    Response.json(exchange, 200, "{\"ok\":true}");
});

server.start();

int port = server.getAddress().getPort();

try {
    HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();

    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
        .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/test"))
        .GET()
        .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(
        request,
        HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
    );

    assert response.statusCode() == 200;
} finally {
    server.stop(0);
}
```

Use case:

```text id="269ajf"
test HTTP client
mock downstream
contract-ish local stub
integration test without external dependency
```

# 39. Mock Downstream Server Pattern

```java id="krna5k"
public final class StubServer implements AutoCloseable {
    private final HttpServer server;

    public StubServer() throws IOException {
        this.server = HttpServer.create(
            new InetSocketAddress("localhost", 0),
            0
        );

        server.setExecutor(Executors.newCachedThreadPool());
    }

    public URI baseUri() {
        return URI.create("http://localhost:" + server.getAddress().getPort());
    }

    public void stubJson(String path, int status, String json) {
        server.createContext(path, exchange -> {
            Response.json(exchange, status, json);
        });
    }

    public void start() {
        server.start();
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        server.stop(0);
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="pnlbsb"
try (StubServer stub = new StubServer()) {
    stub.stubJson("/customer/123", 200, "{\"id\":\"123\"}");
    stub.start();

    URI base = stub.baseUri();

    // test client using base
}
```

# 40. Request Logging Pattern

```java id="1t05z9"
public final class AccessLogFilter extends Filter {
    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        long start = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            chain.doFilter(exchange);
        } finally {
            long durationMs =
                Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - start).toMillis();

            System.out.printf(
                "method=%s path=%s remote=%s durationMs=%d%n",
                exchange.getRequestMethod(),
                exchange.getRequestURI().getPath(),
                exchange.getRemoteAddress(),
                durationMs
            );
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Access log";
    }
}
```

Production-style rule:

```text id="fgdnbg"
Log method, path template, status if captured, duration, requestId.
Do not log Authorization/Cookie/body by default.
```

---

# 41. Capturing Status Code in Filter

Because status is sent by handler, create a response utility that records status:

```java id="h7e6qj"
public final class ExchangeAttributes {
    public static final String STATUS = "status";

    private ExchangeAttributes() {}

    public static void status(HttpExchange exchange, int status) {
        exchange.setAttribute(STATUS, status);
    }

    public static int status(HttpExchange exchange) {
        Object value = exchange.getAttribute(STATUS);
        return value instanceof Integer i ? i : 0;
    }
}
```

In response helper:

```java id="vxuotk"
ExchangeAttributes.status(exchange, status);
exchange.sendResponseHeaders(status, bytes.length);
```

In log filter:

```java id="bydpze"
int status = ExchangeAttributes.status(exchange);
```

---

# 42. Body Size Filter

```java id="vpp1dx"
public final class ContentLengthLimitFilter extends Filter {
    private final long maxBytes;

    public ContentLengthLimitFilter(long maxBytes) {
        this.maxBytes = maxBytes;
    }

    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        String contentLengthHeader =
            exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("Content-Length");

        if (contentLengthHeader != null) {
            long contentLength = Long.parseLong(contentLengthHeader);

            if (contentLength > maxBytes) {
                Response.json(
                    exchange,
                    413,
                    "{\"code\":\"PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE\",\"message\":\"Payload too large\"}"
                );
                return;
            }
        }

        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Content-Length limit";
    }
}
```

Still enforce limit while reading body, because clients may omit or lie about `Content-Length`.

---

# 43. Security Headers Filter

```java id="ghy5ej"
public final class SecurityHeadersFilter extends Filter {
    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        Headers headers = exchange.getResponseHeaders();

        headers.set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");

        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Security headers";
    }
}
```

# 44. Router Pattern

For more than a few endpoints, build a tiny router.

```java id="4u8hgv"
public record RouteKey(String method, String path) {}
```

```java id="4cfkst"
public final class Router implements HttpHandler {
    private final Map<RouteKey, HttpHandler> routes = new HashMap<>();

    public Router get(String path, HttpHandler handler) {
        routes.put(new RouteKey("GET", path), handler);
        return this;
    }

    public Router post(String path, HttpHandler handler) {
        routes.put(new RouteKey("POST", path), handler);
        return this;
    }

    @Override
    public void handle(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        RouteKey key = new RouteKey(
            exchange.getRequestMethod(),
            exchange.getRequestURI().getPath()
        );

        HttpHandler handler = routes.get(key);

        if (handler == null) {
            Response.json(
                exchange,
                404,
                "{\"code\":\"NOT_FOUND\",\"message\":\"Not found\"}"
            );
            return;
        }

        handler.handle(exchange);
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="vwtg33"
Router router = new Router()
    .get("/health", safe(this::health))
    .post("/orders", safe(this::createOrder));

server.createContext("/", router);
```

Warning:

```text id="kpqam7"
Ini exact path matching.
Untuk path variable seperti /orders/{id}, perlu matcher tambahan.
```

---

# 45. Path Variable Router Sketch

```java id="7kfo5b"
public static Optional<String> matchOneVariable(
    String patternPrefix,
    String path
) {
    if (!path.startsWith(patternPrefix)) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    String rest = path.substring(patternPrefix.length());

    if (rest.isBlank() || rest.contains("/")) {
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    return Optional.of(URLDecoder.decode(rest, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
}
```

Use:

```java id="gppiw1"
String path = exchange.getRequestURI().getPath();

if ("GET".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
    Optional<String> id = matchOneVariable("/orders/", path);

    if (id.isPresent()) {
        getOrder(exchange, id.get());
        return;
    }
}

Response.json(exchange, 404, "{\"code\":\"NOT_FOUND\"}");
```

---

# 46. Streaming Response

```java id="vajjoz"
server.createContext("/events", safe(exchange -> {
    exchange.getResponseHeaders()
        .set("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");

    exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, 0);

    try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            output.write(("event " + i + "\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            output.flush();

            Thread.sleep(1000);
        }
    }
}));
```

Handle interruption:

```java id="h7q3g6"
catch (InterruptedException e) {
    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
}
```

---

# 47. File Download Handler

```java id="hhubm4"
public static HttpHandler downloadFile(Path file) {
    return safe(exchange -> {
        if (!"GET".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
            exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET");
            Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
            return;
        }

        if (!Files.isRegularFile(file)) {
            Response.text(exchange, 404, "Not Found\n");
            return;
        }

        String contentType = Files.probeContentType(file);

        if (contentType == null) {
            contentType = "application/octet-stream";
        }

        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", contentType);
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, Files.size(file));

        try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
            Files.copy(file, output);
        }
    });
}
```

---

# 48. Safe Static File Handler Sketch

If not using `SimpleFileServer`, validate path carefully.

```java id="0py9j4"
public final class SafeStaticFileHandler implements HttpHandler {
    private final Path root;

    public SafeStaticFileHandler(Path root) throws IOException {
        this.root = root.toRealPath();
    }

    @Override
    public void handle(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        if (!"GET".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
            exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET");
            Response.text(exchange, 405, "Method Not Allowed\n");
            return;
        }

        String rawPath = exchange.getRequestURI().getPath();

        if (rawPath.equals("/")) {
            rawPath = "/index.html";
        }

        String decoded = URLDecoder.decode(rawPath, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        Path target = root.resolve(decoded.substring(1)).normalize();

        if (!target.startsWith(root)) {
            Response.text(exchange, 403, "Forbidden\n");
            return;
        }

        if (!Files.isRegularFile(target)) {
            Response.text(exchange, 404, "Not Found\n");
            return;
        }

        String contentType = Files.probeContentType(target);

        if (contentType == null) {
            contentType = "application/octet-stream";
        }

        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", contentType);
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, Files.size(target));

        try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
            Files.copy(target, output);
        }
    }
}
```

Security rule:

```text id="nav4re"
Never serve user-controlled path without normalize + startsWith(root).
Be careful with symlinks.
```

---

# 49. Rate Limit Sketch

Simple per-IP counter:

```java id="3jgf5c"
public final class SimpleRateLimitFilter extends Filter {
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<String, LongAdder> counters =
        new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private final int maxRequests;

    public SimpleRateLimitFilter(int maxRequests) {
        this.maxRequests = maxRequests;
    }

    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        String remoteIp = exchange.getRemoteAddress()
            .getAddress()
            .getHostAddress();

        LongAdder counter = counters.computeIfAbsent(
            remoteIp,
            ignored -> new LongAdder()
        );

        counter.increment();

        if (counter.sum() > maxRequests) {
            Response.json(
                exchange,
                429,
                "{\"code\":\"RATE_LIMITED\",\"message\":\"Too many requests\"}"
            );
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Simple rate limit";
    }
}
```

Warning:

```text id="nulcfg"
Ini hanya sketch.
Production rate limiting butuh time window, cleanup, proxy-aware IP, dan distributed strategy.
```

---

# 50. Handling OPTIONS

```java id="v0s726"
if ("OPTIONS".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
    exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Allow", "GET, POST, OPTIONS");
    exchange.sendResponseHeaders(204, -1);
    exchange.close();
    return;
}
```

---

# 51. HEAD Request

HEAD should return headers like GET but no body.

```java id="me6o6f"
if ("HEAD".equals(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
    byte[] bytes = "Hello\n".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

    exchange.getResponseHeaders()
        .set("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");

    exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, -1);
    exchange.close();
    return;
}
```

Practical note:

```text id="jrcc2z"
If you implement HEAD, avoid writing response body.
```

---

# 52. Form URL Encoded Parsing

```java id="eq2xl6"
public static Map<String, List<String>> parseFormUrlEncoded(String body) {
    return parseQuery(body);
}
```

Use:

```java id="di318o"
String contentType = exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("Content-Type");

if (!"application/x-www-form-urlencoded".equalsIgnoreCase(contentType)) {
    Response.text(exchange, 415, "Unsupported Media Type\n");
    return;
}

String body = new String(
    readLimited(exchange.getRequestBody(), 100_000),
    StandardCharsets.UTF_8
);

Map<String, List<String>> form = parseFormUrlEncoded(body);
```

---

# 53. Multipart Upload

The JDK standard HTTP server does **not** provide high-level multipart parsing.

Options:

```text id="6znqfj"
1. Avoid multipart for simple tools.
2. Use application/octet-stream upload.
3. Use external library for multipart.
4. Use framework if production API needs uploads.
```

For simple binary upload:

```java id="tezdh0"
try (InputStream input = exchange.getRequestBody()) {
    Files.copy(input, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
}
```

But still enforce size limit.

---

# 54. Metrics Sketch

```java id="5yhm7z"
public final class MetricsFilter extends Filter {
    private final LongAdder requests = new LongAdder();

    @Override
    public void doFilter(HttpExchange exchange, Chain chain)
            throws IOException {
        requests.increment();
        chain.doFilter(exchange);
    }

    public long requestCount() {
        return requests.sum();
    }

    @Override
    public String description() {
        return "Metrics";
    }
}
```

Expose:

```java id="3r4h67"
MetricsFilter metrics = new MetricsFilter();

server.createContext("/metrics", safe(exchange -> {
    Response.text(exchange, 200, "requests_total " + metrics.requestCount() + "\n");
}));
```

---

# 55. Server Lifecycle Wrapper

```java id="uvr2xs"
public final class EmbeddedHttpServer implements AutoCloseable {
    private final HttpServer server;
    private final ExecutorService executor;

    public EmbeddedHttpServer(int port) throws IOException {
        this.server = HttpServer.create(
            new InetSocketAddress("localhost", port),
            0
        );

        this.executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(20);

        server.setExecutor(executor);

        server.createContext("/health", safe(exchange ->
            Response.json(exchange, 200, "{\"status\":\"UP\"}")
        ));
    }

    public void start() {
        server.start();
    }

    public int port() {
        return server.getAddress().getPort();
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        server.stop(5);
        executor.shutdown();
    }
}
```

Use:

```java id="eefpsr"
try (EmbeddedHttpServer server = new EmbeddedHttpServer(0)) {
    server.start();
    System.out.println("port=" + server.port());
}
```

Port `0` means OS chooses a free port.

---

# 56. Production Caveats

The built-in HTTP server is useful, but intentionally simple.

Missing or limited compared to real web frameworks:

```text id="h601bd"
advanced routing
request body binding
JSON serialization/deserialization
validation framework
OpenAPI generation
middleware ecosystem
observability integrations
compression negotiation
multipart parser
session management
advanced auth/authz
rate limiting
structured access log
robust static asset pipeline
HTTP/2 server features depending on implementation
container integration
Jakarta Servlet/JAX-RS features
```

Use it as:

```text id="kkvxgr"
embedded utility server
test stub server
local file server
simple internal endpoint
learning tool
```

For enterprise REST API, prefer Jakarta REST / Spring / Quarkus / Micronaut / Helidon / Vert.x depending stack.

---

# 57. Security Checklist

```text id="az2mjm"
[ ] Bind to localhost unless external access is intended.
[ ] Do not expose admin/debug endpoints publicly.
[ ] Add authentication for sensitive endpoints.
[ ] Use HTTPS or reverse proxy TLS if crossing network.
[ ] Do not log Authorization/Cookie/body with secrets.
[ ] Limit request body size.
[ ] Validate Content-Type.
[ ] Normalize and validate file paths.
[ ] Avoid serving arbitrary filesystem paths.
[ ] Set security headers where useful.
[ ] Handle CORS deliberately.
[ ] Do not use wildcard CORS with credentials.
[ ] Add timeout/resource limits at infrastructure level if public.
```

---

# 58. Reliability Checklist

```text id="wjtq8q"
[ ] Explicit executor set.
[ ] Handler catches exceptions.
[ ] Response body closed.
[ ] Request body size bounded.
[ ] Blocking downstream calls have timeout.
[ ] Server.stop used on shutdown.
[ ] Executor shutdown handled.
[ ] Large responses streamed.
[ ] Static file paths validated.
[ ] Health/readiness endpoint exists if used operationally.
```

---

# 59. Observability Checklist

```text id="7a1scj"
[ ] Access log has method/path/status/duration.
[ ] Request ID generated or propagated.
[ ] Errors logged without leaking secrets.
[ ] Metrics endpoint or counters exist if needed.
[ ] Thread names/executor known.
[ ] Remote address logged carefully.
[ ] Do not log full query if it may contain secrets.
```

---

# 60. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem |
|---|---|
| No explicit executor | unpredictable/non-obvious concurrency model |
| Handler throws uncaught exception | broken response / noisy failure |
| Not closing response body | resource leak / hanging client |
| `readAllBytes()` on unbounded request body | memory attack |
| Binding to `0.0.0.0` accidentally | public exposure |
| No auth on admin endpoint | security risk |
| Serving files without path validation | path traversal |
| Logging full headers/body | secret/PII leak |
| Using this as full production framework | missing framework features |
| No graceful shutdown | dropped requests |
| No timeout on downstream call | handler thread hangs |
| Manual JSON escaping for real API | correctness/security risk |
| Wildcard CORS everywhere | browser security issue |

---

# 61. Decision Matrix

| Need | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Quick static file server | `jwebserver` |
| Programmatic static file server | `SimpleFileServer` |
| Mock HTTP server in tests | `HttpServer` |
| Simple local API | `HttpServer` |
| HTTPS embedded toy/internal | `HttpsServer` |
| Complex REST API | Jakarta REST / Spring / Quarkus / Micronaut |
| Multipart upload API | Framework/library |
| Production public web app | Real web framework/server |
| Admin endpoint local-only | `HttpServer` bound to localhost |
| Serve generated docs locally | `jwebserver` / `SimpleFileServer` |

---

# 62. Compact Summary

```text id="3u7cjy"
Module
- jdk.httpserver

Package
- com.sun.net.httpserver

HttpServer
- simple embedded HTTP server
- bind address + port
- createContext(path, handler)
- setExecutor(...)
- start()
- stop(delay)

HttpsServer
- HTTPS version
- needs SSLContext + HttpsConfigurator

HttpHandler
- handles request
- one handle(HttpExchange) method

HttpExchange
- request method, URI, headers, body
- response headers, status, body
- must send headers and close body/exchange

Filter
- cross-cutting behavior
- logging, CORS, auth, request ID, security headers

Authenticator
- built-in auth hook
- BasicAuthenticator available

SimpleFileServer
- standard static file server API
- useful for local static serving

jwebserver
- command-line static file server
- minimal; no servlet/CGI

Best Use
- testing
- mocks
- local dev
- simple internal tools
- small embedded endpoints

Avoid For
- complex production REST API
- advanced security/auth
- complex routing
- high-scale public web service
```

Kesimpulan praktis: **Java standard library HTTP server sangat berguna untuk test stub, local tool, mock downstream, health/debug endpoint, dan static file serving sederhana.** Untuk production enterprise API, treat it as lightweight embedded infrastructure, bukan pengganti Jakarta REST atau framework web penuh.

---

- [JEP 408: Simple Web Server](https://openjdk.org/jeps/408?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/12/2026, 9:18:02 PM

Berikut **cheat sheet Java Utilities & Data Processing** untuk **Java 17+**: **Java Time API, Regular Expression, Math, Arbitrary Precision, Localization, Internationalization, dan Data Compression**.

# Java Utilities & Data Processing Cheat Sheet

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text id="uro6hg"
Utilities & Data Processing
├── Temporal
│   └── java.time
├── Pattern Matching Text
│   └── java.util.regex
├── Math
│   └── Math, StrictMath, Random
├── Arbitrary Precision
│   └── BigDecimal, BigInteger, MathContext
├── Localization / Internationalization
│   └── Locale, NumberFormat, DateTimeFormatter, ResourceBundle, MessageFormat
└── Data Compression
    └── java.util.zip
```

Rule utama:

```text id="d4nfmx"
Time       -> always clarify timezone and business meaning
Regex      -> compile reusable patterns
Math       -> know precision and overflow risk
BigDecimal -> use for money/exact decimal
I18N       -> never hardcode user-facing locale assumptions
Compression-> stream large data, do not load all into memory
```

Java SE sendiri menyediakan core APIs untuk text, date, number, message formatting, dan general-purpose computing di module `java.base`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Java Time API

## 2.1 Type Selection

| Need | Type |
|---|---|
| Timestamp global | `Instant` |
| Date only | `LocalDate` |
| Time only | `LocalTime` |
| Date-time tanpa timezone | `LocalDateTime` |
| Date-time dengan offset | `OffsetDateTime` |
| Date-time dengan timezone rules | `ZonedDateTime` |
| Timeout / TTL / SLA | `Duration` |
| Calendar amount | `Period` |
| Billing month | `YearMonth` |
| Timezone region | `ZoneId` |
| Fixed UTC offset | `ZoneOffset` |
| Testable current time | `Clock` |

`LocalDateTime` adalah immutable date-time tanpa timezone dalam ISO calendar system; ini penting karena `LocalDateTime` bukan timestamp global. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/time/LocalDateTime.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2.2 Instant

```java id="0rj2sj"
Instant now = Instant.now();

Instant createdAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-12T10:15:30Z");
```

Use case:

```text id="fyhoax"
created_at
updated_at
audit timestamp
event timestamp
message timestamp
distributed log timestamp
```

Convert to timezone:

```java id="n8xzsi"
ZonedDateTime jakartaTime =
    createdAt.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

---

## 2.3 LocalDate

```java id="zlgpto"
LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();

LocalDate dueDate = LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 12);
```

Use case:

```text id="a5oggq"
birth date
business date
due date
reporting date
holiday
billing date
```

Compare:

```java id="kzpb7c"
boolean overdue = dueDate.isBefore(LocalDate.now());
```

---

## 2.4 ZonedDateTime

```java id="qhaqfr"
ZoneId zone = ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta");

ZonedDateTime nowJakarta = ZonedDateTime.now(zone);
```

Convert from instant:

```java id="tqnrab"
Instant instant = Instant.now();

ZonedDateTime userTime =
    instant.atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));
```

Rule:

```text id="xquj7x"
Store event time as Instant.
Display event time using user's ZoneId.
```

---

## 2.5 Duration vs Period

```java id="tmhktm"
Duration timeout = Duration.ofSeconds(30);
Duration ttl = Duration.ofMinutes(10);

Period oneMonth = Period.ofMonths(1);
Period oneYear = Period.ofYears(1);
```

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Timeout | `Duration` |
| Cache TTL | `Duration` |
| SLA in hours/minutes | `Duration` |
| Calendar month/year | `Period` |
| Human age | `Period` |
| Billing cycle | `Period` / `YearMonth` |

---

## 2.6 YearMonth

```java id="hz1gf7"
YearMonth billingMonth = YearMonth.of(2026, 7);

LocalDate start = billingMonth.atDay(1);
LocalDate endExclusive = billingMonth.plusMonths(1).atDay(1);
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="uvdwd1"
invoice month
subscription month
salary month
reporting month
```

---

## 2.7 Clock for Testability

Buruk:

```java id="fylz6r"
Instant now = Instant.now();
```

Lebih testable:

```java id="v4be8q"
public final class OrderService {
    private final Clock clock;

    public OrderService(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public void submit(Order order) {
        order.submit(clock.instant());
    }
}
```

Test:

```java id="mguapa"
Clock fixedClock = Clock.fixed(
    Instant.parse("2026-07-12T00:00:00Z"),
    ZoneOffset.UTC
);
```

---

## 2.8 DateTimeFormatter

```java id="hit4bk"
DateTimeFormatter formatter =
    DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
        .withLocale(Locale.US);

String text = LocalDateTime.now().format(formatter);
```

Parse:

```java id="11vsev"
LocalDate date = LocalDate.parse("2026-07-12");

Instant instant = Instant.parse("2026-07-12T10:15:30Z");
```

Custom parse:

```java id="2a1qx2"
DateTimeFormatter formatter =
    DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd/MM/yyyy");

LocalDate date = LocalDate.parse("12/07/2026", formatter);
```

`DateTimeFormatter` adalah formatter/parser untuk date-time objects dan menyediakan formatter ISO predefined seperti ISO date-time dengan offset. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/22/docs/api/java.base/java/time/format/DateTimeFormatter.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2.9 Temporal Adjusters

```java id="abmk8d"
LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();

LocalDate firstDay =
    today.with(TemporalAdjusters.firstDayOfMonth());

LocalDate lastDay =
    today.with(TemporalAdjusters.lastDayOfMonth());

LocalDate nextMonday =
    today.with(TemporalAdjusters.next(DayOfWeek.MONDAY));
```

Use case:

```text id="e8uafc"
month-end processing
billing cycle
calendar schedule
reporting period
```

---

## 2.10 Date Range Pattern

```java id="d0os6w"
public record DateRange(
    LocalDate startInclusive,
    LocalDate endExclusive
) {
    public DateRange {
        if (!startInclusive.isBefore(endExclusive)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid date range");
        }
    }

    public boolean contains(LocalDate date) {
        return !date.isBefore(startInclusive)
            && date.isBefore(endExclusive);
    }

    public boolean overlaps(DateRange other) {
        return startInclusive.isBefore(other.endExclusive)
            && other.startInclusive.isBefore(endExclusive);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="0fcghl"
Prefer [startInclusive, endExclusive).
Avoid inclusive end timestamp for date-time ranges.
```

---

# 3. Regular Expression

## 3.1 Basic Pattern

```java id="5ejhai"
Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile("^[A-Z]{2}\\d{4}$");

Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher("AB1234");

boolean valid = matcher.matches();
```

`Pattern` adalah compiled representation dari regular expression; regex string dikompilasi menjadi `Pattern`, lalu `Pattern` membuat `Matcher` untuk mencocokkan character sequence. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/util/regex/Pattern.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3.2 `matches()` vs `find()`

```java id="9rckxp"
Pattern p = Pattern.compile("\\d+");

p.matcher("123").matches();     // true
p.matcher("abc123").matches();  // false

p.matcher("abc123").find();     // true
```

| Method | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `matches()` | seluruh input harus match |
| `find()` | cari match di dalam input |
| `lookingAt()` | match dari awal input |

---

## 3.3 Capture Group

```java id="bjtpmx"
Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile("order-(\\d+)");

Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher("order-123");

if (matcher.matches()) {
    String id = matcher.group(1); // 123
}
```

Named group:

```java id="adodnw"
Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile("order-(?<id>\\d+)");

Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher("order-123");

if (matcher.matches()) {
    String id = matcher.group("id");
}
```

---

## 3.4 Replace

```java id="volj6c"
String normalized = input.replaceAll("\\s+", " ");
```

With compiled pattern:

```java id="c1zlw0"
private static final Pattern MULTIPLE_SPACES =
    Pattern.compile("\\s+");

String normalized =
    MULTIPLE_SPACES.matcher(input).replaceAll(" ");
```

Rule:

```text id="g6hm2d"
Compile regex once if reused often.
Avoid recompiling pattern in hot path.
```

---

## 3.5 Split

```java id="g9tzc4"
String[] parts = "a,b,c".split(",");
```

Important: `split()` uses regex.

```java id="2c69tr"
"a.b".split(".");    // wrong: "." means any char
"a.b".split("\\.");  // correct
```

Keep empty values:

```java id="t75a5v"
"a,,b,".split(",", -1);
```

---

## 3.6 Common Regex

| Need | Regex |
|---|---|
| digits | `\\d+` |
| whitespace | `\\s+` |
| word chars | `\\w+` |
| UUID rough | `[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}` |
| simple code | `^[A-Z]{2}\\d{4}$` |
| comma split | `,` |
| dot literal | `\\.` |
| start/end | `^...$` |

---

## 3.7 Regex Flags

```java id="sbrfo1"
Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile(
    "hello",
    Pattern.CASE_INSENSITIVE
);
```

Common flags:

| Flag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `CASE_INSENSITIVE` | case-insensitive |
| `MULTILINE` | `^` and `$` per line |
| `DOTALL` | `.` matches newline |
| `UNICODE_CASE` | Unicode-aware case folding |
| `COMMENTS` | allow whitespace/comments in regex |

---

## 3.8 Regex Safety

Danger:

```text id="edkq3z"
catastrophic backtracking
regex DoS
overly permissive validation
regex used as parser for complex formats
```

Bad:

```java id="xoc6zh"
Pattern.compile("(a+)+$");
```

Against long malicious input, this can be expensive.

Rule:

```text id="i8iqi4"
Limit input length before regex.
Avoid nested ambiguous quantifiers.
Do not parse JSON/XML/HTML with regex.
```

---

# 4. Math

## 4.1 `Math`

```java id="q8rsol"
int max = Math.max(a, b);
int min = Math.min(a, b);

double sqrt = Math.sqrt(16);
double pow = Math.pow(2, 10);

long rounded = Math.round(12.6);
double abs = Math.abs(-10.5);
```

`Math` menyediakan operasi numeric dasar seperti exponential, logarithm, square root, dan trigonometric functions. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/Math.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4.2 Exact Arithmetic

Primitive integer overflow diam-diam.

```java id="fb0p0g"
int overflow = Integer.MAX_VALUE + 1;
```

Gunakan exact methods:

```java id="h84tnr"
int sum = Math.addExact(a, b);
int diff = Math.subtractExact(a, b);
int product = Math.multiplyExact(a, b);

long next = Math.incrementExact(value);
```

Jika overflow:

```java id="3gibwz"
try {
    int x = Math.addExact(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 1);
} catch (ArithmeticException e) {
    // overflow detected
}
```

Use for:

```text id="62mwc5"
billing quantity
pagination offset
quota counter
financial count
capacity calculation
```

---

## 4.3 Rounding

```java id="4xk17y"
long rounded = Math.round(12.5); // 13
double floor = Math.floor(12.9); // 12.0
double ceil = Math.ceil(12.1);   // 13.0
```

For money, use `BigDecimal` rounding, not `double`.

---

## 4.4 Random

Basic:

```java id="6ngmwj"
Random random = new Random();

int value = random.nextInt(100); // 0..99
```

Better for concurrency:

```java id="7v7olb"
int value = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(100);
```

For cryptographic random:

```java id="b7jsgd"
SecureRandom secureRandom = new SecureRandom();

byte[] token = new byte[32];
secureRandom.nextBytes(token);
```

Rule:

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Simple simulation | `Random` |
| Concurrent random | `ThreadLocalRandom` |
| Streams/statistics | `SplittableRandom` |
| Token/password/security | `SecureRandom` |

---

## 4.5 StrictMath

```java id="d1hfjz"
double x = StrictMath.sqrt(2);
```

`StrictMath` juga menyediakan operasi numeric dasar seperti exponential, logarithm, square root, dan trigonometric functions. Gunakan saat deterministic floating behavior lintas platform lebih penting daripada performance. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/lang/StrictMath.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Arbitrary Precision

## 5.1 BigDecimal

Gunakan untuk decimal exact: money, billing, tax, pricing.

```java id="b715d4"
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal("100.50");
```

Atau:

```java id="5zyaf6"
BigDecimal amount = BigDecimal.valueOf(100.50);
```

Hindari:

```java id="99ttel"
BigDecimal amount = new BigDecimal(100.50);
```

`BigDecimal` menyediakan arithmetic, scale manipulation, rounding, comparison, hashing, dan format conversion. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/math/BigDecimal.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.2 BigDecimal Operations

```java id="x2em6k"
BigDecimal total = price.add(tax);

BigDecimal remaining = balance.subtract(payment);

BigDecimal subtotal = unitPrice.multiply(quantity);

BigDecimal average = total.divide(
    count,
    2,
    RoundingMode.HALF_UP
);
```

Division without rounding can fail:

```java id="3vaxay"
BigDecimal.ONE.divide(new BigDecimal("3")); // ArithmeticException
```

Use:

```java id="0imvqx"
BigDecimal.ONE.divide(
    new BigDecimal("3"),
    4,
    RoundingMode.HALF_UP
);
```

---

## 5.3 BigDecimal Compare

```java id="i6xj8o"
BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("1.0");
BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("1.00");

a.equals(b);        // false
a.compareTo(b);     // 0
```

Rule:

```text id="jnntcq"
For numeric equality, use compareTo().
For exact scale-sensitive equality, use equals().
```

---

## 5.4 Scale and Rounding

```java id="rwotbs"
BigDecimal value = new BigDecimal("123.4567");

BigDecimal rounded =
    value.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
```

Money value object:

```java id="iwlkn1"
public record Money(BigDecimal amount, Currency currency) {
    public Money {
        Objects.requireNonNull(amount);
        Objects.requireNonNull(currency);

        amount = amount.setScale(
            currency.getDefaultFractionDigits(),
            RoundingMode.HALF_UP
        );
    }

    public Money add(Money other) {
        if (!currency.equals(other.currency)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Currency mismatch");
        }

        return new Money(amount.add(other.amount), currency);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="xmctfi"
Rounding mode is business/legal rule, not developer preference.
```

---

## 5.5 MathContext

```java id="d3q4z5"
MathContext mc = new MathContext(
    10,
    RoundingMode.HALF_UP
);

BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, mc);
```

Use for:

```text id="6angmn"
scientific decimal
controlled precision calculation
calculation pipeline with precision policy
```

---

## 5.6 BigInteger

```java id="rx3zqc"
BigInteger a = new BigInteger("999999999999999999999");
BigInteger b = new BigInteger("2");

BigInteger result = a.multiply(b);
```

Use for:

```text id="e4wba8"
very large exact integer
cryptography-related math
arbitrary precision counters
mathematical algorithms
```

Common:

```java id="6yuir8"
a.add(b);
a.subtract(b);
a.multiply(b);
a.divide(b);
a.mod(b);
a.pow(10);
a.gcd(b);
```

---

# 6. Localization & Internationalization

## 6.1 Locale

```java id="mm4gnk"
Locale indonesia = Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID");
Locale us = Locale.US;
Locale root = Locale.ROOT;
```

Use:

| Need | Locale |
|---|---|
| User-facing Indonesian | `Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID")` |
| System normalization | `Locale.ROOT` |
| US display | `Locale.US` |
| User preference | user-selected locale |

---

## 6.2 NumberFormat

```java id="6lcc3x"
Locale locale = Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID");

NumberFormat numberFormat =
    NumberFormat.getNumberInstance(locale);

String text = numberFormat.format(1234567.89);
```

Currency:

```java id="d7ptxc"
NumberFormat currencyFormat =
    NumberFormat.getCurrencyInstance(locale);

String text = currencyFormat.format(new BigDecimal("1234567.89"));
```

Percent:

```java id="l9l0p3"
NumberFormat percentFormat =
    NumberFormat.getPercentInstance(locale);

String text = percentFormat.format(0.125);
```

Oracle’s internationalization guide notes that `NumberFormat` can format/parse numbers independently from locale-specific conventions such as decimal separators, thousands separators, digits, currency, and percentage formatting. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/intl/internationalization-overview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6.3 Date/Time Localization

```java id="ex52ha"
Locale locale = Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID");

DateTimeFormatter formatter =
    DateTimeFormatter.ofLocalizedDate(FormatStyle.LONG)
        .withLocale(locale);

String text = LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 12).format(formatter);
```

Date-time with zone:

```java id="pqlb4l"
ZonedDateTime time =
    Instant.now().atZone(ZoneId.of("Asia/Jakarta"));

DateTimeFormatter formatter =
    DateTimeFormatter.ofLocalizedDateTime(FormatStyle.MEDIUM)
        .withLocale(Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID"));

String text = formatter.format(time);
```

---

## 6.4 MessageFormat

```java id="g0xy40"
String pattern = "Halo {0}, Anda memiliki {1} pesanan.";

String message = MessageFormat.format(
    pattern,
    "Fajar",
    3
);
```

With locale:

```java id="o2vwgn"
MessageFormat format = new MessageFormat(
    "Pada {0,date,long}, totalnya {1,number,currency}.",
    Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID")
);

String text = format.format(new Object[] {
    new Date(),
    1500000
});
```

`java.text` includes formatting/parsing, string collation, and text iteration classes; its main formatting groups include date, number, and message formatting. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/text/package-summary.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6.5 ResourceBundle

Properties file:

```text id="7doehl"
messages.properties
messages_id_ID.properties
messages_en_US.properties
```

`messages_id_ID.properties`:

```properties id="quh23h"
greeting=Halo
order.created=Pesanan berhasil dibuat
```

Java:

```java id="9z48ig"
Locale locale = Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID");

ResourceBundle bundle =
    ResourceBundle.getBundle("messages", locale);

String greeting = bundle.getString("greeting");
```

`ResourceBundle` is designed to load locale-specific resources so code can remain largely independent of locale-specific information. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/util/ResourceBundle.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6.6 Collator for Sorting

```java id="r17qaj"
Locale locale = Locale.forLanguageTag("id-ID");

Collator collator = Collator.getInstance(locale);

List<String> sorted = names.stream()
    .sorted(collator)
    .toList();
```

Use `Collator` for user-facing alphabetical sorting, not raw Unicode sorting.

---

## 6.7 Locale-Safe Lowercase

Bad:

```java id="38usyb"
String key = input.toLowerCase();
```

Better for system keys:

```java id="6u7fdr"
String key = input.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
```

For user-facing language-specific text:

```java id="5pihyi"
String text = input.toLowerCase(userLocale);
```

Rule:

```text id="w4rr45"
Locale.ROOT for protocol/system normalization.
User locale for human-facing display.
```

---

## 6.8 I18N Checklist

```text id="w9ynt6"
[ ] User-facing text in ResourceBundle?
[ ] Number/currency formatted with Locale?
[ ] Date/time displayed with user Locale and ZoneId?
[ ] System keys normalized with Locale.ROOT?
[ ] Sorting user-visible text with Collator?
[ ] Avoid string concatenation for translatable messages?
[ ] Pluralization considered?
[ ] Currency is explicit, not inferred blindly?
```

---

# 7. Data Compression

## 7.1 GZIP Compress

```java id="y31kja"
public static byte[] gzip(byte[] input) throws IOException {
    ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

    try (GZIPOutputStream gzip = new GZIPOutputStream(output)) {
        gzip.write(input);
    }

    return output.toByteArray();
}
```

`GZIPOutputStream` implements a stream filter for writing compressed data in GZIP format. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/util/zip/GZIPOutputStream.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7.2 GZIP Decompress

```java id="brnfbv"
public static byte[] gunzip(byte[] compressed) throws IOException {
    try (
        ByteArrayInputStream input = new ByteArrayInputStream(compressed);
        GZIPInputStream gzip = new GZIPInputStream(input);
        ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream()
    ) {
        gzip.transferTo(output);
        return output.toByteArray();
    }
}
```

---

## 7.3 GZIP File

Compress file:

```java id="k3h8r6"
public static void gzipFile(Path source, Path target) throws IOException {
    try (
        InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(source);
        OutputStream output = Files.newOutputStream(target);
        GZIPOutputStream gzip = new GZIPOutputStream(output)
    ) {
        input.transferTo(gzip);
    }
}
```

Decompress file:

```java id="z6p8b7"
public static void gunzipFile(Path source, Path target) throws IOException {
    try (
        InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(source);
        GZIPInputStream gzip = new GZIPInputStream(input);
        OutputStream output = Files.newOutputStream(target)
    ) {
        gzip.transferTo(output);
    }
}
```

---

## 7.4 ZIP Create

```java id="2f7r8n"
public static void zipFiles(Path zipPath, List<Path> files)
        throws IOException {
    try (ZipOutputStream zip = new ZipOutputStream(
        Files.newOutputStream(zipPath),
        StandardCharsets.UTF_8
    )) {
        for (Path file : files) {
            ZipEntry entry = new ZipEntry(file.getFileName().toString());

            zip.putNextEntry(entry);
            Files.copy(file, zip);
            zip.closeEntry();
        }
    }
}
```

`ZipOutputStream` is the standard output stream filter for writing files in ZIP format. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/index-files/index-26.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7.5 ZIP Extract Safely

```java id="sx1mqh"
public static void unzip(Path zipFile, Path targetDir)
        throws IOException {
    Files.createDirectories(targetDir);

    Path normalizedTargetDir = targetDir.toRealPath();

    try (ZipInputStream zip = new ZipInputStream(
        Files.newInputStream(zipFile),
        StandardCharsets.UTF_8
    )) {
        ZipEntry entry;

        while ((entry = zip.getNextEntry()) != null) {
            Path target = normalizedTargetDir
                .resolve(entry.getName())
                .normalize();

            if (!target.startsWith(normalizedTargetDir)) {
                throw new SecurityException(
                    "Zip entry escapes target dir: " + entry.getName()
                );
            }

            if (entry.isDirectory()) {
                Files.createDirectories(target);
            } else {
                Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
                Files.copy(zip, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
            }

            zip.closeEntry();
        }
    }
}
```

Security rule:

```text id="y4b8nz"
Always protect ZIP extraction from Zip Slip.
Normalize target path and ensure it stays under target directory.
```

---

## 7.6 Deflater / Inflater

Low-level compression:

```java id="yxipl7"
Deflater deflater = new Deflater(Deflater.BEST_COMPRESSION);
```

Usually prefer:

```text id="61ui18"
GZIPOutputStream for .gz
ZipOutputStream for .zip
```

Use `Deflater` / `Inflater` only when implementing lower-level custom compression flows.

---

## 7.7 Compression Decision Matrix

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Single stream/file compression | GZIP |
| Multiple files/archive | ZIP |
| HTTP content encoding | GZIP if negotiated |
| Custom binary protocol compression | Deflater/Inflater |
| Very large files | streaming compression |
| Security-sensitive unzip | Zip Slip validation |
| Random access archive entries | `ZipFile` |
| Simple sequential zip read | `ZipInputStream` |

---

# 8. Practical Data Processing Patterns

## 8.1 Parse → Validate → Transform

```java id="0edvgn"
List<Order> orders;

try (Stream<String> lines = Files.lines(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
    orders = lines
        .skip(1)
        .map(this::parseOrder)
        .filter(this::isValid)
        .map(this::normalizeOrder)
        .toList();
}
```

For large data, avoid collecting all:

```java id="f6vtf8"
try (BufferedReader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(path)) {
    String line;

    while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
        Order order = parseOrder(line);

        if (isValid(order)) {
            process(order);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 8.2 Batch Processing

```java id="g29fip"
List<Order> batch = new ArrayList<>(1000);

for (Order order : orders) {
    batch.add(order);

    if (batch.size() == 1000) {
        processBatch(batch);
        batch.clear();
    }
}

if (!batch.isEmpty()) {
    processBatch(batch);
}
```

Use for:

```text id="rvrsr8"
database batch insert
API bulk call
file processing
large export/import
```

---

## 8.3 Grouping

```java id="3heav1"
Map<OrderStatus, Long> countByStatus = orders.stream()
    .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
        Order::status,
        Collectors.counting()
    ));
```

---

## 8.4 Numeric Summary

```java id="uqc12a"
IntSummaryStatistics stats = orders.stream()
    .mapToInt(Order::quantity)
    .summaryStatistics();

int min = stats.getMin();
int max = stats.getMax();
double average = stats.getAverage();
long count = stats.getCount();
long sum = stats.getSum();
```

For money:

```java id="5c5oyj"
BigDecimal total = orders.stream()
    .map(Order::amount)
    .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
```

---

# 9. Common Anti-Patterns

| Area | Anti-pattern | Problem |
|---|---|---|
| Time | `LocalDateTime.now()` for audit | timezone ambiguity |
| Time | No `Clock` in time logic | hard to test |
| Regex | compile regex in hot loop | overhead |
| Regex | regex for JSON/XML | wrong tool |
| Regex | unbounded malicious input | ReDoS risk |
| Math | primitive overflow ignored | silent wrong value |
| Math | `double` for money | precision bug |
| BigDecimal | `new BigDecimal(double)` | inaccurate decimal |
| BigDecimal | divide without rounding | runtime exception |
| I18N | hardcoded text | no localization |
| I18N | lowercase without locale | locale bug |
| I18N | raw string sort | wrong user-visible order |
| Compression | unzip without validation | Zip Slip |
| Compression | load huge file into byte[] | memory spike |
| Compression | assume compressed data is safe size | zip bomb risk |

---

# 10. Production Checklist

```text id="y9pdpt"
Time
[ ] Event timestamp uses Instant?
[ ] Display uses user ZoneId?
[ ] Business date uses LocalDate?
[ ] Current time injected with Clock?
[ ] Date range uses end-exclusive?

Regex
[ ] Pattern compiled once if reused?
[ ] Input length bounded?
[ ] Catastrophic backtracking avoided?
[ ] matches/find chosen intentionally?

Math
[ ] Overflow-sensitive arithmetic uses Math.*Exact?
[ ] Floating point only used for approximate values?
[ ] Secure random uses SecureRandom?

BigDecimal / BigInteger
[ ] Money uses BigDecimal?
[ ] BigDecimal created from String/valueOf?
[ ] RoundingMode explicit?
[ ] compareTo used for numeric equality?

Localization / I18N
[ ] User-facing messages use ResourceBundle?
[ ] Number/currency uses NumberFormat?
[ ] Date/time uses locale + zone?
[ ] System keys use Locale.ROOT?
[ ] Sorting uses Collator?

Compression
[ ] Large data compressed/decompressed streaming?
[ ] ZIP extraction validates target path?
[ ] Compressed input size bounded?
[ ] Temporary files cleaned?
```

---

# 11. Compact Decision Guide

```text id="dnzm6w"
Temporal
- Instant: global timestamp
- LocalDate: business date
- ZonedDateTime: display/calendar time
- Duration: timeout/TTL
- Period: calendar amount
- Clock: testable now

Regex
- Pattern.compile: reusable regex
- Matcher.matches: full match
- Matcher.find: search inside text
- Named groups for clarity
- Limit input size

Math
- Math: common numeric functions
- StrictMath: deterministic floating behavior
- Math.*Exact: overflow detection
- SecureRandom: security tokens

Arbitrary Precision
- BigDecimal: money/exact decimal
- BigInteger: huge exact integer
- MathContext: precision policy
- RoundingMode: explicit rounding

Localization
- Locale: language/country context
- NumberFormat: locale number/currency/percent
- DateTimeFormatter: localized date/time
- MessageFormat: parameterized messages
- ResourceBundle: translated resources
- Collator: locale-aware sorting

Compression
- GZIP: single compressed stream/file
- ZIP: archive with entries
- Deflater: low-level compression
- Zip Slip protection mandatory
```

Kesimpulan praktis: **utility code yang terlihat kecil sering menjadi sumber bug besar**. Waktu harus punya timezone semantics, regex harus dibatasi dan dikompilasi benar, angka harus jelas presisi/overflow-nya, user-facing data harus locale-aware, dan compression harus streaming serta aman dari Zip Slip/zip bomb.

---

**Sources:**

- [Overview (Java SE 21 & JDK 21)](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/12/2026, 9:20:57 PM

Saya akan buat fokus defensif/production: JCA/JCE, hashing, password hashing, symmetric encryption, AEAD, asymmetric crypto, signatures, key management, TLS, random, encoding, dan anti-pattern.

Berikut **cheat sheet Java Cryptography** untuk **Java 17+**. Fokusnya defensif/production: **JCA/JCE, SecureRandom, hashing, password hashing, HMAC, AES-GCM, RSA/OAEP, digital signature, KeyStore, TLS, encoding, key management, dan anti-pattern**.

> Catatan penting: crypto yang aman biasanya bukan soal “punya algoritma”, tetapi soal **mode, nonce/IV, key lifecycle, randomness, authentication, rotation, dan threat model**.

# Java Cryptography Cheat Sheet

---

# 1. Mental Model

```text id="pdlbg8"
Java Cryptography
├── JCA / JCE
│   ├── Provider
│   ├── SecureRandom
│   ├── MessageDigest
│   ├── Mac
│   ├── Cipher
│   ├── Signature
│   ├── KeyPairGenerator
│   ├── KeyGenerator
│   ├── KeyFactory
│   ├── SecretKeyFactory
│   └── KeyStore
│
├── Data Protection
│   ├── Hashing
│   ├── Password hashing
│   ├── MAC / HMAC
│   ├── Symmetric encryption
│   ├── Asymmetric encryption
│   └── Digital signature
│
└── Key Management
    ├── Generate key
    ├── Store key
    ├── Rotate key
    ├── Destroy/expire key
    └── Audit key usage
```

JCA/JCE adalah provider-based architecture: aplikasi memanggil API seperti `Cipher`, `MessageDigest`, `Mac`, `Signature`, `SecureRandom`, dan `KeyStore`, sementara implementasi algoritmanya disediakan oleh security provider. Oracle JCA Reference Guide juga menyebut high-level classes seperti `Provider`, `Security`, `SecureRandom`, `MessageDigest`, `Signature`, `Cipher`, `Mac`, dan `KEM`. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/24/security/java-cryptography-architecture-jca-reference-guide.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Decision Matrix

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Random token / nonce / salt | `SecureRandom` |
| File checksum / integrity fingerprint | `MessageDigest` with SHA-256/SHA-512 |
| Password storage | Argon2id / bcrypt / PBKDF2, not SHA-256 |
| Message authentication | `Mac` with HMAC-SHA256/HMAC-SHA512 |
| Encrypt data | `Cipher` with AES-GCM |
| Encrypt small key material to public key | RSA-OAEP or hybrid encryption |
| Sign data | `Signature`, e.g. Ed25519 or RSA-PSS |
| Verify signature | `Signature.verify()` |
| Generate symmetric key | `KeyGenerator` |
| Generate key pair | `KeyPairGenerator` |
| Derive key from password | `SecretKeyFactory` with PBKDF2 |
| Store key/cert | `KeyStore` |
| Constant-time compare | `MessageDigest.isEqual()` |

Java’s standard algorithm names are defined separately by the Java Security Standard Algorithm Names specification; use standard names like `AES/GCM/NoPadding`, `HmacSHA256`, `SHA-256`, `PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256`, and signature/key algorithms according to provider support. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/specs/security/standard-names.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Core Classes

| Class | Package | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `SecureRandom` | `java.security` | cryptographic random |
| `MessageDigest` | `java.security` | hash/digest |
| `Mac` | `javax.crypto` | message authentication code |
| `Cipher` | `javax.crypto` | encryption/decryption |
| `Signature` | `java.security` | digital signature |
| `KeyGenerator` | `javax.crypto` | symmetric key generation |
| `KeyPairGenerator` | `java.security` | public/private key pair |
| `SecretKeyFactory` | `javax.crypto` | password-based key derivation |
| `KeyFactory` | `java.security` | key conversion |
| `KeyStore` | `java.security` | key/certificate storage |
| `CertificateFactory` | `java.security.cert` | certificate parsing |
| `Base64` | `java.util` | binary-to-text encoding |

`Cipher` is the core JCE class for encryption/decryption functionality, while `Signature` is used for digital signature algorithms that provide authentication and integrity assurance. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/javax/crypto/Cipher.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. SecureRandom

## 4.1 Generate Random Bytes

```java id="hjka5v"
SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();

byte[] bytes = new byte[32];
random.nextBytes(bytes);
```

`SecureRandom` provides a cryptographically strong random number generator; Oracle’s API documentation states that cryptographically strong random output must be non-deterministic and comply at least with statistical RNG tests such as those specified in FIPS 140-2 section 4.9.1. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/security/SecureRandom.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4.2 Generate Token

```java id="te8v92"
public static String randomToken(int bytesLength) {
    byte[] bytes = new byte[bytesLength];

    new SecureRandom().nextBytes(bytes);

    return Base64.getUrlEncoder()
        .withoutPadding()
        .encodeToString(bytes);
}
```

Use:

```java id="8eqoub"
String token = randomToken(32);
```

Common sizes:

| Purpose | Minimum Practical Size |
|---|---:|
| nonce | algorithm-specific |
| CSRF token | 16-32 bytes |
| API token | 32 bytes+ |
| session secret | 32 bytes+ |
| AES-256 key | 32 bytes |
| salt | 16 bytes+ |

---

## 4.3 Do Not Use `Random` for Crypto

Bad:

```java id="0eg3u3"
Random random = new Random();
```

Good:

```java id="6wh9qy"
SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();
```

Rule:

```text id="m9lwvm"
Use SecureRandom for secrets, tokens, keys, salts, IVs, and nonces.
Use Random/ThreadLocalRandom only for non-security use cases.
```

---

# 5. Binary Encoding

Crypto works on bytes. For transport/storage, encode bytes.

## 5.1 Base64

```java id="c5pmz2"
String encoded = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(bytes);

byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encoded);
```

URL-safe token:

```java id="k0axm1"
String token = Base64.getUrlEncoder()
    .withoutPadding()
    .encodeToString(bytes);
```

## 5.2 Hex

Java 17 has `HexFormat`.

```java id="sse5v1"
HexFormat hex = HexFormat.of();

String text = hex.formatHex(bytes);

byte[] parsed = hex.parseHex(text);
```

Decision:

| Need | Encoding |
|---|---|
| URL token | Base64 URL without padding |
| JSON field | Base64 |
| Human-readable fingerprint | Hex |
| Debug digest | Hex |
| Binary protocol | raw bytes |

---

# 6. Hashing

## 6.1 SHA-256 Digest

```java id="pt8x3e"
public static byte[] sha256(byte[] input) throws NoSuchAlgorithmException {
    MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
    return digest.digest(input);
}
```

String to hash:

```java id="cpn5xk"
byte[] hash = sha256(text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
```

Hex output:

```java id="frcv78"
String fingerprint = HexFormat.of().formatHex(hash);
```

Every Java platform implementation is required to support standard `MessageDigest` algorithms including `SHA-1` and `SHA-256`; for new security-sensitive designs, prefer SHA-256 or stronger and do not use SHA-1 for collision-resistant security. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/security/MessageDigest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6.2 Hash Use Cases

| Use Case | Hash OK? |
|---|---|
| File checksum | yes |
| Content fingerprint | yes |
| Deduplication key | yes |
| Digital signature prehash | yes, if algorithm expects |
| Password storage | no |
| Token storage lookup | maybe, with enough random token entropy |
| Message authentication | use HMAC, not plain hash |

---

## 6.3 Constant-Time Compare

Bad:

```java id="g974zk"
Arrays.equals(expected, actual);
```

Better for secret comparison:

```java id="zto7es"
boolean same = MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, actual);
```

Use for:

```text id="exw1vw"
MAC tag comparison
token hash comparison
signature-like fixed secret comparison
```

---

# 7. Password Hashing

## 7.1 Rule

```text id="nzyupc"
Never store plain passwords.
Never store SHA-256(password).
Never encrypt passwords for login storage.
Store password hash using password hashing algorithm.
```

OWASP’s Password Storage Cheat Sheet says passwords should not be stored in plain text and should be protected with strong, slow password hashing algorithms such as Argon2id, bcrypt, or PBKDF2; it also warns that fast hashes such as SHA-256 are not suitable for password storage. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Password_Storage_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7.2 Recommended Choices

| Situation | Choice |
|---|---|
| New system with library support | Argon2id |
| Legacy compatible | bcrypt |
| Java standard library only / FIPS-ish environments | PBKDF2 |
| Never | MD5/SHA-1/SHA-256 alone |

Java SE standard library includes PBKDF2 via `SecretKeyFactory`, but Argon2id and bcrypt usually require external libraries; OWASP lists Argon2id, bcrypt, and PBKDF2 as strong slow password hashing options. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Password_Storage_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7.3 PBKDF2 Password Hash

```java id="phtmcy"
public record PasswordHash(
    String algorithm,
    int iterations,
    String saltBase64,
    String hashBase64
) {}
```

Hash:

```java id="6m7ck6"
public static PasswordHash hashPassword(char[] password)
        throws GeneralSecurityException {
    String algorithm = "PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256";
    int iterations = 310_000;
    int saltLengthBytes = 16;
    int keyLengthBits = 256;

    SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();

    byte[] salt = new byte[saltLengthBytes];
    random.nextBytes(salt);

    PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(
        password,
        salt,
        iterations,
        keyLengthBits
    );

    SecretKeyFactory factory = SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(algorithm);

    byte[] hash = factory.generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();

    spec.clearPassword();

    return new PasswordHash(
        algorithm,
        iterations,
        Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(salt),
        Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(hash)
    );
}
```

Verify:

```java id="qqv4j6"
public static boolean verifyPassword(
    char[] password,
    PasswordHash stored
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    byte[] salt = Base64.getDecoder().decode(stored.saltBase64());
    byte[] expected = Base64.getDecoder().decode(stored.hashBase64());

    PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(
        password,
        salt,
        stored.iterations(),
        expected.length * 8
    );

    SecretKeyFactory factory =
        SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(stored.algorithm());

    byte[] actual = factory.generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();

    spec.clearPassword();

    return MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, actual);
}
```

Production note:

```text id="k6khuf"
Tune iterations based on your hardware and latency budget.
Store algorithm, parameters, salt, and hash together.
Support rehash-on-login when parameters become outdated.
```

---

# 8. HMAC

## 8.1 HMAC-SHA256

```java id="9c8yqg"
public static byte[] hmacSha256(
    byte[] key,
    byte[] message
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    Mac mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256");

    SecretKeySpec keySpec = new SecretKeySpec(key, "HmacSHA256");

    mac.init(keySpec);

    return mac.doFinal(message);
}
```

Use:

```java id="ng7jkm"
byte[] tag = hmacSha256(secretKey, payload);
```

HMAC is for message authentication and integrity with a shared secret; Java’s standard algorithm names include HMAC algorithm names, and the `Mac` class is one of the high-level JCA/JCE classes described by Oracle’s JCA guide. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/specs/security/standard-names.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8.2 HMAC Verification

```java id="5ayg9u"
public static boolean verifyHmac(
    byte[] key,
    byte[] message,
    byte[] expectedTag
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    byte[] actualTag = hmacSha256(key, message);

    return MessageDigest.isEqual(expectedTag, actualTag);
}
```

Use cases:

```text id="8en60t"
webhook signature verification
internal message authentication
signed URL
tamper detection
API request signing
```

Rule:

```text id="k10owc"
Use HMAC when both sides share a secret.
Use digital signature when verifier should not know signing secret.
```

---

# 9. Symmetric Encryption

## 9.1 Prefer AEAD

Use authenticated encryption. For common Java SE usage, prefer:

```text id="zn7wfh"
AES/GCM/NoPadding
```

NIST SP 800-38D specifies Galois/Counter Mode, or GCM, as an authenticated encryption mode and GMAC as its message-authentication-only specialization for block ciphers. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/38/d/final?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9.2 Generate AES Key

```java id="m5vx49"
public static SecretKey generateAes256Key()
        throws GeneralSecurityException {
    KeyGenerator keyGenerator = KeyGenerator.getInstance("AES");
    keyGenerator.init(256);

    return keyGenerator.generateKey();
}
```

---

## 9.3 AES-GCM Encrypt

```java id="1jgr6g"
public record AesGcmCiphertext(
    String algorithm,
    String nonceBase64,
    String ciphertextBase64
) {}
```

```java id="yqrqgb"
public static AesGcmCiphertext encryptAesGcm(
    SecretKey key,
    byte[] plaintext,
    byte[] associatedData
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    byte[] nonce = new byte[12];
    new SecureRandom().nextBytes(nonce);

    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding");

    GCMParameterSpec gcmSpec = new GCMParameterSpec(128, nonce);

    cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key, gcmSpec);

    if (associatedData != null) {
        cipher.updateAAD(associatedData);
    }

    byte[] ciphertext = cipher.doFinal(plaintext);

    return new AesGcmCiphertext(
        "AES/GCM/NoPadding",
        Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(nonce),
        Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(ciphertext)
    );
}
```

GCM is an authenticated encryption mode, so `doFinal()` returns ciphertext plus authentication tag in typical JCE usage for `AES/GCM/NoPadding`; the tag protects ciphertext and any AAD from undetected modification. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/38/d/final?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9.4 AES-GCM Decrypt

```java id="57v4k1"
public static byte[] decryptAesGcm(
    SecretKey key,
    AesGcmCiphertext encrypted,
    byte[] associatedData
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    byte[] nonce = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encrypted.nonceBase64());
    byte[] ciphertext = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encrypted.ciphertextBase64());

    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(encrypted.algorithm());

    GCMParameterSpec gcmSpec = new GCMParameterSpec(128, nonce);

    cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, key, gcmSpec);

    if (associatedData != null) {
        cipher.updateAAD(associatedData);
    }

    return cipher.doFinal(ciphertext);
}
```

If authentication fails, `doFinal()` throws an exception such as `AEADBadTagException`; treat this as tampering, wrong key, wrong nonce, wrong AAD, or corrupted data, not as a recoverable parsing issue. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/docs/api/java.base/javax/crypto/Cipher.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9.5 AES-GCM Rules

```text id="gewvpm"
AES-GCM rules:
1. Never reuse nonce with the same key.
2. Use fresh random 96-bit nonce unless your protocol defines another safe scheme.
3. Use 128-bit authentication tag for general use.
4. Store/transmit nonce with ciphertext.
5. AAD must be identical during encryption and decryption.
6. Do not decrypt modified ciphertext successfully; tag verification must pass.
```

Nonce uniqueness is critical for GCM security; NIST’s GCM recommendation defines GCM as authenticated encryption and its security depends on proper IV/nonce handling for the underlying block cipher mode. ([NIST Computer Security Resource Center](https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/38/d/final?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Password-Based Encryption

## 10.1 Rule

```text id="34otng"
Password is not an encryption key.
Derive a key from password using KDF.
Then use AES-GCM.
```

PBKDF2 in Java SE:

```java id="o73q7y"
public static SecretKey deriveAesKeyFromPassword(
    char[] password,
    byte[] salt,
    int iterations
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(
        password,
        salt,
        iterations,
        256
    );

    SecretKeyFactory factory =
        SecretKeyFactory.getInstance("PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256");

    byte[] keyBytes = factory.generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();

    spec.clearPassword();

    return new SecretKeySpec(keyBytes, "AES");
}
```

Important:

```text id="lg4i6r"
Password hashing for login and password-based encryption are different use cases.
Both need salt and KDF, but the stored format and threat model differ.
```

---

# 11. Asymmetric Encryption

## 11.1 Generate RSA Key Pair

```java id="3914id"
public static KeyPair generateRsaKeyPair()
        throws GeneralSecurityException {
    KeyPairGenerator generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA");
    generator.initialize(3072);

    return generator.generateKeyPair();
}
```

RSA is supported through Java’s security APIs and standard algorithm names, but choose padding/mode carefully; do not use raw RSA or legacy transformations such as `RSA/ECB/PKCS1Padding` for new encryption designs. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/specs/security/standard-names.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11.2 RSA-OAEP Encrypt

```java id="3cwlwv"
public static byte[] rsaOaepEncrypt(
    PublicKey publicKey,
    byte[] plaintext
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(
        "RSA/ECB/OAEPWithSHA-256AndMGF1Padding"
    );

    cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, publicKey);

    return cipher.doFinal(plaintext);
}
```

## 11.3 RSA-OAEP Decrypt

```java id="603wqq"
public static byte[] rsaOaepDecrypt(
    PrivateKey privateKey,
    byte[] ciphertext
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(
        "RSA/ECB/OAEPWithSHA-256AndMGF1Padding"
    );

    cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, privateKey);

    return cipher.doFinal(ciphertext);
}
```

Rule:

```text id="r6kg9q"
Use RSA encryption only for small data such as wrapping a symmetric key.
For real payload, use hybrid encryption:
1. Generate AES key.
2. Encrypt data with AES-GCM.
3. Encrypt AES key with RSA-OAEP.
```

---

# 12. Hybrid Encryption Pattern

```text id="mxo6og"
Hybrid Encryption
1. Generate random AES key.
2. Encrypt plaintext with AES-GCM.
3. Encrypt AES key with recipient public key using RSA-OAEP.
4. Send:
   - encrypted AES key
   - nonce
   - ciphertext+tag
   - algorithm metadata
```

Why?

```text id="o7y7yq"
AES handles large data efficiently.
RSA handles key wrapping.
AES-GCM gives confidentiality + integrity.
RSA-OAEP protects the content key for recipient.
```

---

# 13. Digital Signatures

## 13.1 Ed25519 Key Pair

```java id="hwhhgi"
public static KeyPair generateEd25519KeyPair()
        throws GeneralSecurityException {
    KeyPairGenerator generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("Ed25519");

    return generator.generateKeyPair();
}
```

## 13.2 Sign

```java id="j5h97e"
public static byte[] signEd25519(
    PrivateKey privateKey,
    byte[] message
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    Signature signature = Signature.getInstance("Ed25519");

    signature.initSign(privateKey);
    signature.update(message);

    return signature.sign();
}
```

## 13.3 Verify

```java id="o0b80g"
public static boolean verifyEd25519(
    PublicKey publicKey,
    byte[] message,
    byte[] sig
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    Signature signature = Signature.getInstance("Ed25519");

    signature.initVerify(publicKey);
    signature.update(message);

    return signature.verify(sig);
}
```

`Signature` provides digital signature functionality for authentication and integrity assurance. Java’s standard algorithm names document is the reference for standard algorithm names and provider-supported names may vary by JDK/provider. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/security/Signature.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13.4 Signature vs HMAC

| Need | Use |
|---|---|
| Both sides share same secret | HMAC |
| Many verifiers, one signer | Digital signature |
| Non-repudiation-ish audit | Digital signature |
| Webhook with shared secret | HMAC |
| Software release signing | Digital signature |
| JWT HS256 | HMAC |
| JWT RS256/ES256/EdDSA | Digital signature |

---

# 14. Key Serialization

## 14.1 Encode Public Key

```java id="99tkpt"
String publicKeyBase64 = Base64.getEncoder()
    .encodeToString(publicKey.getEncoded());
```

## 14.2 Decode RSA Public Key

```java id="jhsk43"
public static PublicKey readRsaPublicKey(String base64)
        throws GeneralSecurityException {
    byte[] bytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(base64);

    X509EncodedKeySpec spec = new X509EncodedKeySpec(bytes);

    KeyFactory factory = KeyFactory.getInstance("RSA");

    return factory.generatePublic(spec);
}
```

## 14.3 Decode RSA Private Key

```java id="r7zxxd"
public static PrivateKey readRsaPrivateKey(String base64)
        throws GeneralSecurityException {
    byte[] bytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(base64);

    PKCS8EncodedKeySpec spec = new PKCS8EncodedKeySpec(bytes);

    KeyFactory factory = KeyFactory.getInstance("RSA");

    return factory.generatePrivate(spec);
}
```

Common formats:

| Key | Encoding |
|---|---|
| Public key | X.509 SubjectPublicKeyInfo |
| Private key | PKCS#8 |
| Certificate | X.509 certificate |
| Secret key | raw bytes, usually wrapped/protected |

---

# 15. KeyStore

## 15.1 Load KeyStore

```java id="b6cwf5"
public static KeyStore loadKeyStore(
    Path path,
    char[] password
) throws GeneralSecurityException, IOException {
    KeyStore keyStore = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12");

    try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
        keyStore.load(input, password);
    }

    return keyStore;
}
```

`KeyStore` is the main Java abstraction for storing keys and certificates; Java security APIs use provider-based implementations for keystore types such as PKCS12. ([Oracle Docs](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/21/docs/api/java.base/java/security/KeyStoreSpi.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15.2 Read Private Key

```java id="xw5hj5"
public static PrivateKey readPrivateKey(
    KeyStore keyStore,
    String alias,
    char[] keyPassword
) throws GeneralSecurityException {
    Key key = keyStore.getKey(alias, keyPassword);

    if (!(key instanceof PrivateKey privateKey)) {
        throw new KeyStoreException("Alias is not a private key: " + alias);
    }

    return privateKey;
}
```

## 15.3 Read Certificate

```java id="cypq00"
Certificate certificate = keyStore.getCertificate("server");

PublicKey publicKey = certificate.getPublicKey();
```

---

# 16. TLS / HTTPS

## 16.1 Default TLS

For normal HTTPS clients:

```java id="1u54l3"
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
```

Java’s HTTP client and JSSE use the configured JVM trust material by default; custom trust/key material is configured through `SSLContext` and related key/trust manager APIs. ([OpenJDK CR](https://cr.openjdk.org/~pminborg/panama/21/v1/javadoc/java.base/java/security/class-use/KeyStore.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16.2 Custom SSLContext

```java id="iz8nqv"
KeyStore trustStore = loadKeyStore(
    Path.of("truststore.p12"),
    trustStorePassword
);

TrustManagerFactory tmf = TrustManagerFactory.getInstance(
    TrustManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm()
);

tmf.init(trustStore);

SSLContext sslContext = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS");
```
