```java
else if (status == SUBMITTED) { ... }
else if (status == APPROVED) { ... }
```

Better with transition table:

```java
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED,
    CANCELLED
}
```

```java
public final class OrderStateMachine {
    private static final Map<OrderStatus, Set<OrderStatus>> ALLOWED = Map.of(
        OrderStatus.DRAFT, EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.SUBMITTED, OrderStatus.CANCELLED),
        OrderStatus.SUBMITTED, EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.APPROVED, OrderStatus.REJECTED),
        OrderStatus.APPROVED, EnumSet.noneOf(OrderStatus.class),
        OrderStatus.REJECTED, EnumSet.noneOf(OrderStatus.class),
        OrderStatus.CANCELLED, EnumSet.noneOf(OrderStatus.class)
    );

    public void validateTransition(OrderStatus from, OrderStatus to) {
        if (!ALLOWED.getOrDefault(from, Set.of()).contains(to)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid transition: " + from + " -> " + to);
        }
    }
}
```

Use for:

```text
workflow
order lifecycle
ticket status
approval process
Camunda-adjacent domain state
```

---

# 6.6 Observer / Event Listener

```java
public interface DomainEventHandler<E extends DomainEvent> {
    void handle(E event);
}
```

```java
public final class OrderApprovedEmailHandler
        implements DomainEventHandler<OrderApprovedEvent> {

    @Override
    public void handle(OrderApprovedEvent event) {
        // send email
    }
}
```

Use when:

```text
multiple reactions to one event
publisher should not know all consumers
```

Caution:

```text
In-process observer is not reliable messaging.
Use outbox/message broker for cross-service integration.
```

---

# 6.7 Specification Pattern

Encapsulate business rule as object.

```java
public interface Specification<T> {
    boolean isSatisfiedBy(T value);

    default Specification<T> and(Specification<T> other) {
        return value -> this.isSatisfiedBy(value) && other.isSatisfiedBy(value);
    }
}
```

Example:

```java
public final class SubmittedOrderSpec implements Specification<Order> {
    public boolean isSatisfiedBy(Order order) {
        return order.status() == OrderStatus.SUBMITTED;
    }
}
```

```java
public final class SameTenantSpec implements Specification<Order> {
    private final TenantId tenantId;

    public boolean isSatisfiedBy(Order order) {
        return order.tenantId().equals(tenantId);
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
Specification<Order> canApprove =
    new SubmittedOrderSpec().and(new SameTenantSpec(principal.tenantId()));
```

Use when:

```text
business rules need composition
authorization/domain rules get complex
```

---

# 7. Enterprise / Architecture Patterns

---

# 7.1 Layered Architecture

```text
Resource / Controller
  ↓
Application Service / Use Case
  ↓
Domain Model
  ↓
Repository Interface
  ↓
Infrastructure Adapter
```

Rule:

```text
Controller handles HTTP.
Service coordinates use case.
Domain owns business rule.
Repository hides persistence.
Adapter talks to external world.
```

---

# 7.2 Ports and Adapters / Hexagonal

```text
Domain Core
├── inbound ports
│   └── use case interfaces
├── outbound ports
│   ├── repository
│   ├── external API gateway
│   └── event publisher
└── adapters
    ├── REST adapter
    ├── JDBC adapter
    ├── HTTP client adapter
    └── message broker adapter
```

Outbound port:

```java
public interface PaymentGateway {
    PaymentResult charge(PaymentRequest request);
}
```

Adapter:

```java
public final class HttpPaymentGateway implements PaymentGateway {
    public PaymentResult charge(PaymentRequest request) {
        // HTTP call to payment provider
    }
}
```

Benefit:

```text
domain does not depend on HTTP/JDBC/vendor
easier testing
easier replacement
```

---

# 7.3 Dependency Injection Pattern

Bad:

```java
public class OrderService {
    private final OrderRepository repository = new JdbcOrderRepository();
}
```

Good:

```java
public class OrderService {
    private final OrderRepository repository;

    public OrderService(OrderRepository repository) {
        this.repository = Objects.requireNonNull(repository);
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Dependencies come from outside.
Class should not create its own infrastructure dependencies.
```

---

# 7.4 Unit of Work Pattern

```java
public interface UnitOfWork {
    <T> T execute(TransactionCallback<T> callback);
}
```

```java
@FunctionalInterface
public interface TransactionCallback<T> {
    T execute(Connection connection) throws Exception;
}
```

JDBC implementation:

```java
public final class JdbcUnitOfWork implements UnitOfWork {
    private final DataSource dataSource;

    @Override
    public <T> T execute(TransactionCallback<T> callback) {
        try (Connection connection = dataSource.getConnection()) {
            boolean oldAutoCommit = connection.getAutoCommit();

            try {
                connection.setAutoCommit(false);

                T result = callback.execute(connection);

                connection.commit();

                return result;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                connection.rollback();
                throw new RuntimeException(e);
            } finally {
                connection.setAutoCommit(oldAutoCommit);
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
unitOfWork.execute(connection -> {
    Order order = orderRepository.findById(connection, orderId).orElseThrow();
    order.approve(userId);
    orderRepository.save(connection, order);
    return null;
});
```

---

# 7.5 Gateway Pattern

Wrap external system.

```java
public interface CustomerGateway {
    CustomerProfile fetchCustomer(CustomerId customerId);
}
```

```java
public final class HttpCustomerGateway implements CustomerGateway {
    private final HttpClient client;

    @Override
    public CustomerProfile fetchCustomer(CustomerId customerId) {
        // timeout, status handling, mapping, error translation
    }
}
```

Gateway should handle:

```text
transport details
timeout
retry policy
status mapping
response parsing
safe logging
```

Domain should not know:

```text
URL
HTTP status
JSON format
auth header
```

---

# 7.6 Anti-Corruption Layer

When external model is ugly or different.

```java
public final class PartnerOrderTranslator {
    public OrderCommand toCommand(PartnerOrderDto dto) {
        return new OrderCommand(
            new CustomerId(dto.customerReference()),
            dto.items().stream()
                .map(this::toLine)
                .toList()
        );
    }
}
```

Use when:

```text
partner terminology differs
legacy API has weird fields
external status mapping differs
you do not want vendor model leaking into domain
```

---

# 8. Pattern Selection Cheat Sheet

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Too many constructors | Builder |
| Need named creation | Static factory |
| Creation depends on type | Factory |
| External API incompatible | Adapter |
| Add logging/retry/cache around service | Decorator |
| Hide complex subsystem | Facade |
| Protect access | Proxy |
| Choose algorithm by type | Strategy |
| Same workflow, different steps | Template Method |
| Validate through multiple checks | Chain of Responsibility |
| Domain lifecycle transition | State Machine |
| Business rule composition | Specification |
| Use case input | Command |
| Domain happened event | Domain Event |
| DB + message consistency | Transactional Outbox |
| Retry POST safely | Idempotency |
| External integration model isolation | Anti-Corruption Layer |
| Persistence abstraction | Repository |
| Transaction scope abstraction | Unit of Work |
| Business service boundary | Application Service |
| Keep domain independent | Ports and Adapters |

---

# 9. Common Java Enterprise Patterns

## 9.1 Request → Command → Use Case

```text
HTTP Request DTO
  ↓ validate basic shape
Command
  ↓ use case
Domain
  ↓ repository
Response DTO
```

Example:

```java
public record ApproveOrderRequest(String reason) {}

public record ApproveOrderCommand(
    OrderId orderId,
    UserId approverId,
    String reason
) {}
```

---

## 9.2 Entity vs Value Object

Entity:

```java
public final class Order {
    private final OrderId id;
    private OrderStatus status;
}
```

Value object:

```java
public record Address(
    String street,
    String city,
    String postalCode
) {}
```

Difference:

```text
Entity has identity and lifecycle.
Value object is defined by values.
```

---

## 9.3 Aggregate Root

```java
public final class Order {
    private final OrderId id;
    private final List<OrderLine> lines;
    private OrderStatus status;

    public void addLine(ProductId productId, int quantity) {
        if (status != OrderStatus.DRAFT) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot modify submitted order");
        }

        lines.add(new OrderLine(productId, quantity));
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
External code modifies aggregate through aggregate root methods.
Do not expose mutable internals.
```

---

## 9.4 Domain Method over Setter

Bad:

```java
order.setStatus(OrderStatus.APPROVED);
```

Better:

```java
order.approve(approverId, clock.instant());
```

Why:

```text
method captures business meaning
method enforces invariant
method can publish event/audit info
```

---

## 9.5 Domain Exception

```java
public class InvalidOrderStateException extends RuntimeException {
    public InvalidOrderStateException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }
}
```

Use:

```java
if (status != OrderStatus.SUBMITTED) {
    throw new InvalidOrderStateException("Only submitted order can be approved");
}
```

---

# 10. Tricks for Cleaner Code

## 10.1 Replace Boolean Parameter with Explicit Method

Bad:

```java
sendEmail(user, true);
```

Better:

```java
sendWelcomeEmail(user);
sendPasswordResetEmail(user);
```

---

## 10.2 Replace Primitive Obsession with Value Object

Bad:

```java
public void approve(String orderId, String userId) {}
```

Better:

```java
public void approve(OrderId orderId, UserId userId) {}
```

```java
public record OrderId(UUID value) {
    public OrderId {
        Objects.requireNonNull(value);
    }
}
```

---

## 10.3 Replace String Status with Enum

Bad:

```java
String status = "APPROVED";
```

Better:

```java
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED
}
```

---

## 10.4 Replace Magic String with Constant or Enum

Bad:

```java
if ("order:approve".equals(permission)) {}
```

Better:

```java
public final class Permissions {
    public static final String ORDER_APPROVE = "order:approve";

    private Permissions() {}
}
```

Or:

```java
public enum Permission {
    ORDER_APPROVE("order:approve");

    private final String value;

    Permission(String value) {
        this.value = value;
    }

    public String value() {
        return value;
    }
}
```

---

## 10.5 Replace Nested If with Polymorphism

Bad:

```java
if (method == CREDIT_CARD) {
    processCreditCard();
} else if (method == BANK_TRANSFER) {
    processBankTransfer();
} else if (method == WALLET) {
    processWallet();
}
```

Better:

```java
PaymentProcessor processor = processorFactory.create(method);
processor.process(payment);
```

---

# 11. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem |
|---|---|
| God service | too many responsibilities |
| Anemic domain everywhere | business logic scattered |
| Static utility for everything | hard to test/change |
| Singleton service locator | hidden dependencies |
| Boolean parameter | unclear call site |
| Primitive obsession | invalid data too easy |
| Stringly typed status | typo/runtime bugs |
| Exposing mutable collections | invariant broken |
| Catch `Exception` and ignore | failure hidden |
| Returning `null` for everything | NPE-prone |
| Optional as field/parameter | awkward model |
| Overusing inheritance | fragile hierarchy |
| Pattern worship | complexity without benefit |
| Repository returns DTO only | domain bypass |
| Business logic in mapper | hidden behavior |
| Business logic in controller | hard to reuse/test |
| Transaction per repository call | broken unit of work |
| Remote call inside DB lock | latency and deadlock risk |
| Retry without idempotency | duplicate side effects |

---

# 12. Senior Design Checklist

```text
Responsibility
[ ] Does each class have one clear reason to change?
[ ] Is business logic in domain/service, not controller/mapper?
[ ] Are external systems hidden behind gateways/adapters?

Correctness
[ ] Are invariants enforced in domain methods?
[ ] Are invalid states hard to represent?
[ ] Are state transitions explicit?
[ ] Are transaction boundaries clear?

Maintainability
[ ] Is naming domain-specific?
[ ] Are dependencies injected?
[ ] Are interfaces used at meaningful boundaries?
[ ] Are patterns solving real problems, not added for style?

Security
[ ] Is authorization enforced near protected action?
[ ] Are secrets excluded from logs?
[ ] Is user input validated at boundary?
[ ] Is tenant isolation enforced in queries?

Reliability
[ ] Are retries bounded and idempotent?
[ ] Are timeouts configured?
[ ] Is DB + message consistency handled?
[ ] Are failures observable?

Testing
[ ] Can domain logic be unit-tested without infrastructure?
[ ] Are adapters tested separately?
[ ] Are edge cases and invalid transitions tested?
```

---

# 13. Compact Summary

```text
Java Tricks
- guard clause
- requireNonNull
- record
- sealed interface
- switch expression
- pattern matching instanceof
- List.copyOf
- EnumSet / EnumMap
- computeIfAbsent
- Optional return type

Common Patterns
- DTO
- Mapper
- Command
- Result
- Repository
- Service / Use Case
- Unit of Work
- Domain Event
- Transactional Outbox
- Idempotency
- Retry
- Bulkhead

Design Patterns
- Factory
- Builder
- Adapter
- Facade
- Decorator
- Proxy
- Strategy
- Template Method
- Command
- Chain of Responsibility
- State
- Observer
- Specification

Enterprise Architecture
- Layered Architecture
- Ports and Adapters
- Gateway
- Anti-Corruption Layer
- Aggregate Root
- Value Object
- Domain Service
```

Kesimpulan praktis: **jangan memakai design pattern karena hafal namanya.** Pakai pattern ketika ia membuat **responsibility lebih jelas, perubahan lebih aman, testing lebih mudah, dan bug production lebih kecil**. Untuk senior engineer, targetnya bukan “kode terlihat pintar”, tetapi **kode yang business-safe, testable, observable, dan mudah diubah tanpa merusak invariant**.
